"use client";

import { useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useState } from "react";
import {
  accountsReceivableAPI,
  invoiceAPI,
  salesOrderAPI,
  customerAPI,
} from "../../lib/api";

import type { AccountsReceivable } from "../../types/accounts-receivable";
import type { SalesInvoice } from "../../types/invoice";
import type { SalesOrder } from "../../types/order";
import type { Customer } from "../../types/customer";

type ARRow = {
  AR_ID: number;
  invoice_id: number;
  customer_name: string;
  due_date: string | null;
  amount_due: number;
  amount_paid: number;
  balance: number;
  status: string;
};

const money = (value: number) =>
  new Intl.NumberFormat("en-PH", {
    style: "currency",
    currency: "PHP",
  }).format(value);

const amount = (value: string | number) => Number(value) || 0;

export default function AccountsReceivablePage() {
  const [records, setRecords] = useState<ARRow[]>([]);
  const [search, setSearch] = useState("");
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [error, setError] = useState("");

  const loadData = useCallback(async () => {
    try {
      setLoading(true);
      setError("");

      const [arData, invoiceData, orderData, customerData] =
        await Promise.all([
          accountsReceivableAPI.getAll(),
          invoiceAPI.getAll(),
          salesOrderAPI.getAll(),
          customerAPI.getAll(),
        ]);

      const invoices = new Map<number, SalesInvoice>(
        invoiceData.map((invoice: SalesInvoice) => [
          invoice.invoice_id,
          invoice,
        ])
      );

      const orders = new Map<number, SalesOrder>(
        orderData.map((order: SalesOrder) => [
          order.order_id,
          order,
        ])
      );

      const customers = new Map<number, Customer>(
        customerData.map((customer: Customer) => [
          customer.customer_id,
          customer,
        ])
      );

      const rows: ARRow[] = arData.map(
        (record: AccountsReceivable) => {
          const invoice = invoices.get(record.invoice_id);
          const order = invoice
            ? orders.get(invoice.order_id)
            : undefined;
          const customer = order
            ? customers.get(order.customer_id)
            : undefined;

          return {
            AR_ID: record.AR_ID,
            invoice_id: record.invoice_id,
            customer_name: customer?.name ?? "Unknown Customer",
            due_date: invoice?.due_date ?? null,
            amount_due: amount(record.amount_due),
            amount_paid: amount(record.amount_paid),
            balance: amount(record.balance),
            status: record.status ?? "Unpaid",
          };
        }
      );

      setRecords(rows);
    } catch (err) {
      setError(
        err instanceof Error
          ? err.message
          : "Failed to load accounts receivable records."
      );
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    loadData();
  }, [loadData]);

  const filteredRecords = useMemo(() => {
    const keyword = search.toLowerCase();

    return records.filter(
      (record) =>
        String(record.AR_ID).includes(keyword) ||
        String(record.invoice_id).includes(keyword) ||
        record.customer_name.toLowerCase().includes(keyword)
    );
  }, [records, search]);

  const totals = useMemo(() => {
    return {
      amountDue: records.reduce(
        (sum, record) => sum + record.amount_due,
        0
      ),
      amountPaid: records.reduce(
        (sum, record) => sum + record.amount_paid,
        0
      ),
      balance: records.reduce(
        (sum, record) => sum + record.balance,
        0
      ),
      unpaid: records.filter(
        (record) => record.status !== "Paid"
      ).length,
    };
  }, [records]);

  const statusStyle = (status: string) => {
    switch (status) {
      case "Paid":
        return "bg-green-100 text-green-700";
      case "Partially Paid":
        return "bg-yellow-100 text-yellow-700";
      default:
        return "bg-red-100 text-red-700";
    }
  };

  return (
    <main className="min-h-screen bg-gray-50 p-6 text-gray-900 md:p-10">
      <div className="mx-auto max-w-7xl space-y-8">
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-3 sm:flex-row sm:items-center sm:justify-between">
          <div>
            <p className="text-sm font-medium text-gray-500">
              Sales Module
            </p>
            <h1 className="text-3xl font-bold">
              Accounts Receivable
            </h1>
            <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-gray-500">
              Monitor customer balances and outstanding invoices.
            </p>
          </div>

          <button
            onClick={loadData}
            disabled={loading}
            className="rounded-lg border border-gray-300 bg-white px-4 py-2 text-sm font-medium hover:bg-gray-100 disabled:opacity-50"
          >
            {loading ? "Refreshing..." : "Refresh"}
          </button>
        </div>

        <section className="grid gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4">
          <SummaryCard
            title="Total Receivables"
            value={money(totals.amountDue)}
            color="text-blue-600"
          />
          <SummaryCard
            title="Amount Collected"
            value={money(totals.amountPaid)}
            color="text-green-600"
          />
          <SummaryCard
            title="Outstanding Balance"
            value={money(totals.balance)}
            color="text-red-600"
          />
          <SummaryCard
            title="Unpaid Accounts"
            value={String(totals.unpaid)}
            color="text-orange-600"
          />
        </section>

        <section className="rounded-xl border border-gray-200 bg-white p-5 shadow-sm">
          <div className="mb-5 flex flex-col gap-3 sm:flex-row sm:items-center sm:justify-between">
            <h2 className="text-lg font-semibold">
              Receivable Records
            </h2>

            <input
              type="text"
              placeholder="Search invoice or customer..."
              value={search}
              onChange={(event) => setSearch(event.target.value)}
              className="w-full rounded-lg border border-gray-300 px-4 py-2 text-sm outline-none focus:border-blue-500 sm:max-w-xs"
            />
          </div>

          {error && (
            <div className="mb-4 rounded-lg bg-red-50 p-3 text-sm text-red-700">
              {error}
            </div>
          )}

          {loading ? (
            <p className="py-10 text-center text-sm text-gray-500">
              Loading accounts receivable...
            </p>
          ) : (
            <div className="overflow-x-auto">
              <table className="w-full text-left text-sm">
                <thead className="border-b bg-gray-50 text-xs uppercase text-gray-500">
                  <tr>
                    <th className="px-4 py-3">AR No.</th>
                    <th className="px-4 py-3">Invoice No.</th>
                    <th className="px-4 py-3">Customer</th>
                    <th className="px-4 py-3">Due Date</th>
                    <th className="px-4 py-3">Amount Due</th>
                    <th className="px-4 py-3">Amount Paid</th>
                    <th className="px-4 py-3">Balance</th>
                    <th className="px-4 py-3">Status</th>
                  </tr>
                </thead>

                <tbody className="divide-y divide-gray-100">
                  {filteredRecords.map((record) => (
                    <tr
                      key={record.AR_ID}
                      className="hover:bg-gray-50"
                    >
                      <td className="px-4 py-3 font-medium">
                        AR-{record.AR_ID}
                      </td>
                      <td className="px-4 py-3">
                        INV-{record.invoice_id}
                      </td>
                      <td className="px-4 py-3">
                        {record.customer_name}
                      </td>
                      <td className="px-4 py-3">
                        {record.due_date
                          ? new Date(
                              `${record.due_date}T00:00:00`
                            ).toLocaleDateString("en-PH")
                          : "â€”"}
                      </td>
                      <td className="px-4 py-3">
                        {money(record.amount_due)}
                      </td>
                      <td className="px-4 py-3 text-green-700">
                        {money(record.amount_paid)}
                      </td>
                      <td className="px-4 py-3 font-semibold">
                        {money(record.balance)}
                      </td>
                      <td className="px-4 py-3">
                        <span
                          className={`rounded-full px-3 py-1 text-xs font-medium ${statusStyle(record.status)}`}
                        >
                          {record.status}
                        </span>
                      </td>
                    </tr>
                  ))}

                  {filteredRecords.length === 0 && (
                    <tr>
                      <td
                        colSpan={8}
                        className="px-4 py-10 text-center text-gray-500"
                      >
                        {search
                          ? "No matching records found."
                          : "No accounts receivable records yet."}
                      </td>
                    </tr>
                  )}
                </tbody>
              </table>
            </div>
          )}

          <div className="mt-4 text-sm text-gray-500">
            Showing {filteredRecords.length} of {records.length} records
          </div>
        </section>
      </div>
    </main>
  );
}

function SummaryCard({
  title,
  value,
  color,
}: {
  title: string;
  value: string;
  color: string;
}) {
  return (
    <div className="rounded-xl border border-gray-200 bg-white p-5 shadow-sm">
      <p className="text-sm text-gray-500">{title}</p>
      <p className={`mt-2 text-2xl font-bold ${color}`}>
        {value}
      </p>
    </div>
  );
}

"use client";

import { useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useState } from "react";
import {
  paymentCollectionAPI,
  accountsReceivableAPI,
  invoiceAPI,
  salesOrderAPI,
  customerAPI,
} from "../../lib/api";

import type { Collection, CollectionCreate } from "../../types/payment";
import type { AccountsReceivable } from "../../types/accounts-receivable";
import type { SalesInvoice } from "../../types/invoice";
import type { SalesOrder } from "../../types/order";
import type { Customer } from "../../types/customer";

type CollectionRow = Collection & {
  customer_name: string;
  invoice_id: number | null;
};

const money = (value: string | number) =>
  new Intl.NumberFormat("en-PH", {
    style: "currency",
    currency: "PHP",
  }).format(Number(value) || 0);

const today = () => {
  const date = new Date();
  const year = date.getFullYear();
  const month = String(date.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, "0");
  const day = String(date.getDate()).padStart(2, "0");
  return `${year}-${month}-${day}`;
};

export default function CollectionsPage() {
  const [records, setRecords] = useState<CollectionRow[]>([]);
  const [receivables, setReceivables] = useState<AccountsReceivable[]>([]);
  const [search, setSearch] = useState("");
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState("");
  const [success, setSuccess] = useState("");

  const [form, setForm] = useState<CollectionCreate>({
    AR_ID: 0,
    payment_date: today(),
    payment_method: "Cash",
    amount_paid: 0,
  });

  const loadData = useCallback(async () => {
    try {
      setLoading(true);
      setError("");

      const [
        paymentData,
        arData,
        invoiceData,
        orderData,
        customerData,
      ] = await Promise.all([
        paymentCollectionAPI.getAll(),
        accountsReceivableAPI.getAll(),
        invoiceAPI.getAll(),
        salesOrderAPI.getAll(),
        customerAPI.getAll(),
      ]);

      const invoiceMap = new Map<number, SalesInvoice>(
        invoiceData.map((item: SalesInvoice) => [
          item.invoice_id,
          item,
        ])
      );

      const orderMap = new Map<number, SalesOrder>(
        orderData.map((item: SalesOrder) => [
          item.order_id,
          item,
        ])
      );

      const customerMap = new Map<number, Customer>(
        customerData.map((item: Customer) => [
          item.customer_id,
          item,
        ])
      );

      const arMap = new Map<number, AccountsReceivable>(
        arData.map((item: AccountsReceivable) => [
          item.AR_ID,
          item,
        ])
      );

      const rows: CollectionRow[] = paymentData.map(
        (payment: Collection) => {
          const ar = arMap.get(payment.AR_ID);
          const invoice = ar
            ? invoiceMap.get(ar.invoice_id)
            : undefined;
          const order = invoice
            ? orderMap.get(invoice.order_id)
            : undefined;
          const customer = order
            ? customerMap.get(order.customer_id)
            : undefined;

          return {
            ...payment,
            invoice_id: ar?.invoice_id ?? null,
            customer_name: customer?.name ?? "Unknown Customer",
          };
        }
      );

      setRecords(rows);
      setReceivables(arData);
    } catch (err) {
      setError(
        err instanceof Error
          ? err.message
          : "Failed to load payment collections."
      );
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    loadData();
  }, [loadData]);

  const filteredRecords = useMemo(() => {
    const keyword = search.toLowerCase();

    return records.filter(
      (record) =>
        String(record.payment_id).includes(keyword) ||
        String(record.invoice_id ?? "").includes(keyword) ||
        record.customer_name.toLowerCase().includes(keyword) ||
        (record.payment_method ?? "").toLowerCase().includes(keyword)
    );
  }, [records, search]);

  const totals = useMemo(
    () => ({
      collected: records.reduce(
        (sum, record) => sum + Number(record.amount_paid),
        0
      ),
      count: records.length,
      cash: records.filter(
        (record) => record.payment_method === "Cash"
      ).length,
    }),
    [records]
  );

  const handleSubmit = async (event: React.FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>) => {
    event.preventDefault();
    setError("");
    setSuccess("");

    if (!form.AR_ID) {
      setError("Please select an unpaid or partially paid account.");
      return;
    }

    if (form.amount_paid <= 0) {
      setError("Payment amount must be greater than zero.");
      return;
    }

    const selectedAR = receivables.find(
      (record) => record.AR_ID === form.AR_ID
    );

    if (!selectedAR) {
      setError("Selected accounts receivable record was not found.");
      return;
    }

    if (form.amount_paid > Number(selectedAR.balance)) {
      setError("Payment cannot exceed the remaining balance.");
      return;
    }

    try {
      setSaving(true);

      await paymentCollectionAPI.create(form);

      setSuccess("Payment successfully recorded.");
      setForm({
        AR_ID: 0,
        payment_date: today(),
        payment_method: "Cash",
        amount_paid: 0,
      });

      await loadData();
    } catch (err) {
      setError(
        err instanceof Error
          ? err.message
          : "Failed to record payment."
      );
    } finally {
      setSaving(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <main className="min-h-screen bg-gray-50 p-6 text-gray-900 md:p-10">
      <div className="mx-auto max-w-7xl space-y-8">
        <div>
          <p className="text-sm font-medium text-gray-500">
            Sales Module
          </p>
          <h1 className="text-3xl font-bold">
            Payment Collections
          </h1>
          <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-gray-500">
            Record and monitor payments received from customers.
          </p>
        </div>

        <section className="grid gap-4 sm:grid-cols-3">
          <SummaryCard
            title="Total Collected"
            value={money(totals.collected)}
            color="text-green-600"
          />
          <SummaryCard
            title="Payment Transactions"
            value={String(totals.count)}
            color="text-blue-600"
          />
          <SummaryCard
            title="Cash Transactions"
            value={String(totals.cash)}
            color="text-orange-600"
          />
        </section>

        <section className="rounded-xl border border-gray-200 bg-white p-6 shadow-sm">
          <h2 className="mb-5 text-lg font-semibold">
            Record New Payment
          </h2>

          <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="grid gap-4 md:grid-cols-2">
            <div>
              <label className="mb-1 block text-sm font-medium">
                Accounts Receivable
              </label>
              <select
                value={form.AR_ID || ""}
                onChange={(event) =>
                  setForm({
                    ...form,
                    AR_ID: Number(event.target.value),
                  })
                }
                required
                className="w-full rounded-lg border border-gray-300 px-3 py-2 text-sm"
              >
                <option value="">Select an account</option>
                {receivables
                  .filter((record) => Number(record.balance) > 0)
                  .map((record) => (
                    <option key={record.AR_ID} value={record.AR_ID}>
                      AR-{record.AR_ID} Â· Invoice #{record.invoice_id} Â· Balance: {money(record.balance)}
                    </option>
                  ))}
              </select>
            </div>

            <div>
              <label className="mb-1 block text-sm font-medium">
                Payment Date
              </label>
              <input
                type="date"
                value={form.payment_date}
                onChange={(event) =>
                  setForm({
                    ...form,
                    payment_date: event.target.value,
                  })
                }
                required
                className="w-full rounded-lg border border-gray-300 px-3 py-2 text-sm"
              />
            </div>

            <div>
              <label className="mb-1 block text-sm font-medium">
                Payment Method
              </label>
              <select
                value={form.payment_method ?? "Cash"}
                onChange={(event) =>
                  setForm({
                    ...form,
                    payment_method: event.target.value,
                  })
                }
                className="w-full rounded-lg border border-gray-300 px-3 py-2 text-sm"
              >
                <option value="Cash">Cash</option>
                <option value="Bank Transfer">Bank Transfer</option>
                <option value="E-Wallet">E-Wallet</option>
                <option value="Other">Other</option>
              </select>
            </div>

            <div>
              <label className="mb-1 block text-sm font-medium">
                Amount Received (â‚±)
              </label>
              <input
                type="number"
                min="0.01"
                step="0.01"
                value={form.amount_paid || ""}
                onChange={(event) =>
                  setForm({
                    ...form,
                    amount_paid: Number(event.target.value),
                  })
                }
                required
                className="w-full rounded-lg border border-gray-300 px-3 py-2 text-sm"
                placeholder="Enter payment amount"
              />
            </div>

            {error && (
              <div className="md:col-span-2 rounded-lg bg-red-50 p-3 text-sm text-red-700">
                {error}
              </div>
            )}

            {success && (
              <div className="md:col-span-2 rounded-lg bg-green-50 p-3 text-sm text-green-700">
                {success}
              </div>
            )}

            <div className="md:col-span-2">
              <button
                type="submit"
                disabled={saving || loading || receivables.every((record) => Number(record.balance) <= 0)}
                className="rounded-lg bg-blue-600 px-5 py-2.5 text-sm font-medium text-white hover:bg-blue-700 disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-50"
              >
                {saving ? "Recording Payment..." : "Record Payment"}
              </button>
            </div>
          </form>
        </section>

        <section className="rounded-xl border border-gray-200 bg-white p-5 shadow-sm">
          <div className="mb-5 flex flex-col gap-3 sm:flex-row sm:items-center sm:justify-between">
            <h2 className="text-lg font-semibold">
              Payment History
            </h2>

            <div className="flex gap-2">
              <input
                type="text"
                placeholder="Search payment or customer..."
                value={search}
                onChange={(event) => setSearch(event.target.value)}
                className="w-full rounded-lg border border-gray-300 px-4 py-2 text-sm sm:max-w-xs"
              />
              <button
                onClick={loadData}
                disabled={loading}
                className="rounded-lg border border-gray-300 px-4 py-2 text-sm hover:bg-gray-100 disabled:opacity-50"
              >
                Refresh
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>

          {loading ? (
            <p className="py-10 text-center text-sm text-gray-500">
              Loading payment history...
            </p>
          ) : (
            <div className="overflow-x-auto">
              <table className="w-full text-left text-sm">
                <thead className="border-b bg-gray-50 text-xs uppercase text-gray-500">
                  <tr>
                    <th className="px-4 py-3">Payment No.</th>
                    <th className="px-4 py-3">AR No.</th>
                    <th className="px-4 py-3">Invoice No.</th>
                    <th className="px-4 py-3">Customer</th>
                    <th className="px-4 py-3">Payment Date</th>
                    <th className="px-4 py-3">Amount</th>
                    <th className="px-4 py-3">Method</th>
                  </tr>
                </thead>

                <tbody className="divide-y divide-gray-100">
                  {filteredRecords.map((record) => (
                    <tr
                      key={record.payment_id}
                      className="hover:bg-gray-50"
                    >
                      <td className="px-4 py-3 font-medium">
                        PAY-{record.payment_id}
                      </td>
                      <td className="px-4 py-3">
                        AR-{record.AR_ID}
                      </td>
                      <td className="px-4 py-3">
                        {record.invoice_id
                          ? `INV-${record.invoice_id}`
                          : "â€”"}
                      </td>
                      <td className="px-4 py-3">
                        {record.customer_name}
                      </td>
                      <td className="px-4 py-3">
                        {new Date(
                          `${record.payment_date}T00:00:00`
                        ).toLocaleDateString("en-PH")}
                      </td>
                      <td className="px-4 py-3 font-semibold">
                        {money(record.amount_paid)}
                      </td>
                      <td className="px-4 py-3">
                        {record.payment_method ?? "â€”"}
                      </td>
                    </tr>
                  ))}

                  {filteredRecords.length === 0 && (
                    <tr>
                      <td
                        colSpan={7}
                        className="px-4 py-10 text-center text-gray-500"
                      >
                        No payment records found.
                      </td>
                    </tr>
                  )}
                </tbody>
              </table>
            </div>
          )}

          <div className="mt-4 text-sm text-gray-500">
            Showing {filteredRecords.length} of {records.length} payments
          </div>
        </section>
      </div>
    </main>
  );
}

function SummaryCard({
  title,
  value,
  color,
}: {
  title: string;
  value: string;
  color: string;
}) {
  return (
    <div className="rounded-xl border border-gray-200 bg-white p-5 shadow-sm">
      <p className="text-sm text-gray-500">{title}</p>
      <p className={`mt-2 text-2xl font-bold ${color}`}>
        {value}
      </p>
    </div>
  );
}

"use client";

import { useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useState } from "react";

import {
  accountsReceivableAPI,
  invoiceAPI,
  salesOrderAPI,
  customerAPI,
} from "../../lib/api";

import type { AccountsReceivable } from "../../types/accounts-receivable";
import type { SalesInvoice } from "../../types/invoice";
import type { SalesOrder } from "../../types/order";
import type { Customer } from "../../types/customer";

type CustomerBalanceRow = {
  customer_id: number;
  customer_name: string;
  total_invoiced: number;
  total_paid: number;
  balance: number;
  invoice_count: number;
  status: string;
};

const PAGE_SIZE = 10;

const money = (value: number) =>
  new Intl.NumberFormat("en-PH", {
    style: "currency",
    currency: "PHP",
  }).format(value);

const amount = (value: string | number) =>
  Number(value) || 0;

export default function CustomerBalancePage() {
  const [records, setRecords] = useState<CustomerBalanceRow[]>([]);
  const [search, setSearch] = useState("");
  const [statusFilter, setStatusFilter] = useState("All");
  const [currentPage, setCurrentPage] = useState(1);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [error, setError] = useState("");

  const loadData = useCallback(async () => {
    try {
      setLoading(true);
      setError("");

      const [
        arData,
        invoiceData,
        orderData,
        customerData,
      ] = await Promise.all([
        accountsReceivableAPI.getAll(),
        invoiceAPI.getAll(),
        salesOrderAPI.getAll(),
        customerAPI.getAll(),
      ]);

      const invoices = new Map<number, SalesInvoice>(
        invoiceData.map((invoice: SalesInvoice) => [
          invoice.invoice_id,
          invoice,
        ])
      );

      const orders = new Map<number, SalesOrder>(
        orderData.map((order: SalesOrder) => [
          order.order_id,
          order,
        ])
      );

      const customers = new Map<number, Customer>(
        customerData.map((customer: Customer) => [
          customer.customer_id,
          customer,
        ])
      );

      const balances = new Map<number, CustomerBalanceRow>();

      arData.forEach((record: AccountsReceivable) => {
        const invoice = invoices.get(record.invoice_id);

        const order = invoice
          ? orders.get(invoice.order_id)
          : undefined;

        if (!order) return;

        const customer = customers.get(order.customer_id);

        if (!customer) return;

        const existing = balances.get(customer.customer_id);

        if (existing) {
          existing.total_invoiced += amount(record.amount_due);
          existing.total_paid += amount(record.amount_paid);
          existing.balance += amount(record.balance);
          existing.invoice_count += 1;
        } else {
          balances.set(customer.customer_id, {
            customer_id: customer.customer_id,
            customer_name: customer.name,
            total_invoiced: amount(record.amount_due),
            total_paid: amount(record.amount_paid),
            balance: amount(record.balance),
            invoice_count: 1,
            status: "Unpaid",
          });
        }
      });

      const rows = Array.from(balances.values()).map((customer) => {
        let status = "Unpaid";

        if (customer.balance <= 0) {
          status = "Paid";
        } else if (customer.total_paid > 0) {
          status = "Partially Paid";
        }

        return {
          ...customer,
          status,
        };
      });

      setRecords(rows);
      setCurrentPage(1);
    } catch (err) {
      setError(
        err instanceof Error
          ? err.message
          : "Failed to load customer balances."
      );
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    void loadData();
  }, [loadData]);

  const filteredRecords = useMemo(() => {
    const keyword = search.trim().toLowerCase();

    return records.filter((record) => {
      const matchesSearch =
        String(record.customer_id).includes(keyword) ||
        record.customer_name.toLowerCase().includes(keyword);

      const matchesStatus =
        statusFilter === "All" ||
        record.status === statusFilter;

      return matchesSearch && matchesStatus;
    });
  }, [records, search, statusFilter]);

  const totalPages = Math.max(
    1,
    Math.ceil(filteredRecords.length / PAGE_SIZE)
  );

  const safePage = Math.min(currentPage, totalPages);
  const startIndex = (safePage - 1) * PAGE_SIZE;

  const paginatedRecords = filteredRecords.slice(
    startIndex,
    startIndex + PAGE_SIZE
  );

  const totals = useMemo(
    () => ({
      invoiced: records.reduce(
        (sum, record) => sum + record.total_invoiced,
        0
      ),
      paid: records.reduce(
        (sum, record) => sum + record.total_paid,
        0
      ),
      balance: records.reduce(
        (sum, record) => sum + record.balance,
        0
      ),
      customers: records.length,
      unpaid: records.filter(
        (record) => record.balance > 0
      ).length,
    }),
    [records]
  );

  const statusStyle = (status: string) => {
    switch (status) {
      case "Paid":
        return "bg-green-100 text-green-700";
      case "Partially Paid":
        return "bg-yellow-100 text-yellow-700";
      default:
        return "bg-red-100 text-red-700";
    }
  };

  return (
    <main className="min-h-screen bg-gray p-6 text-gray-900 md:p-10">
      <div className="mx-auto max-w-7xl space-y-8">

        {error && (
          <div className="rounded-lg border border-red-200 bg-red-50 p-4 text-sm text-red-700">
            {error}
          </div>
        )}

        {/* Summary Cards */}
        <section className="grid gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4">
          <SummaryCard
            title="Total Customers"
            value={String(totals.customers)}
            color="text-black-600"
          />

          <SummaryCard
            title="Total Invoiced"
            value={money(totals.invoiced)}
            color="text-gray-900"
          />

          <SummaryCard
            title="Total Paid"
            value={money(totals.paid)}
            color="text-green-600"
          />

          <SummaryCard
            title="Outstanding Balance"
            value={money(totals.balance)}
            color="text-red-600"
          />
        </section>

        {/* Customer Balance Table */}
        <section className="overflow-hidden rounded-xl border border-[#D1D5DB] bg-[#E5E7EB] shadow-sm">
          <div className="p-5">
            <div className="mb-5 flex flex-col gap-4 lg:flex-row lg:items-center lg:justify-between">
              <div>
                <h2 className="text-lg font-semibold text-gray-900">
                  Customer Balance Records
                </h2>

                <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-gray-600">
                  Consolidated balances from all accounts receivable records.
                </p>
              </div>

              <div className="flex flex-col gap-3 sm:flex-row">
                <input
                  type="search"
                  placeholder="Search customer..."
                  value={search}
                  onChange={(event) => {
                    setSearch(event.target.value);
                    setCurrentPage(1);
                  }}
                  className="w-full rounded-lg border border-gray-300 bg-white px-4 py-2 text-sm text-gray-900 outline-none placeholder:text-gray-400 focus:border-blue-500 sm:w-64"
                />

                <select
                  value={statusFilter}
                  onChange={(event) => {
                    setStatusFilter(event.target.value);
                    setCurrentPage(1);
                  }}
                  className="rounded-lg border border-gray-300 bg-white px-4 py-2 text-sm text-gray-900 outline-none focus:border-blue-500"
                >
                  <option value="All">All Statuses</option>
                  <option value="Paid">Paid</option>
                  <option value="Partially Paid">Partially Paid</option>
                  <option value="Unpaid">Unpaid</option>
                </select>
              </div>
            </div>

            {loading ? (
              <p className="py-10 text-center text-sm text-gray-600">
                Loading customer balances...
              </p>
            ) : (
              <div className="overflow-x-auto">
                <table className="w-full text-left text-sm">
                  <thead className="border-b border-[#D1D5DB] bg-[#D1D5DB] text-xs uppercase text-gray-700">
                    <tr>
                      <th className="px-4 py-3 text-left">Customer ID</th>
                      <th className="px-4 py-3 text-center">Customer</th>
                      <th className="px-4 py-3 text-center">Invoices</th>
                      <th className="px-4 py-3 text-center">Total Invoiced</th>
                      <th className="px-4 py-3 text-center">Total Paid</th>
                      <th className="px-4 py-3 text-center">Balance</th>
                      <th className="px-4 py-3 text-center">Status</th>
                    </tr>
                  </thead>

                  <tbody className="divide-y divide-[#D1D5DB]">
                    {paginatedRecords.map((record) => (
                      <tr
                        key={record.customer_id}
                        className="transition-colors hover:bg-[#DDE1E5]"
                      >
                        <td className="px-4 py-3 font-medium text-gray-800">
                          CUS-{record.customer_id}
                        </td>

                        <td className="px-4 py-3 text-center font-medium text-gray-800">
                          {record.customer_name}
                        </td>

                        <td className="px-4 py-3 text-center text-gray-700">
                          {record.invoice_count}
                        </td>

                        <td className="px-4 py-3 text-center text-gray-800">
                          {money(record.total_invoiced)}
                        </td>

                        <td className="px-4 py-3 text-center text-green-700">
                          {money(record.total_paid)}
                        </td>

                        <td className="px-4 py-3 text-center font-semibold text-red-700">
                          {money(record.balance)}
                        </td>

                        <td className="px-4 py-3 text-center">
                          <span
                            className={`inline-block rounded-full px-3 py-1 text-xs font-medium ${statusStyle(record.status)}`}
                          >
                            {record.status}
                          </span>
                        </td>
                      </tr>
                    ))}

                    {filteredRecords.length === 0 && (
                      <tr>
                        <td
                          colSpan={7}
                          className="px-4 py-10 text-center text-gray-600"
                        >
                          {search || statusFilter !== "All"
                            ? "No matching customer balances found."
                            : "No customer balance records yet."}
                        </td>
                      </tr>
                    )}
                  </tbody>
                </table>
              </div>
            )}
          </div>

          {/* Pagination */}
          {!loading && (
            <div className="flex flex-col gap-3 border-t border-slate-200 bg-gray px-5 py-4 sm:flex-row sm:items-center sm:justify-between">
              <p className="text-sm text-slate-500">
                Showing{" "}
                <span className="font-semibold text-slate-700">
                  {filteredRecords.length === 0 ? 0 : startIndex + 1}
                </span>{" "}
                to{" "}
                <span className="font-semibold text-slate-700">
                  {Math.min(startIndex + PAGE_SIZE, filteredRecords.length)}
                </span>{" "}
                of{" "}
                <span className="font-semibold text-slate-700">
                  {filteredRecords.length}
                </span>{" "}
                customers
              </p>

              <div className="flex items-center justify-end gap-2">
                <button
                  type="button"
                  disabled={safePage === 1}
                  onClick={() => setCurrentPage(safePage - 1)}
                  className="rounded-lg border border-slate-300 px-3 py-2 text-sm font-medium text-slate-700 transition hover:bg-slate-50 disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-40"
                >
                  Previous
                </button>

                <span className="px-2 text-sm font-medium text-slate-600">
                  Page {safePage} of {totalPages}
                </span>

                <button
                  type="button"
                  disabled={safePage === totalPages}
                  onClick={() => setCurrentPage(safePage + 1)}
                  className="rounded-lg border border-slate-300 px-3 py-2 text-sm font-medium text-slate-700 transition hover:bg-slate-50 disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-40"
                >
                  Next
                </button>
              </div>
            </div>
          )}
        </section>
      </div>
    </main>
  );
}

function SummaryCard({
  title,
  value,
  color,
}: {
  title: string;
  value: string;
  color: string;
}) {
  return (
    <div className="rounded-xl border border-[#D1D5DB] bg-[#E5E7EB] p-5 shadow-sm">
      <p className="text-sm text-gray-600">{title}</p>

      <p className={`mt-2 text-2xl font-bold ${color}`}>
        {value}
      </p>
    </div>
  );
}

"use client";

import { useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useState } from "react";
import { customerAPI, salesOrderAPI } from "../../lib/api";
import type { Customer } from "../../types/customer";
import type {
  SalesOrder,
  SalesOrderCreate,
  SalesOrderItem,
  SalesOrderStatus,
} from "../../types/order";

interface OrderLine {
  IM_ID: number;
  quantity: number;
  discount: number;
}

const statuses: SalesOrderStatus[] = [
  "Pending",
  "Approved",
  "Processing",
  "Completed",
  "Cancelled",
];

const money = (amount: string | number) =>
  `â‚±${Number(amount).toLocaleString("en-PH", {
    minimumFractionDigits: 2,
    maximumFractionDigits: 2,
  })}`;

export default function OrdersPage() {
  const [orders, setOrders] = useState<SalesOrder[]>([]);
  const [customers, setCustomers] = useState<Customer[]>([]);
  const [items, setItems] = useState<SalesOrderItem[]>([]);

  const [search, setSearch] = useState("");
  const [itemSearch, setItemSearch] = useState("");

  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState("");

  const [showForm, setShowForm] = useState(false);
  const [customerId, setCustomerId] = useState("");
  const [lines, setLines] = useState<OrderLine[]>([
    { IM_ID: 0, quantity: 1, discount: 0 },
  ]);

  const loadOrders = useCallback(async () => {
    setLoading(true);
    setError("");

    try {
      const data = await salesOrderAPI.getAll();
      setOrders(data);
    } catch (err) {
      setError(
        err instanceof Error ? err.message : "Unable to load sales orders."
      );
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  }, []);

  const loadCustomers = useCallback(async () => {
    try {
      const data = await customerAPI.getAll();
      setCustomers(data);
    } catch (err) {
      setError(
        err instanceof Error ? err.message : "Unable to load customers."
      );
    }
  }, []);

  const loadItems = useCallback(async () => {
    try {
      const data = await salesOrderAPI.getItems(itemSearch);
      setItems(data);
    } catch (err) {
      setError(
        err instanceof Error ? err.message : "Unable to load items."
      );
    }
  }, [itemSearch]);

  useEffect(() => {
    void loadOrders();
    void loadCustomers();
  }, [loadOrders, loadCustomers]);

  useEffect(() => {
    const timeout = setTimeout(() => {
      void loadItems();
    }, 300);

    return () => clearTimeout(timeout);
  }, [loadItems]);

  const customerMap = useMemo(
    () => new Map(customers.map((customer) => [customer.customer_id, customer.name])),
    [customers]
  );

  const itemMap = useMemo(
    () => new Map(items.map((item) => [item.IM_ID, item])),
    [items]
  );

  const filteredOrders = useMemo(() => {
    const term = search.trim().toLowerCase();

    if (!term) return orders;

    return orders.filter((order) => {
      const customerName =
        customerMap.get(order.customer_id) ?? "";

      return (
        String(order.order_id).includes(term) ||
        customerName.toLowerCase().includes(term) ||
        order.status.toLowerCase().includes(term)
      );
    });
  }, [orders, search, customerMap]);

  const totalSales = orders.reduce(
    (sum, order) => sum + Number(order.total_amount),
    0
  );

  const pendingOrders = orders.filter(
    (order) => order.status === "Pending"
  ).length;

  const completedOrders = orders.filter(
    (order) => order.status === "Completed"
  ).length;

  function updateLine(index: number, changes: Partial<OrderLine>) {
    setLines((current) =>
      current.map((line, i) =>
        i === index ? { ...line, ...changes } : line
      )
    );
  }

  function addLine() {
    setLines((current) => [
      ...current,
      { IM_ID: 0, quantity: 1, discount: 0 },
    ]);
  }

  function removeLine(index: number) {
    setLines((current) => current.filter((_, i) => i !== index));
  }

  function resetForm() {
    setCustomerId("");
    setLines([{ IM_ID: 0, quantity: 1, discount: 0 }]);
    setItemSearch("");
    setShowForm(false);
  }

  async function handleCreateOrder() {
    setError("");

    if (!customerId) {
      setError("Please select a customer.");
      return;
    }

    if (
      lines.length === 0 ||
      lines.some(
        (line) =>
          line.IM_ID <= 0 ||
          line.quantity <= 0 ||
          line.discount < 0 ||
          line.discount > 100
      )
    ) {
      setError("Please select valid items, quantities, and discounts.");
      return;
    }

    const data: SalesOrderCreate = {
      customer_id: Number(customerId),
      details: lines.map((line) => ({
        IM_ID: line.IM_ID,
        quantity: line.quantity,
        discount: line.discount,
      })),
    };

    setSaving(true);

    try {
      await salesOrderAPI.create(data);
      resetForm();
      await loadOrders();
    } catch (err) {
      setError(
        err instanceof Error ? err.message : "Unable to create sales order."
      );
    } finally {
      setSaving(false);
    }
  }

  async function handleStatusChange(
    order: SalesOrder,
    status: SalesOrderStatus
  ) {
    if (order.status === status) return;

    setError("");

    try {
      const updated = await salesOrderAPI.updateStatus(
        order.order_id,
        status
      );

      setOrders((current) =>
        current.map((item) =>
          item.order_id === updated.order_id ? updated : item
        )
      );
    } catch (err) {
      setError(
        err instanceof Error ? err.message : "Unable to update order status."
      );
    }
  }

  return (
    <main className="min-h-screen bg-gray-50 p-6 text-gray-900 md:p-10">
      <div className="mx-auto max-w-7xl space-y-8">
        <header>
          <p className="text-sm font-medium text-gray-500">
            Sales Module / Sales Order
          </p>

          <div className="mt-2 flex flex-col justify-between gap-4 sm:flex-row sm:items-center">
            <div>
              <h1 className="text-3xl font-bold tracking-tight">
                Sales Orders
              </h1>
              <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-gray-600">
                Manage confirmed orders for computer parts and computer sets.
              </p>
            </div>

            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setShowForm(true)}
              className="rounded-lg bg-gray-900 px-5 py-3 text-sm font-medium text-white hover:bg-gray-700"
            >
              + Create Sales Order
            </button>
          </div>
        </header>

        {error && (
          <div
            role="alert"
            className="rounded-lg border border-red-200 bg-red-50 p-4 text-sm text-red-700"
          >
            {error}
          </div>
        )}

        <section className="grid gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4">
          <div className="rounded-xl border border-gray-200 bg-white p-5">
            <p className="text-sm text-gray-500">Total Orders</p>
            <p className="mt-2 text-2xl font-semibold">{orders.length}</p>
          </div>

          <div className="rounded-xl border border-gray-200 bg-white p-5">
            <p className="text-sm text-gray-500">Total Sales</p>
            <p className="mt-2 text-2xl font-semibold">{money(totalSales)}</p>
          </div>

          <div className="rounded-xl border border-gray-200 bg-white p-5">
            <p className="text-sm text-gray-500">Pending Orders</p>
            <p className="mt-2 text-2xl font-semibold text-amber-600">
              {pendingOrders}
            </p>
          </div>

          <div className="rounded-xl border border-gray-200 bg-white p-5">
            <p className="text-sm text-gray-500">Completed Orders</p>
            <p className="mt-2 text-2xl font-semibold text-green-600">
              {completedOrders}
            </p>
          </div>
        </section>

        <section className="overflow-hidden rounded-xl border border-gray-200 bg-white">
          <div className="flex flex-col justify-between gap-4 border-b border-gray-200 p-5 sm:flex-row sm:items-center">
            <div>
              <h2 className="font-semibold">Sales Order List</h2>
              <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-gray-500">
                View and update your sales orders.
              </p>
            </div>

            <input
              type="search"
              value={search}
              onChange={(e) => setSearch(e.target.value)}
              placeholder="Search order, customer, or status..."
              className="w-full rounded-lg border border-gray-300 px-4 py-2 text-sm outline-none focus:border-gray-900 sm:max-w-sm"
            />
          </div>

          <div className="overflow-x-auto">
            <table className="w-full text-left text-sm">
              <thead className="bg-gray-50 text-gray-500">
                <tr>
                  <th className="px-5 py-4 font-medium">Order No.</th>
                  <th className="px-5 py-4 font-medium">Customer</th>
                  <th className="px-5 py-4 font-medium">Order Date</th>
                  <th className="px-5 py-4 font-medium">Items</th>
                  <th className="px-5 py-4 font-medium">Total Amount</th>
                  <th className="px-5 py-4 font-medium">Status</th>
                </tr>
              </thead>

              <tbody className="divide-y divide-gray-100">
                {loading ? (
                  <tr>
                    <td colSpan={6} className="px-5 py-10 text-center text-gray-500">
                      Loading sales orders...
                    </td>
                  </tr>
                ) : filteredOrders.length === 0 ? (
                  <tr>
                    <td colSpan={6} className="px-5 py-10 text-center text-gray-500">
                      No sales orders found.
                    </td>
                  </tr>
                ) : (
                  filteredOrders.map((order) => (
                    <tr key={order.order_id} className="hover:bg-gray-50">
                      <td className="px-5 py-4 font-medium">
                        SO-{String(order.order_id).padStart(4, "0")}
                      </td>
                      <td className="px-5 py-4">
                        {customerMap.get(order.customer_id) ??
                          `Customer #${order.customer_id}`}
                      </td>
                      <td className="px-5 py-4">
                        {new Date(order.order_date).toLocaleDateString("en-PH")}
                      </td>
                      <td className="px-5 py-4">{order.details.length}</td>
                      <td className="px-5 py-4 font-medium">
                        {money(order.total_amount)}
                      </td>
                      <td className="px-5 py-4">
                        <select
                          value={order.status}
                          onChange={(e) =>
                            void handleStatusChange(
                              order,
                              e.target.value as SalesOrderStatus
                            )
                          }
                          className="rounded-lg border border-gray-300 bg-white px-3 py-2 text-xs outline-none focus:border-gray-900"
                          aria-label={`Status for order ${order.order_id}`}
                        >
                          {statuses.map((status) => (
                            <option key={status} value={status}>
                              {status}
                            </option>
                          ))}
                        </select>
                      </td>
                    </tr>
                  ))
                )}
              </tbody>
            </table>
          </div>
        </section>
      </div>

      {showForm && (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center overflow-y-auto bg-black/40 p-4">
          <div className="my-auto w-full max-w-2xl rounded-xl bg-white p-6 shadow-xl">
            <div className="mb-6 flex items-start justify-between gap-4">
              <div>
                <h2 className="text-xl font-bold">Create Sales Order</h2>
                <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-gray-500">
                  Select a customer and add the items for this order.
                </p>
              </div>

              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={resetForm}
                className="text-gray-500 hover:text-gray-900"
                aria-label="Close form"
              >
                âœ•
              </button>
            </div>

            <div className="space-y-5">
              <div>
                <label className="mb-2 block text-sm font-medium">
                  Customer
                </label>
                <select
                  value={customerId}
                  onChange={(e) => setCustomerId(e.target.value)}
                  className="w-full rounded-lg border border-gray-300 px-4 py-3 text-sm outline-none focus:border-gray-900"
                >
                  <option value="">Select customer</option>
                  {customers
                    .filter((customer) => customer.status)
                    .map((customer) => (
                      <option
                        key={customer.customer_id}
                        value={customer.customer_id}
                      >
                        {customer.name}
                      </option>
                    ))}
                </select>
              </div>

              <div>
                <div className="mb-2 flex flex-col justify-between gap-2 sm:flex-row sm:items-center">
                  <label className="text-sm font-medium">Order Items</label>
                  <input
                    type="search"
                    value={itemSearch}
                    onChange={(e) => setItemSearch(e.target.value)}
                    placeholder="Search items..."
                    className="rounded-lg border border-gray-300 px-3 py-2 text-sm outline-none focus:border-gray-900"
                  />
                </div>

                <div className="space-y-3">
                  {lines.map((line, index) => (
                    <div
                      key={index}
                      className="grid gap-3 rounded-lg border border-gray-200 p-3 sm:grid-cols-[minmax(0,1fr)_90px_100px_auto]"
                    >
                      <select
                        value={line.IM_ID || ""}
                        onChange={(e) =>
                          updateLine(index, {
                            IM_ID: Number(e.target.value),
                          })
                        }
                        className="min-w-0 rounded-lg border border-gray-300 px-3 py-2 text-sm"
                      >
                        <option value="">Select item</option>
                        {items.map((item) => (
                          <option key={item.IM_ID} value={item.IM_ID}>
                            {item.SKU} - {item.Item_Name} (
                            {money(item.Price ?? 0)})
                          </option>
                        ))}
                      </select>

                      <input
                        type="number"
                        min="1"
                        value={line.quantity}
                        onChange={(e) =>
                          updateLine(index, {
                            quantity: Number(e.target.value),
                          })
                        }
                        aria-label="Quantity"
                        placeholder="Qty"
                        className="w-full rounded-lg border border-gray-300 px-3 py-2 text-sm"
                      />

                      <input
                        type="number"
                        min="0"
                        max="100"
                        value={line.discount}
                        onChange={(e) =>
                          updateLine(index, {
                            discount: Number(e.target.value),
                          })
                        }
                        aria-label="Discount percentage"
                        placeholder="Discount %"
                        className="w-full rounded-lg border border-gray-300 px-3 py-2 text-sm"
                      />

                      <button
                        type="button"
                        onClick={() => removeLine(index)}
                        disabled={lines.length === 1}
                        className="rounded-lg px-3 py-2 text-sm text-red-600 hover:bg-red-50 disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-30"
                      >
                        Remove
                      </button>
                    </div>
                  ))}
                </div>

                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={addLine}
                  className="mt-3 text-sm font-medium text-gray-700 hover:text-black"
                >
                  + Add another item
                </button>
              </div>

              <div className="flex flex-col-reverse justify-end gap-3 border-t border-gray-200 pt-5 sm:flex-row">
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={resetForm}
                  disabled={saving}
                  className="rounded-lg border border-gray-300 px-5 py-3 text-sm font-medium hover:bg-gray-50"
                >
                  Cancel
                </button>

                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => void handleCreateOrder()}
                  disabled={saving}
                  className="rounded-lg bg-gray-900 px-5 py-3 text-sm font-medium text-white hover:bg-gray-700 disabled:opacity-50"
                >
                  {saving ? "Creating..." : "Create Order"}
                </button>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </main>
  );
}

"use client";

import { useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useState } from "react";
import {
  invoiceAPI,
  salesOrderAPI,
  paymentCollectionAPI,
  accountsReceivableAPI,
  salesReturnAPI,
} from "../../lib/api";

import type { SalesInvoice } from "../../types/invoice";
import type { SalesOrder } from "../../types/order";
import type { Collection } from "../../types/payment";
import type { AccountsReceivable } from "../../types/accounts-receivable";
import type { SalesReturn } from "../../types/return";

type ReportCategory =
  | "Dashboard"
  | "Customer Management"
  | "Sales Processing"
  | "Delivery & Returns"
  | "Billing & Collections";

type ReportRow = {
  title: string;
  period: string;
  count: number;
  amount: number;
  category: ReportCategory;
};

const PAGE_CATEGORIES: ReportCategory[] = [
  "Dashboard",
  "Customer Management",
  "Sales Processing",
  "Delivery & Returns",
  "Billing & Collections",
];

const money = (value: number) =>
  new Intl.NumberFormat("en-PH", {
    style: "currency",
    currency: "PHP",
    minimumFractionDigits: 2,
    maximumFractionDigits: 2,
  }).format(value || 0);

const getToday = () => {
  const date = new Date();
  const year = date.getFullYear();
  const month = String(date.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, "0");
  const day = String(date.getDate()).padStart(2, "0");

  return `${year}-${month}-${day}`;
};

function isWithinDateRange(
  dateValue: string | null | undefined,
  startDate: string,
  endDate: string
) {
  if (!startDate && !endDate) return true;
  if (!dateValue) return false;

  const dateString = dateValue.slice(0, 10);

  if (startDate && dateString < startDate) return false;
  if (endDate && dateString > endDate) return false;

  return true;
}

const categoryDescriptions: Record<ReportCategory, string> = {
  Dashboard: "Overall summary of your sales operations.",
  "Customer Management":
    "Customer balances and receivables information.",
  "Sales Processing":
    "Sales order transactions and order values.",
  "Delivery & Returns":
    "Sales return transactions and returned amounts.",
  "Billing & Collections":
    "Invoices, payment collections, and outstanding receivables.",
};

export default function ReportsPage() {
  const [invoices, setInvoices] = useState<SalesInvoice[]>([]);
  const [orders, setOrders] = useState<SalesOrder[]>([]);
  const [collections, setCollections] = useState<Collection[]>([]);
  const [receivables, setReceivables] = useState<AccountsReceivable[]>([]);
  const [returns, setReturns] = useState<SalesReturn[]>([]);

  const [activeCategory, setActiveCategory] =
    useState<ReportCategory>("Dashboard");

  const [startDate, setStartDate] = useState("");
  const [endDate, setEndDate] = useState("");

  const [appliedStartDate, setAppliedStartDate] = useState("");
  const [appliedEndDate, setAppliedEndDate] = useState("");

  const [rangeError, setRangeError] = useState("");
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [error, setError] = useState("");

  const loadData = useCallback(async () => {
    try {
      setLoading(true);
      setError("");

      const [
        invoiceData,
        orderData,
        collectionData,
        receivableData,
        returnData,
      ] = await Promise.all([
        invoiceAPI.getAll(),
        salesOrderAPI.getAll(),
        paymentCollectionAPI.getAll(),
        accountsReceivableAPI.getAll(),
        salesReturnAPI.getAll(),
      ]);

      setInvoices(invoiceData);
      setOrders(orderData);
      setCollections(collectionData);
      setReceivables(receivableData);
      setReturns(returnData);
    } catch (err) {
      setError(
        err instanceof Error
          ? err.message
          : "Failed to load sales reports."
      );
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    void loadData();
  }, [loadData]);

  function applyDateRange() {
    setRangeError("");

    if (!startDate || !endDate) {
      setRangeError("Please select both a start date and an end date.");
      return;
    }

    if (startDate > endDate) {
      setRangeError("Start date cannot be later than end date.");
      return;
    }

    setAppliedStartDate(startDate);
    setAppliedEndDate(endDate);
  }

  function clearDateRange() {
    setStartDate("");
    setEndDate("");
    setAppliedStartDate("");
    setAppliedEndDate("");
    setRangeError("");
  }

  const dateRangeLabel = useMemo(() => {
    if (!appliedStartDate && !appliedEndDate) {
      return "All Time";
    }

    const formatDate = (value: string) =>
      new Date(`${value}T00:00:00`).toLocaleDateString("en-PH", {
        year: "numeric",
        month: "short",
        day: "numeric",
      });

    return `${formatDate(appliedStartDate)} â€“ ${formatDate(appliedEndDate)}`;
  }, [appliedStartDate, appliedEndDate]);

  const filtered = useMemo(
    () => ({
      invoices: invoices.filter((item) =>
        isWithinDateRange(
          item.invoice_date,
          appliedStartDate,
          appliedEndDate
        )
      ),
      orders: orders.filter((item) =>
        isWithinDateRange(
          item.order_date,
          appliedStartDate,
          appliedEndDate
        )
      ),
      collections: collections.filter((item) =>
        isWithinDateRange(
          item.payment_date,
          appliedStartDate,
          appliedEndDate
        )
      ),
      returns: returns.filter((item) =>
        isWithinDateRange(
          item.return_date,
          appliedStartDate,
          appliedEndDate
        )
      ),
    }),
    [
      invoices,
      orders,
      collections,
      returns,
      appliedStartDate,
      appliedEndDate,
    ]
  );

  const totals = useMemo(
    () => ({
      revenue: filtered.invoices.reduce(
        (sum, item) => sum + Number(item.total_due || 0),
        0
      ),
      orderAmount: filtered.orders.reduce(
        (sum, item) => sum + Number(item.total_amount || 0),
        0
      ),
      collected: filtered.collections.reduce(
        (sum, item) => sum + Number(item.amount_paid || 0),
        0
      ),
      outstanding: receivables.reduce(
        (sum, item) => sum + Number(item.balance || 0),
        0
      ),
      returned: filtered.returns.reduce(
        (sum, item) => sum + Number(item.return_amount || 0),
        0
      ),
    }),
    [filtered, receivables]
  );

  const reportRows = useMemo<ReportRow[]>(
    () => [
      {
        title: "Customer Balances",
        period: "Current Balance",
        count: receivables.length,
        amount: totals.outstanding,
        category: "Customer Management",
      },
      {
        title: "Sales Orders",
        period: "Order Date",
        count: filtered.orders.length,
        amount: totals.orderAmount,
        category: "Sales Processing",
      },
      {
        title: "Sales Returns",
        period: "Return Date",
        count: filtered.returns.length,
        amount: totals.returned,
        category: "Delivery & Returns",
      },
      {
        title: "Sales Revenue",
        period: "Invoice Date",
        count: filtered.invoices.length,
        amount: totals.revenue,
        category: "Billing & Collections",
      },
      {
        title: "Payment Collections",
        period: "Payment Date",
        count: filtered.collections.length,
        amount: totals.collected,
        category: "Billing & Collections",
      },
      {
        title: "Accounts Receivable",
        period: "Current Balance",
        count: receivables.length,
        amount: totals.outstanding,
        category: "Billing & Collections",
      },
    ],
    [filtered, totals, receivables]
  );

  const visibleRows = useMemo(() => {
    if (activeCategory === "Dashboard") {
      return reportRows.filter(
        (row) =>
          row.category === "Sales Processing" ||
          row.category === "Delivery & Returns" ||
          row.category === "Billing & Collections"
      );
    }

    return reportRows.filter(
      (row) => row.category === activeCategory
    );
  }, [reportRows, activeCategory]);

  function exportCSV() {
    const rows = [
      [
        "Report",
        "Category",
        "Period",
        "Total Records",
        "Total Amount (PHP)",
      ],
      ...visibleRows.map((row) => [
        row.title,
        row.category,
        row.period,
        String(row.count),
        row.amount.toFixed(2),
      ]),
    ];

    const csv = rows
      .map((row) =>
        row
          .map((value) => `"${String(value).replace(/"/g, '""')}"`)
          .join(",")
      )
      .join("\n");

    const blob = new Blob(["\uFEFF" + csv], {
      type: "text/csv;charset=utf-8;",
    });

    const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
    const link = document.createElement("a");

    link.href = url;
    link.download = `sales-reports-${activeCategory
      .toLowerCase()
      .replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, "-")}-${getToday()}.csv`;

    document.body.appendChild(link);
    link.click();
    document.body.removeChild(link);

    URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
  }

  return (
    <div className="min-h-full space-y-5 bg-green-50 p-4 text-slate-800 sm:p-6">
      <section className="rounded-xl border border-[#D1D5DB] bg-[#E5E7EB] p-4 shadow-sm">
        <div className="mb-4">
          <h2 className="text-sm font-semibold text-slate-800">
            Reporting Date Range
          </h2>
          <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-slate-500">
            Select the start and end dates to filter your report data.
          </p>
        </div>

        <div className="flex flex-col gap-3 lg:flex-row lg:items-end">
          <div className="w-full lg:flex-1">
            <label
              htmlFor="report-start-date"
              className="mb-1.5 block text-xs font-medium text-slate-600"
            >
              Start Date
            </label>
            <input
              id="report-start-date"
              type="date"
              value={startDate}
              max={endDate || undefined}
              onChange={(event) => {
                setStartDate(event.target.value);
                setRangeError("");
              }}
              className="w-full rounded-lg border border-[#D1D5DB] bg-white px-3 py-2.5 text-sm text-slate-800 outline-none focus:border-blue-500 focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-100"
            />
          </div>

          <div className="w-full lg:flex-1">
            <label
              htmlFor="report-end-date"
              className="mb-1.5 block text-xs font-medium text-slate-600"
            >
              End Date
            </label>
            <input
              id="report-end-date"
              type="date"
              value={endDate}
              min={startDate || undefined}
              onChange={(event) => {
                setEndDate(event.target.value);
                setRangeError("");
              }}
              className="w-full rounded-lg border border-[#D1D5DB] bg-white px-3 py-2.5 text-sm text-slate-800 outline-none focus:border-blue-500 focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-100"
            />
          </div>

          <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={applyDateRange}
              className="rounded-lg bg-blue-700 px-5 py-2.5 text-sm font-semibold text-white transition hover:bg-blue-800"
            >
              Apply Filter
            </button>

            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={clearDateRange}
              className="rounded-lg border border-[#D1D5DB] bg-white px-5 py-2.5 text-sm font-semibold text-slate-700 transition hover:bg-slate-50"
            >
              Clear
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>

        {rangeError && (
          <p className="mt-3 text-xs font-medium text-red-600">
            {rangeError}
          </p>
        )}

        <div className="mt-3 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
          <span className="text-xs text-slate-500">
            Applied date range:
          </span>
          <span className="rounded-md bg-white px-2.5 py-1 text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700">
            {dateRangeLabel}
          </span>
        </div>

        <div
          role="alert"

        >

            âœ•

        <div className="flex gap-2 overflow-x-auto pb-2">

                />

                color="text-orange-700"
              />
            )}

            {activeCategory === "Sales Processing" && (
              <SummaryCard
                title="Sales Orders"
                value={String(filtered.orders.length)}
                description={`${money(totals.orderAmount)} total order value`}

              />
            )}

            {activeCategory === "Delivery & Returns" && (

                title="Sales Returns"
                value={money(totals.returned)}
                description={`${filtered.returns.length} return records`}
                color="text-red-700"
              />
            )}

            {activeCategory === "Billing & Collections" && (
              <>
                <SummaryCard
                  title="Sales Revenue"
                  value={money(totals.revenue)}
                  description="Based on invoice dates"
                  color="text-green-700"
                />

                <SummaryCard
                  title="Total Collections"
                  value={money(totals.collected)}
                  description="Based on payment dates"
                  color="text-purple-700"
                />

                <SummaryCard
                  title="Outstanding Receivables"
                  value={money(totals.outstanding)}
                  description="Current balance across all periods"
                  color="text-orange-700"
                />
              </>
            )}
          </section>

          <section className="overflow-hidden rounded-xl border border-[#D1D5DB] bg-[#E5E7EB] shadow-sm">
            <div className="border-b border-[#D1D5DB] p-5">
              <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3">
                <div>
                  <h2 className="font-semibold text-slate-900">
                    Report Summary
                  </h2>
                  <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-slate-500">
                    {visibleRows.length} report{" "}
                    {visibleRows.length === 1 ? "record" : "records"}{" "}
                    displayed.
                  </p>
                </div>

                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={exportCSV}
                  disabled={visibleRows.length === 0}
                  className="inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-lg bg-green-700 px-5 py-2.5 text-sm font-semibold text-white transition hover:bg-green-800 disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-50"
                >
                  <span aria-hidden="true">â†“</span>
                  Export to CSV
                </button>
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className="overflow-x-auto">
              <table className="w-full text-left text-sm">
                <thead className="border-b border-[#D1D5DB] bg-[#D1D5DB] text-xs uppercase tracking-wide text-slate-600">
                  <tr>
                    <th className="whitespace-nowrap px-5 py-3">
                      Report
                    </th>
                    <th className="whitespace-nowrap px-5 py-3">
                      Date Basis
                    </th>
                    <th className="whitespace-nowrap px-5 py-3 text-center">
                      Total Records
                    </th>
                    <th className="whitespace-nowrap px-5 py-3 text-right">
                      Total Amount
                    </th>
                  </tr>
                </thead>

                <tbody className="divide-y divide-[#D1D5DB]">
                  {visibleRows.length > 0 ? (
                    visibleRows.map((row) => (
                      <tr
                        key={`${row.category}-${row.title}`}
                        className="transition-colors hover:bg-[#DDE1E5]"
                      >
                        <td className="whitespace-nowrap px-5 py-4 font-medium text-slate-800">
                          {row.title}
                        </td>

                        <td className="whitespace-nowrap px-5 py-4 text-slate-600">
                          {row.period}
                        </td>

                        <td className="px-5 py-4 text-center text-slate-700">
                          {row.count.toLocaleString("en-PH")}
                        </td>

                        <td className="whitespace-nowrap px-5 py-4 text-right font-semibold text-slate-800">
                          {money(row.amount)}
                        </td>
                      </tr>
                    ))
                  ) : (
                    <tr>
                      <td
                        colSpan={4}
                        className="px-5 py-10 text-center text-sm text-slate-500"
                      >
                        No report records available for this category.
                      </td>
                    </tr>
                  )}
                </tbody>
              </table>
            </div>
          </section>
        </>
      ) : null}
    </div>
  );
}

function SummaryCard({
  title,
  value,
  description,
  color,
}: {
  title: string;
  value: string;
  description: string;
  color: string;
}) {
  return (
    <div className="rounded-xl border border-[#D1D5DB] bg-[#E5E7EB] p-5 shadow-sm">
      <p className="text-sm font-medium text-slate-500">{title}</p>
      <p className={`mt-2 text-2xl font-bold ${color}`}>
        {value}
      </p>
      <p className="mt-2 text-xs text-slate-500">{description}</p>
    </div>
  );
}

    maximumFractionDigits: 2,
  })}`;

export default function ReturnsPage() {

  const [invoices, setInvoices] = useState<SalesInvoice[]>([]);
  const [customers, setCustomers] = useState<Customer[]>([]);
