    try {
      const [
        returnData,
        orderData,
        invoiceData,
        customerData,
        itemData,
      ] = await Promise.all([
        salesReturnAPI.getAll(),
        salesOrderAPI.getAll(),
        invoiceAPI.getAll(),
        customerAPI.getAll(),
        salesOrderAPI.getItems(),
      ]);

      setReturns(returnData);
      setOrders(orderData);
      setInvoices(invoiceData);
      setCustomers(customerData);
      setItems(itemData);
    } catch (err) {
      setError(
        err instanceof Error
          ? err.message
          : "Unable to load sales returns."
      );
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    void loadData();
  }, [loadData]);

  const orderMap = useMemo(
    () => new Map(orders.map((order) => [order.order_id, order])),
    [orders]
  );

  const customerMap = useMemo(
    () => new Map(customers.map((customer) => [customer.customer_id, customer])),
    [customers]
  );

  const itemMap = useMemo(
    () => new Map(items.map((item) => [item.IM_ID, item])),
    [items]
  );

  const invoiceMap = useMemo(
    () => new Map(invoices.map((invoice) => [invoice.order_id, invoice])),
    [invoices]
  );

  const returnedQuantities = useMemo(() => {
    const totals = new Map<number, number>();

    returns.forEach((record) => {
      const current = totals.get(record.Order_Detail_ID) ?? 0;

      totals.set(
        record.Order_Detail_ID,
        current + record.quantity
      );
    });

    return totals;
  }, [returns]);

  const availableDetails = useMemo(
    () =>
      orders
        .filter((order) => invoiceMap.has(order.order_id))
        .filter((order) => order.status !== "Cancelled")
        .flatMap((order) =>
          order.details
            .filter((detail) => {
              const returned =
                returnedQuantities.get(detail.order_detail_id) ?? 0;

              return returned < detail.quantity;
            })
            .map((detail) => ({
              ...detail,
              order,
              remaining:
                detail.quantity -
                (returnedQuantities.get(detail.order_detail_id) ?? 0),
            }))
        ),
    [orders, invoiceMap, returnedQuantities]
  );

  const selectedDetail = availableDetails.find(
    (detail) => detail.order_detail_id === Number(orderDetailId)
  );

  const selectedInvoice = selectedDetail
    ? invoiceMap.get(selectedDetail.order_id)
    : undefined;

  const calculatedAmount =
    selectedDetail && Number(quantity) > 0
      ? (Number(selectedDetail.line_total) /
          selectedDetail.quantity) *
        Number(quantity)
      : 0;

  const totalReturnedAmount = returns.reduce(
    (sum, record) => sum + Number(record.return_amount ?? 0),
    0
  );

  const totalReturnedItems = returns.reduce(
    (sum, record) => sum + record.quantity,
    0
  );

  const filteredReturns = useMemo(() => {
    const term = search.trim().toLowerCase();

    if (!term) return returns;

    return returns.filter((record) => {
      const order = orders.find(
        (item) => item.order_id ===
          orderMap.get(
            orderMap.get(
              invoices.find((invoice) =>
                invoice.invoice_id === record.invoice_id
              )?.order_id ?? -1
            )?.order_id ?? -1
          )?.order_id
      );

      const relatedOrder = orders.find((item) =>
        item.details.some(
          (detail) =>
            detail.order_detail_id === record.Order_Detail_ID
        )
      );

      const customerName = relatedOrder
        ? customerMap.get(relatedOrder.customer_id)?.name ?? ""
        : "";

      const itemName = relatedOrder
        ? itemMap.get(
            relatedOrder.details.find(
              (detail) =>
                detail.order_detail_id === record.Order_Detail_ID
            )?.IM_ID ?? -1
          )?.Item_Name ?? ""
        : "";

      return (
        String(record.return_id).includes(term) ||
        String(record.invoice_id).includes(term) ||
        String(relatedOrder?.order_id ?? "").includes(term) ||
        customerName.toLowerCase().includes(term) ||
        itemName.toLowerCase().includes(term)
      );
    });
  }, [returns, orders, invoices, orderMap, customerMap, itemMap]);

  function resetForm() {
    setOrderDetailId("");
    setReturnDate(new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10));
    setQuantity("1");
    setReason("");
    setReturnAmount("");
    setShowForm(false);
  }

  async function handleCreateReturn() {
    setError("");

    if (!selectedDetail || !selectedInvoice) {
      setError("Please select an available sales order item.");
      return;
    }

    const returnQuantity = Number(quantity);

    if (!Number.isInteger(returnQuantity) || returnQuantity <= 0) {
      setError("Return quantity must be a positive whole number.");
      return;
    }

    if (returnQuantity > selectedDetail.remaining) {
      setError(
        `Only ${selectedDetail.remaining} item(s) remain eligible for return.`
      );
      return;
    }

    if (!returnDate) {
      setError("Please select a return date.");
      return;
    }

    if (returnAmount.trim() && Number(returnAmount) < 0) {
      setError("Return amount cannot be negative.");
      return;
    }

    const data: SalesReturnCreate = {
      Order_Detail_ID: selectedDetail.order_detail_id,
      invoice_id: selectedInvoice.invoice_id,
      return_date: returnDate,
      quantity: returnQuantity,
      reason: reason.trim() || null,
      return_amount: returnAmount.trim()
        ? Number(returnAmount)
        : null,
    };

    setSaving(true);

    try {
      await salesReturnAPI.create(data);
      resetForm();
      await loadData();
    } catch (err) {
      setError(
        err instanceof Error
          ? err.message
          : "Unable to create sales return."
      );
    } finally {
      setSaving(false);
    }
  }

  return (
    <main className="min-h-screen bg-gray-50 p-6 text-gray-900 md:p-10">
      <div className="mx-auto max-w-7xl space-y-8">

        <header>

          <div className="mt-2 flex flex-col justify-between gap-4 sm:flex-row sm:items-center">

            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => {
                setError("");
                setShowForm(true);
              }}
              disabled={availableDetails.length === 0}
              className="rounded-lg bg-gray-900 px-5 py-3 text-sm font-medium text-white hover:bg-gray-700 disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-50"
            >
              + Create Sales Return
            </button>
          </div>
        </header>

        {error && (
          <div
            role="alert"
            className="rounded-lg border border-red-200 bg-red-50 p-4 text-sm text-red-700"
          >
            {error}
          </div>
        )}

        <section className="grid gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3">
          <div className="rounded-xl border border-gray-200 bg-white p-5">
            <p className="text-sm text-gray-500">Total Returns</p>
            <p className="mt-2 text-2xl font-semibold">
              {returns.length}
            </p>
          </div>

          <div className="rounded-xl border border-gray-200 bg-white p-5">
            <p className="text-sm text-gray-500">Total Returned Amount</p>
            <p className="mt-2 text-2xl font-semibold">
              {money(totalReturnedAmount)}
            </p>
          </div>

          <div className="rounded-xl border border-gray-200 bg-white p-5">
            <p className="text-sm text-gray-500">Total Returned Items</p>
            <p className="mt-2 text-2xl font-semibold">
              {totalReturnedItems}
            </p>
          </div>
        </section>

        <section className="overflow-hidden rounded-xl border border-gray-200 bg-white">
          <div className="flex flex-col justify-between gap-4 border-b border-gray-200 p-5 sm:flex-row sm:items-center">
            <div>
              <h2 className="font-semibold">Sales Return History</h2>
              <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-gray-500">
                View all returned items and their details.
              </p>
            </div>

            <input
              type="search"
              value={search}
              onChange={(e) => setSearch(e.target.value)}
              placeholder="Search return, order, item, or customer..."
              className="w-full rounded-lg border border-gray-300 px-4 py-2 text-sm outline-none focus:border-gray-900 sm:max-w-sm"
            />
          </div>

          <div className="overflow-x-auto">
            <table className="w-full text-left text-sm">
              <thead className="bg-gray-50 text-gray-500">
                <tr>
                  <th className="px-5 py-4 font-medium">Return No.</th>
                  <th className="px-5 py-4 font-medium">Order No.</th>
                  <th className="px-5 py-4 font-medium">Invoice No.</th>
                  <th className="px-5 py-4 font-medium">Customer</th>
                  <th className="px-5 py-4 font-medium">Returned Item</th>
                  <th className="px-5 py-4 font-medium">Return Date</th>
                  <th className="px-5 py-4 font-medium">Quantity</th>
                  <th className="px-5 py-4 font-medium">Return Amount</th>
                </tr>
              </thead>

              <tbody className="divide-y divide-gray-100">
                {loading ? (
                  <tr>
                    <td colSpan={8} className="px-5 py-10 text-center text-gray-500">
                      Loading sales returns...
                    </td>
                  </tr>
                ) : filteredReturns.length === 0 ? (
                  <tr>
                    <td colSpan={8} className="px-5 py-10 text-center text-gray-500">
                      No sales returns found.
                    </td>
                  </tr>
                ) : (
                  filteredReturns.map((record) => {
                    const relatedOrder = orders.find((order) =>
                      order.details.some(
                        (detail) =>
                          detail.order_detail_id === record.Order_Detail_ID
                      )
                    );

                    const relatedDetail = relatedOrder?.details.find(
                      (detail) =>
                        detail.order_detail_id === record.Order_Detail_ID
                    );

                    const customer = relatedOrder
                      ? customerMap.get(relatedOrder.customer_id)
                      : undefined;

                    const item = relatedDetail
                      ? itemMap.get(relatedDetail.IM_ID)
                      : undefined;

                    return (
                      <tr key={record.return_id} className="hover:bg-gray-50">
                        <td className="px-5 py-4 font-medium">
                          RET-{String(record.return_id).padStart(4, "0")}
                        </td>

                        <td className="px-5 py-4">
                          {relatedOrder
                            ? `SO-${String(relatedOrder.order_id).padStart(4, "0")}`
                            : "â€”"}
                        </td>

                        <td className="px-5 py-4">
                          SI-{String(record.invoice_id).padStart(4, "0")}
                        </td>

                        <td className="px-5 py-4">
                          {customer?.name ?? "Unknown customer"}
                        </td>

                        <td className="px-5 py-4">
                          {item?.Item_Name ?? `Item #${relatedDetail?.IM_ID ?? "â€”"}`}
                          {item?.SKU && (
                            <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-gray-500">
                              {item.SKU}
                            </p>
                          )}
                        </td>

                        <td className="px-5 py-4">
                          {new Date(
                            `${record.return_date}T00:00:00`
                          ).toLocaleDateString("en-PH")}
                        </td>

                        <td className="px-5 py-4">{record.quantity}</td>

                        <td className="px-5 py-4 font-medium">
                          {money(record.return_amount ?? 0)}
                        </td>
                      </tr>
                    );
                  })
                )}
              </tbody>
            </table>
          </div>
        </section>
      </div>

      {showForm && (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center overflow-y-auto bg-black/40 p-4">
          <div className="my-auto w-full max-w-lg rounded-xl bg-white p-6 shadow-xl">

            <div className="mb-6 flex items-start justify-between gap-4">
              <div>
                <h2 className="text-xl font-bold">
                  Create Sales Return
                </h2>

                <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-gray-500">
                  Record an item returned by a customer.
                </p>
              </div>

              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={resetForm}
                disabled={saving}
                className="text-gray-500 hover:text-gray-900"
                aria-label="Close form"
              >
                âœ•
              </button>
            </div>

            <div className="space-y-5">

              <div>
                <label className="mb-2 block text-sm font-medium">
                  Sales Order Item
                </label>

                <select
                  value={orderDetailId}
                  onChange={(e) => {
                    setOrderDetailId(e.target.value);
                    setQuantity("1");
                    setReturnAmount("");
                  }}
                  className="w-full rounded-lg border border-gray-300 px-4 py-3 text-sm outline-none focus:border-gray-900"
                >
                  <option value="">Select order item</option>

                  {availableDetails.map((detail) => {
                    const item = itemMap.get(detail.IM_ID);

                    return (
                      <option
                        key={detail.order_detail_id}
                        value={detail.order_detail_id}
                      >
                        SO-{String(detail.order_id).padStart(4, "0")}
                        {" â€” "}
                        {item?.Item_Name ?? `Item #${detail.IM_ID}`}
                        {" â€” "}
                        {detail.remaining} remaining
                      </option>
                    );
                  })}
                </select>
              </div>

              {selectedDetail && (
                <div className="rounded-lg bg-gray-50 p-4">
                  <p className="text-sm text-gray-500">
                    Selected Item
                  </p>

                  <p className="mt-1 font-semibold">
                    {itemMap.get(selectedDetail.IM_ID)?.Item_Name ??
                      `Item #${selectedDetail.IM_ID}`}
                  </p>

                  <div className="mt-3 grid grid-cols-2 gap-3 text-sm">
                    <div>
                      <p className="text-gray-500">Ordered Quantity</p>
                      <p className="font-medium">
                        {selectedDetail.quantity}
                      </p>
                    </div>

                    <div>
                      <p className="text-gray-500">Remaining Quantity</p>
                      <p className="font-medium">
                        {selectedDetail.remaining}
                      </p>
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  <p className="mt-3 text-sm text-gray-500">
                    Invoice: SI-{String(selectedInvoice?.invoice_id).padStart(4, "0")}
                  </p>
                </div>
              )}

              <div>
                <label className="mb-2 block text-sm font-medium">
                  Return Quantity
                </label>

                <input
                  type="number"
                  min="1"
                  max={selectedDetail?.remaining ?? 1}
                  value={quantity}
                  onChange={(e) => setQuantity(e.target.value)}
                  className="w-full rounded-lg border border-gray-300 px-4 py-3 text-sm outline-none focus:border-gray-900"
                />
              </div>

              <div>
                <label className="mb-2 block text-sm font-medium">
                  Return Date
                </label>

                <input
                  type="date"
                  value={returnDate}
                  onChange={(e) => setReturnDate(e.target.value)}
                  className="w-full rounded-lg border border-gray-300 px-4 py-3 text-sm outline-none focus:border-gray-900"
                />
              </div>

              <div>
                <label className="mb-2 block text-sm font-medium">
                  Reason for Return
                </label>

                <textarea
                  value={reason}
                  onChange={(e) => setReason(e.target.value)}
                  rows={3}
                  placeholder="Enter the reason for returning the item..."
                  className="w-full rounded-lg border border-gray-300 px-4 py-3 text-sm outline-none focus:border-gray-900"
                />
              </div>

              <div>
                <label className="mb-2 block text-sm font-medium">
                  Return Amount (Optional)
                </label>

                <input
                  type="number"
                  min="0"
                  step="0.01"
                  value={returnAmount}
                  onChange={(e) => setReturnAmount(e.target.value)}
                  placeholder="Leave blank for automatic calculation"
                  className="w-full rounded-lg border border-gray-300 px-4 py-3 text-sm outline-none focus:border-gray-900"
                />

                <p className="mt-2 text-xs text-gray-500">
                  Estimated amount: {money(calculatedAmount)}.
                  Leave blank to let the backend calculate it.
                </p>
              </div>

              <div className="flex flex-col-reverse justify-end gap-3 border-t border-gray-200 pt-5 sm:flex-row">
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={resetForm}
                  disabled={saving}
                  className="rounded-lg border border-gray-300 px-5 py-3 text-sm font-medium hover:bg-gray-50"
                >
                  Cancel
                </button>

                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => void handleCreateReturn()}
                  disabled={saving || availableDetails.length === 0}
                  className="rounded-lg bg-gray-900 px-5 py-3 text-sm font-medium text-white hover:bg-gray-700 disabled:opacity-50"
                >
                  {saving ? "Saving..." : "Create Return"}
                </button>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </main>
  );
}

"use client";

import { useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useState } from "react";
import { salesOrderAPI } from "../../lib/api";
import type { FinishedGoodItem } from "../../types/order";

const PAGE_SIZE = 10;

const formatCurrency = (value: string | number | null | undefined) => {
  if (value === null || value === undefined || value === "") {
    return "â€”";
  }

  return new Intl.NumberFormat("en-PH", {
    style: "currency",
    currency: "PHP",
    minimumFractionDigits: 2,
    maximumFractionDigits: 2,
  }).format(Number(value));
};

const formatPercentage = (
  value: string | number | null | undefined,
) => {
  if (value === null || value === undefined || value === "") {
    return "â€”";
  }

  return `${Number(value).toFixed(2)}%`;
};

export default function SellingPricesPage() {
  const [items, setItems] = useState<FinishedGoodItem[]>([]);
  const [search, setSearch] = useState("");
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState("");
  const [success, setSuccess] = useState("");
  const [page, setPage] = useState(1);

  const [selectedItem, setSelectedItem] =
    useState<FinishedGoodItem | null>(null);
  const [markupInput, setMarkupInput] = useState("");

  const loadItems = useCallback(async (searchTerm = "") => {
    try {
      setLoading(true);
      setError("");

      const data = await salesOrderAPI.getFinishedGoods(searchTerm);
      setItems(data);
    } catch (err) {
      setError(
        err instanceof Error
          ? err.message
          : "Unable to load finished goods.",
      );
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    const timeout = setTimeout(() => {
      setPage(1);
      loadItems(search.trim());
    }, 300);

    return () => clearTimeout(timeout);
  }, [search, loadItems]);

  const productionCost = Number(selectedItem?.production_cost ?? 0);
  const markupPercentage = Number(markupInput);

  const calculatedPrice =
    productionCost * (1 + markupPercentage / 100);

  const validMarkup =
    markupInput.trim() !== "" &&
    Number.isFinite(markupPercentage) &&
    markupPercentage >= 0;

  const totalPages = Math.max(
    1,
    Math.ceil(items.length / PAGE_SIZE),
  );

  const paginatedItems = useMemo(() => {
    const start = (page - 1) * PAGE_SIZE;
    return items.slice(start, start + PAGE_SIZE);
  }, [items, page]);

  const openEditor = (item: FinishedGoodItem) => {
    setSelectedItem(item);
    setMarkupInput(
      item.markup_percentage === null ||
        item.markup_percentage === undefined
        ? ""
        : String(item.markup_percentage),
    );
    setError("");
    setSuccess("");
  };

  const closeEditor = () => {
    if (saving) return;

    setSelectedItem(null);
    setMarkupInput("");
    setError("");
  };

  const saveSellingPrice = async () => {
    if (!selectedItem) return;

    if (
      selectedItem.production_cost === null ||
      selectedItem.production_cost === undefined
    ) {
      setError(
        "Production cost has not been provided by Finance for this item.",
      );
      return;
    }

    if (!validMarkup) {
      setError("Please enter a valid markup percentage.");
      return;
    }

    try {
      setSaving(true);
      setError("");

      const updatedItem = await salesOrderAPI.updateSellingPrice(
        selectedItem.IM_ID,
        markupPercentage,
      );

      setItems((currentItems) =>
        currentItems.map((item) =>
          item.IM_ID === selectedItem.IM_ID
            ? { ...item, ...updatedItem }
            : item,
        ),
      );

      setSuccess(
        `Selling price for ${selectedItem.Item_Name} was updated successfully.`,
      );

      setSelectedItem(null);
      setMarkupInput("");
    } catch (err) {
      setError(
        err instanceof Error
          ? err.message
          : "Unable to update the selling price.",
      );
    } finally {
      setSaving(false);
    }
  };

  const firstRecord =
    items.length === 0 ? 0 : (page - 1) * PAGE_SIZE + 1;

  const lastRecord = Math.min(page * PAGE_SIZE, items.length);

  return (
    <div className="min-h-full bg-green-50 p-6 text-[#374151]">
      <div className="mb-6">
        <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-gray-500">
          Set selling prices for finished goods using the production
          cost provided by Finance and your chosen markup percentage.
        </p>
      </div>

      {success && (
        <div className="mb-4 rounded-lg border border-green-200 bg-green-50 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-green-800">
          {success}
        </div>
      )}

      {error && !selectedItem && (
        <div className="mb-4 rounded-lg border border-red-200 bg-red-50 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-red-700">
          {error}
        </div>
      )}

      <section className="rounded-xl border border-[#D1D5DB] bg-[#E5E7EB] p-5">
        <div className="mb-5 flex flex-col justify-between gap-4 sm:flex-row sm:items-center">
          <div>
            <h2 className="text-lg font-semibold text-[#374151]">
              Finished Goods
            </h2>
            <p className="text-sm text-gray-600">
              Only finished goods are displayed in this list.
            </p>
          </div>

          <div className="w-full sm:max-w-xs">
            <input
              type="text"
              value={search}
              onChange={(event) => setSearch(event.target.value)}
              placeholder="Search item name or SKU..."
              className="w-full rounded-lg border border-[#D1D5DB] bg-white px-4 py-2.5 text-sm outline-none transition focus:border-[#3F7657] focus:ring-2 focus:ring-[#3F7657]/20"
            />
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="overflow-x-auto rounded-lg border border-[#D1D5DB] bg-white">
          <table className="w-full min-w-[900px] text-left text-sm">
            <thead className="bg-[#244C3A] text-white">
              <tr>
                <th className="px-4 py-3 font-semibold">Item</th>
                <th className="px-4 py-3 font-semibold">SKU</th>
                <th className="px-4 py-3 text-right font-semibold">
                  Production Cost
                </th>
                <th className="px-4 py-3 text-right font-semibold">
                  Markup
                </th>
                <th className="px-4 py-3 text-right font-semibold">
                  Selling Price
                </th>
                <th className="px-4 py-3 text-center font-semibold">
                  Action
                </th>
              </tr>
            </thead>

            <tbody className="divide-y divide-gray-200">
              {loading ? (
                <tr>
                  <td
                    colSpan={6}
                    className="px-4 py-10 text-center text-gray-500"
                  >
                    Loading finished goods...
                  </td>
                </tr>
              ) : paginatedItems.length === 0 ? (
                <tr>
                  <td
                    colSpan={6}
                    className="px-4 py-10 text-center text-gray-500"
                  >
                    No finished goods found.
                  </td>
                </tr>
              ) : (
                paginatedItems.map((item) => (
                  <tr
                    key={item.IM_ID}
                    className="transition hover:bg-[#F3F6F4]"
                  >
                    <td className="px-4 py-3 font-medium text-gray-800">
                      {item.Item_Name}
                    </td>

                    <td className="px-4 py-3 text-gray-600">
                      {item.SKU}
                    </td>

                    <td className="px-4 py-3 text-right tabular-nums">
                      {formatCurrency(item.production_cost)}
                    </td>

                    <td className="px-4 py-3 text-right tabular-nums">
                      {formatPercentage(item.markup_percentage)}
                    </td>

                    <td className="px-4 py-3 text-right font-semibold tabular-nums text-[#244C3A]">
                      {formatCurrency(item.Price)}
                    </td>

                    <td className="px-4 py-3 text-center">
                      <button
                        type="button"
                        onClick={() => openEditor(item)}
                        className="rounded-md bg-[#3F7657] px-4 py-2 text-xs font-semibold text-white transition hover:bg-[#315F46]"
                      >
                        Set Price
                      </button>
                    </td>
                  </tr>
                ))
              )}
            </tbody>
          </table>
        </div>

        <div className="mt-4 flex flex-col items-center justify-between gap-3 text-sm sm:flex-row">
          <p className="text-gray-600">
            Showing {firstRecord} to {lastRecord} of {items.length} items
          </p>

          <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
            <button
              type="button"
              disabled={page <= 1 || loading}
              onClick={() =>
                setPage((current) => Math.max(1, current - 1))
              }
              className="rounded-md border border-[#D1D5DB] bg-white px-3 py-2 text-gray-700 transition hover:bg-gray-100 disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-40"
            >
              Previous
            </button>

            <span className="text-gray-600">
              Page {page} of {totalPages}
            </span>

            <button
              type="button"
              disabled={page >= totalPages || loading}
              onClick={() =>
                setPage((current) => Math.min(totalPages, current + 1))
              }
              className="rounded-md border border-[#D1D5DB] bg-white px-3 py-2 text-gray-700 transition hover:bg-gray-100 disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-40"
            >
              Next
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </section>

      {selectedItem && (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center bg-black/40 p-4">
          <div className="w-full max-w-lg rounded-xl bg-white shadow-xl">
            <div className="rounded-t-xl bg-[#244C3A] px-6 py-4">
              <h2 className="text-lg font-semibold text-white">
                Set Selling Price
              </h2>
              <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-green-100">
                {selectedItem.Item_Name} ({selectedItem.SKU})
              </p>
            </div>

            <div className="space-y-5 p-6">
              {error && (
                <div className="rounded-lg border border-red-200 bg-red-50 px-3 py-2 text-sm text-red-700">
                  {error}
                </div>
              )}

              <div>
                <label className="mb-1 block text-sm font-medium text-gray-700">
                  Production Cost
                </label>
                <div className="rounded-lg border border-[#D1D5DB] bg-gray-100 px-4 py-3 font-semibold text-gray-700">
                  {formatCurrency(selectedItem.production_cost)}
                </div>
                <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-gray-500">
                  Provided by Finance. This value cannot be edited here.
                </p>
              </div>

              <div>
                <label
                  htmlFor="markup"
                  className="mb-1 block text-sm font-medium text-gray-700"
                >
                  Markup Percentage (%)
                </label>
                <input
                  id="markup"
                  type="number"
                  min="0"
                  step="0.01"
                  value={markupInput}
                  onChange={(event) => setMarkupInput(event.target.value)}
                  placeholder="Enter markup percentage"
                  className="w-full rounded-lg border border-[#D1D5DB] px-4 py-3 outline-none transition focus:border-[#3F7657] focus:ring-2 focus:ring-[#3F7657]/20"
                />
              </div>

              <div className="rounded-lg border border-[#C8D8CC] bg-[#E8F0EA] p-4">
                <p className="text-sm font-medium text-[#315F46]">
                  Calculated Selling Price
                </p>

                <p className="mt-1 text-2xl font-bold text-[#244C3A]">
                  {validMarkup &&
                  selectedItem.production_cost !== null &&
                  selectedItem.production_cost !== undefined
                    ? formatCurrency(calculatedPrice)
                    : "â€”"}
                </p>

                <p className="mt-2 text-xs text-gray-600">
                  Production Cost Ã— (1 + Markup Ã· 100)
                </p>
              </div>

              <div className="flex justify-end gap-3 pt-2">
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={closeEditor}
                  disabled={saving}
                  className="rounded-lg border border-[#D1D5DB] px-5 py-2.5 text-sm font-medium text-gray-700 transition hover:bg-gray-100 disabled:opacity-50"
                >
                  Cancel
                </button>

                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={saveSellingPrice}
                  disabled={
                    saving ||
                    !validMarkup ||
                    selectedItem.production_cost === null ||
                    selectedItem.production_cost === undefined
                  }
                  className="rounded-lg bg-[#3F7657] px-5 py-2.5 text-sm font-semibold text-white transition hover:bg-[#315F46] disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-50"
                >
                  {saving ? "Saving..." : "Save Selling Price"}
                </button>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

"use client";

import { useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useState } from "react";
import {
  paymentCollectionAPI,
  salesOrderAPI,
  invoiceAPI,
  accountsReceivableAPI,
  salesReturnAPI,
} from "../lib/api";

import type { Collection } from "../types/payment";
import type { SalesOrder, SalesOrderItem } from "../types/order";
import type { SalesInvoice } from "../types/invoice";
import type { AccountsReceivable } from "../types/accounts-receivable";
import type { SalesReturn } from "../types/return";

const PAGE_TITLE = "Sales Analytics";

const money = (value: string | number | null | undefined) => {
  const amount = Number(value ?? 0);

  return `â‚±${(Number.isFinite(amount) ? amount : 0).toLocaleString(
    "en-PH",
    {
      minimumFractionDigits: 2,
      maximumFractionDigits: 2,
    }
  )}`;
};

const numberValue = (value: string | number | null | undefined) => {
  const result = Number(value ?? 0);
  return Number.isFinite(result) ? result : 0;
};

const formatDate = (value: string | null | undefined) => {
  if (!value) return "â€”";

  const datePart = value.split("T")[0];
  const date = new Date(`${datePart}T00:00:00`);

  if (Number.isNaN(date.getTime())) return "â€”";

  return date.toLocaleDateString("en-PH", {
    year: "numeric",
    month: "long",
    day: "numeric",
  });
};

const getMonthKey = (value: string) => value.split("T")[0].slice(0, 7);

const getLastSixMonths = () => {
  const months: { key: string; label: string }[] = [];
  const today = new Date();

  for (let i = 5; i >= 0; i--) {
    const date = new Date(
      today.getFullYear(),
      today.getMonth() - i,
      1
    );

    months.push({
      key: `${date.getFullYear()}-${String(
        date.getMonth() + 1
      ).padStart(2, "0")}`,
      label: date.toLocaleDateString("en-PH", {
        month: "short",
        year: "2-digit",
      }),
    });
  }

  return months;
};

const cardClass =
  "rounded-xl border border-[#D1D5DB] bg-[#E5E7EB] p-5 shadow-sm";

export default function DashboardPage() {
  const [orders, setOrders] = useState<SalesOrder[]>([]);
  const [invoices, setInvoices] = useState<SalesInvoice[]>([]);
  const [collections, setCollections] = useState<Collection[]>([]);
  const [receivables, setReceivables] = useState<AccountsReceivable[]>([]);
  const [returns, setReturns] = useState<SalesReturn[]>([]);
  const [items, setItems] = useState<SalesOrderItem[]>([]);

  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [error, setError] = useState("");

  const loadDashboard = useCallback(async () => {
    try {
      setLoading(true);
      setError("");

      const [
        orderData,
        invoiceData,
        collectionData,
        receivableData,
        returnData,
        itemData,
      ] = await Promise.all([
        salesOrderAPI.getAll(),
        invoiceAPI.getAll(),
        paymentCollectionAPI.getAll(),
        accountsReceivableAPI.getAll(),
        salesReturnAPI.getAll(),
        salesOrderAPI.getItems(""),
      ]);

      setOrders(orderData);
      setInvoices(invoiceData);
      setCollections(collectionData);
      setReceivables(receivableData);
      setReturns(returnData);
      setItems(itemData);
    } catch (err) {
      setError(
        err instanceof Error
          ? err.message
          : "Unable to load sales analytics."
      );
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    loadDashboard();
  }, [loadDashboard]);

  const analytics = useMemo(() => {
    const totalSales = invoices.reduce(
      (sum, invoice) => sum + numberValue(invoice.total_due),
      0
    );

    const totalCollections = collections.reduce(
      (sum, collection) => sum + numberValue(collection.amount_paid),
      0
    );

    const outstandingBalance = receivables.reduce(
      (sum, record) => sum + numberValue(record.balance),
      0
    );

    const totalReturns = returns.reduce(
      (sum, record) => sum + numberValue(record.return_amount),
      0
    );

    const activeOrders = orders.filter(
      (order) => order.status !== "Cancelled"
    );

    const orderStatuses = [
      "Pending",
      "Approved",
      "Processing",
      "Completed",
      "Cancelled",
    ].map((status) => ({
      status,
      count: orders.filter((order) => order.status === status).length,
    }));

    const overdueAccounts = receivables.filter(
      (record) =>
        numberValue(record.balance) > 0 &&
        record.status?.toLowerCase() === "overdue"
    ).length;

    return {
      totalSales,
      totalCollections,
      outstandingBalance,
      totalReturns,
      totalOrders: orders.length,
      activeOrders: activeOrders.length,
      orderStatuses,
      overdueAccounts,
    };
  }, [orders, invoices, collections, receivables, returns]);

  const monthlyAnalytics = useMemo(() => {
    const months = getLastSixMonths();

    return months.map((month) => {
      const monthlySales = invoices
        .filter(
          (invoice) =>
            getMonthKey(invoice.invoice_date) === month.key
        )
        .reduce(
          (sum, invoice) => sum + numberValue(invoice.total_due),
          0
        );

      const monthlyCollections = collections
        .filter(
          (collection) =>
            getMonthKey(collection.payment_date) === month.key
        )
        .reduce(
          (sum, collection) =>
            sum + numberValue(collection.amount_paid),
          0
        );

      return {
        ...month,
        sales: monthlySales,
        collections: monthlyCollections,
      };
    });
  }, [invoices, collections]);

  const topProducts = useMemo(() => {
    const productMap = new Map<
      number,
      {
        IM_ID: number;
        name: string;
        sku: string;
        quantity: number;
        sales: number;
      }
    >();

    const detailMap = new Map<
      number,
      { IM_ID: number; quantity: number; lineTotal: number }
    >();

    const itemMap = new Map(
      items.map((item) => [item.IM_ID, item])
    );

    const returnedQuantities = new Map<number, number>();
    const returnedAmounts = new Map<number, number>();

    returns.forEach((record) => {
      const detailId = record.Order_Detail_ID;

      returnedQuantities.set(
        detailId,
        (returnedQuantities.get(detailId) ?? 0) +
          numberValue(record.quantity)
      );

      returnedAmounts.set(
        detailId,
        (returnedAmounts.get(detailId) ?? 0) +
          numberValue(record.return_amount)
      );
    });

    orders
      .filter((order) => order.status !== "Cancelled")
      .forEach((order) => {
        order.details?.forEach((detail) => {
          detailMap.set(detail.order_detail_id, {
            IM_ID: detail.IM_ID,
            quantity: numberValue(detail.quantity),
            lineTotal: numberValue(detail.line_total),
          });
        });
      });

    detailMap.forEach((detail, detailId) => {
      const item = itemMap.get(detail.IM_ID);
      const returnedQty = returnedQuantities.get(detailId) ?? 0;
      const returnedAmount = returnedAmounts.get(detailId) ?? 0;

      const netQuantity = Math.max(0, detail.quantity - returnedQty);
      const netSales = Math.max(0, detail.lineTotal - returnedAmount);

      const existing = productMap.get(detail.IM_ID);

      if (existing) {
        existing.quantity += netQuantity;
        existing.sales += netSales;
      } else {
        productMap.set(detail.IM_ID, {
          IM_ID: detail.IM_ID,
          name: item?.Item_Name ?? `Item #${detail.IM_ID}`,
          sku: item?.SKU ?? "â€”",
          quantity: netQuantity,
          sales: netSales,
        });
      }
    });

    return Array.from(productMap.values())
      .filter((product) => product.quantity > 0)
      .sort((a, b) => b.quantity - a.quantity)
      .slice(0, 5);
  }, [orders, items, returns]);

  const recentInvoices = useMemo(() => {
    return [...invoices]
      .sort(
        (a, b) =>
          new Date(b.invoice_date).getTime() -
          new Date(a.invoice_date).getTime()
      )
      .slice(0, 5);
  }, [invoices]);

  // Compact line graph dimensions
  const chartWidth = 560;
  const chartHeight = 160;

  const chartPadding = {
    top: 10,
    right: 16,
    bottom: 28,
    left: 64,
  };

  const maxMonthlyValue = Math.max(
    1,
    ...monthlyAnalytics.flatMap((month) => [
      month.sales,
      month.collections,
    ])
  );

  const chartX = (index: number) =>
    chartPadding.left +
    (index *
      (chartWidth - chartPadding.left - chartPadding.right)) /
      Math.max(monthlyAnalytics.length - 1, 1);

  const chartY = (value: number) =>
    chartHeight -
    chartPadding.bottom -
    (value / maxMonthlyValue) *
      (chartHeight - chartPadding.top - chartPadding.bottom);

  const salesLine = monthlyAnalytics
    .map((month, index) => `${chartX(index)},${chartY(month.sales)}`)
    .join(" ");

  const collectionsLine = monthlyAnalytics
    .map(
      (month, index) =>
        `${chartX(index)},${chartY(month.collections)}`
    )
    .join(" ");

  const chartAxisLabels = [0, 0.25, 0.5, 0.75, 1];

  return (
    <div className="min-h-full space-y-6 bg-green-50 p-4 text-slate-900 sm:p-6">
      {/* Dashboard Header */}
      <div className="flex flex-col gap-3 sm:flex-row sm:items-center sm:justify-between">
        <div>
          <h1 className="mt-1 text-2xl font-bold text-slate-900">
            {PAGE_TITLE}
          </h1>
          <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-slate-600">
            Overview of sales performance and financial activities.
          </p>
        </div>
      </div>

      {error && (
        <div className="rounded-lg border border-red-200 bg-red-50 p-4 text-sm text-red-700">
          <p className="font-semibold">Unable to load dashboard data</p>
          <p className="mt-1">{error}</p>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* KPI Cards */}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2 xl:grid-cols-4">
        <div className={cardClass}>
          <p className="text-sm font-medium text-slate-600">
            Total Sales
          </p>
          <p className="mt-2 text-2xl font-bold text-slate-900">
            {loading ? "â€”" : money(analytics.totalSales)}
          </p>
          <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-slate-600">
            Based on sales invoices
          </p>
        </div>

        <div className={cardClass}>
          <p className="text-sm font-medium text-slate-600">
            Total Orders
          </p>
          <p className="mt-2 text-2xl font-bold text-slate-900">
            {loading ? "â€”" : analytics.totalOrders.toLocaleString()}
          </p>
          <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-slate-600">
            {analytics.activeOrders} non-cancelled orders
          </p>
        </div>

        <div className={cardClass}>
          <p className="text-sm font-medium text-slate-600">
            Outstanding Balance
          </p>
          <p className="mt-2 text-2xl font-bold text-amber-600">
            {loading ? "â€”" : money(analytics.outstandingBalance)}
          </p>
          <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-slate-600">
            Remaining accounts receivable
          </p>
        </div>

        <div className={cardClass}>
          <p className="text-sm font-medium text-slate-600">
            Total Collections
          </p>
          <p className="mt-2 text-2xl font-bold text-emerald-600">
            {loading ? "â€”" : money(analytics.totalCollections)}
          </p>
          <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-slate-600">
            Recorded customer payments
          </p>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Sales Performance and Top Selling Products */}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 items-start gap-4 xl:grid-cols-2">
        {/* Sales Performance Graph */}
        <section className="w-full rounded-xl border border-[#D1D5DB] bg-[#E5E7EB] p-4 shadow-sm">
          <div className="mb-3">
            <h2 className="text-lg font-bold text-slate-900">
              Sales Performance
            </h2>
            <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-slate-600">
              Monthly sales and collections for the last six months.
            </p>
          </div>

          {loading ? (
            <p className="py-8 text-center text-sm text-slate-600">
              Loading sales performance...
            </p>
          ) : monthlyAnalytics.every(
              (month) => month.sales === 0 && month.collections === 0
            ) ? (
            <p className="py-8 text-center text-sm text-slate-600">
              No sales or collection records available for this period.
            </p>
          ) : (
            <>
              {/* Graph Legend */}
              <div className="mb-2 flex flex-wrap gap-4 text-xs">
                <span className="flex items-center gap-2 text-slate-700">
                  <span className="h-2.5 w-2.5 rounded-full bg-blue-600" />
                  Sales
                </span>

                <span className="flex items-center gap-2 text-slate-700">
                  <span className="h-2.5 w-2.5 rounded-full bg-emerald-500" />
                  Collections
                </span>
              </div>

              {/* Line Graph */}
              <div className="mx-auto w-full max-w-[560px]">
                <svg
                  viewBox={`0 0 ${chartWidth} ${chartHeight}`}
                  className="mx-auto h-auto w-full"
                  role="img"
                  aria-label="Line graph showing monthly sales and collections for the last six months"
                >
                  {/* Horizontal Grid Lines and Labels */}
                  {chartAxisLabels.map((ratio) => {
                    const value = maxMonthlyValue * ratio;
                    const y = chartY(value);

                    return (
                      <g key={ratio}>
                        <line
                          x1={chartPadding.left}
                          y1={y}
                          x2={chartWidth - chartPadding.right}
                          y2={y}
                          stroke="#cbd5e1"
                          strokeDasharray="4 4"
                        />

                        <text
                          x={chartPadding.left - 10}
                          y={y + 4}
                          textAnchor="end"
                          fontSize="11"
                          fill="#475569"
                        >
                          {value >= 1000
                            ? `â‚±${(value / 1000).toFixed(0)}k`
                            : `â‚±${value.toFixed(0)}`}
                        </text>
                      </g>
                    );
                  })}

                  {/* Sales Line */}
                  <polyline
                    points={salesLine}
                    fill="none"
                    stroke="#2563eb"
                    strokeWidth="2.5"
                    strokeLinecap="round"
                    strokeLinejoin="round"
                  />

                  {/* Collections Line */}
                  <polyline
                    points={collectionsLine}
                    fill="none"
                    stroke="#10b981"
                    strokeWidth="2.5"
                    strokeLinecap="round"
                    strokeLinejoin="round"
                  />

                  {/* Data Points and Month Labels */}
                  {monthlyAnalytics.map((month, index) => (
                    <g key={month.key}>
                      <circle
                        cx={chartX(index)}
                        cy={chartY(month.sales)}
                        r="4"
                        fill="#2563eb"
                        stroke="white"
                        strokeWidth="1.5"
                      >
                        <title>
                          {month.label} Sales: {money(month.sales)}
                        </title>
                      </circle>

                      <circle
                        cx={chartX(index)}
                        cy={chartY(month.collections)}
                        r="4"
                        fill="#10b981"
                        stroke="white"
                        strokeWidth="1.5"
                      >
                        <title>
                          {month.label} Collections: {money(month.collections)}
                        </title>
                      </circle>

                      <text
                        x={chartX(index)}
                        y={chartHeight - 12}
                        textAnchor="middle"
                        fontSize="11"
                        fill="#475569"
                      >
                        {month.label}
                      </text>
                    </g>
                  ))}
                </svg>
              </div>

              {/* Monthly Totals */}
              <div className="mt-3 grid grid-cols-1 gap-3 border-t border-[#D1D5DB] pt-3 sm:grid-cols-2">
                <div>
                  <p className="text-xs text-slate-600">
                    Sales in displayed period
                  </p>
                  <p className="mt-1 font-semibold text-blue-700">
                    {money(
                      monthlyAnalytics.reduce(
                        (sum, month) => sum + month.sales,
                        0
                      )
                    )}
                  </p>
                </div>

                <div>
                  <p className="text-xs text-slate-600">
                    Collections in displayed period
                  </p>
                  <p className="mt-1 font-semibold text-emerald-700">
                    {money(
                      monthlyAnalytics.reduce(
                        (sum, month) => sum + month.collections,
                        0
                      )
                    )}
                  </p>
                </div>
              </div>
            </>
          )}
        </section>

        {/* Top Selling Products */}
        <section className={cardClass}>
          <div className="mb-5">
            <h2 className="text-lg font-bold text-slate-900">
              Top Selling Products
            </h2>
            <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-slate-600">
              Products ranked by net quantity sold, after recorded returns.
            </p>
          </div>

          {loading ? (
            <p className="py-8 text-center text-sm text-slate-600">
              Loading product analytics...
            </p>
          ) : topProducts.length === 0 ? (
            <p className="py-8 text-center text-sm text-slate-600">
              No product sales records available yet.
            </p>
          ) : (
            <div className="overflow-x-auto">
              <table className="w-full min-w-[650px] text-left text-sm">
                <thead>
                  <tr className="border-b border-[#D1D5DB] bg-[#D1D5DB] text-xs uppercase tracking-wide text-slate-700">
                    <th className="px-4 py-3 text-left">Rank</th>
                    <th className="px-4 py-3 text-center">Product</th>
                    <th className="px-4 py-3 text-center">SKU</th>
                    <th className="px-4 py-3 text-center">
                      Quantity Sold
                    </th>
                    <th className="px-4 py-3 text-center">
                      Net Sales
                    </th>
                  </tr>
                </thead>

                <tbody>
                  {topProducts.map((product, index) => (
                    <tr
                      key={product.IM_ID}
                      className="border-b border-[#D1D5DB] last:border-0 hover:bg-[#DDE1E5]"
                    >
                      <td className="px-4 py-4 font-semibold text-slate-600">
                        #{index + 1}
                      </td>

                      <td className="px-4 py-4 text-center">
                        <p className="text-center font-semibold text-slate-800">
                          {product.name}
                        </p>
                        <p className="text-center text-xs text-slate-600">
                          Item ID: {product.IM_ID}
                        </p>
                      </td>

                      <td className="px-4 py-4 text-center text-slate-700">
                        {product.sku}
                      </td>

                      <td className="px-4 py-4 text-center font-semibold text-slate-800">
                        {product.quantity.toLocaleString()}
                      </td>

                      <td className="px-4 py-4 text-center font-semibold text-slate-800">
                        {money(product.sales)}
                      </td>
                    </tr>
                  ))}
                </tbody>
              </table>
            </div>
          )}
        </section>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: C:\Users\Keneth Vivas\ComputerERP\sales\frontend\components\billing-collections\AccountsReceivablePanel.tsx

"use client";

import { useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useState } from "react";
import {
  accountsReceivableAPI,
  invoiceAPI,
  salesOrderAPI,
  customerAPI,
} from "../../lib/api";

import type { AccountsReceivable } from "../../types/accounts-receivable";
import type { SalesInvoice } from "../../types/invoice";
import type { SalesOrder } from "../../types/order";
import type { Customer } from "../../types/customer";

type ARRow = {
  AR_ID: number;
  invoice_id: number;
  customer_name: string;
  due_date: string | null;
  amount_due: number;
  amount_paid: number;
  balance: number;
  status: string;
};

const PAGE_SIZE = 10;

const money = (value: number) =>
  new Intl.NumberFormat("en-PH", {
    style: "currency",
    currency: "PHP",
  }).format(value);

const amount = (value: string | number) => Number(value) || 0;

const getLocalDate = () => {
  const date = new Date();
  const year = date.getFullYear();
  const month = String(date.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, "0");
  const day = String(date.getDate()).padStart(2, "0");

  return `${year}-${month}-${day}`;
};

const formatDate = (value: string | null) => {
  if (!value) return "â€”";

  const dateOnly = value.slice(0, 10);
  const date = new Date(`${dateOnly}T00:00:00`);

  if (Number.isNaN(date.getTime())) return "â€”";

  return date.toLocaleDateString("en-PH", {
    day: "2-digit",
    month: "long",
    year: "numeric",
  });
};

const isOverdue = (record: ARRow, today: string) => {
  return (
    record.balance > 0 &&
    Boolean(record.due_date) &&
    record.due_date!.slice(0, 10) < today
  );
};

export default function AccountsReceivablePanel() {
  const [records, setRecords] = useState<ARRow[]>([]);
  const [search, setSearch] = useState("");
  const [page, setPage] = useState(1);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [error, setError] = useState("");
  const [today, setToday] = useState("");

  useEffect(() => {
    setToday(getLocalDate());
  }, []);

  const loadData = useCallback(async () => {
    try {
      setLoading(true);
      setError("");

      const [arData, invoiceData, orderData, customerData] =
        await Promise.all([
          accountsReceivableAPI.getAll(),
          invoiceAPI.getAll(),
          salesOrderAPI.getAll(),
          customerAPI.getAll(),
        ]);

      const invoices = new Map<number, SalesInvoice>(
        invoiceData.map((invoice: SalesInvoice) => [
          invoice.invoice_id,
          invoice,
        ])
      );

      const orders = new Map<number, SalesOrder>(
        orderData.map((order: SalesOrder) => [
          order.order_id,
          order,
        ])
      );

      const customers = new Map<number, Customer>(
        customerData.map((customer: Customer) => [
          customer.customer_id,
          customer,
        ])
      );

      const rows: ARRow[] = arData.map(
        (record: AccountsReceivable) => {
          const invoice = invoices.get(record.invoice_id);
          const order = invoice
            ? orders.get(invoice.order_id)
            : undefined;
          const customer = order
            ? customers.get(order.customer_id)
            : undefined;

          return {
            AR_ID: record.AR_ID,
            invoice_id: record.invoice_id,
            customer_name: customer?.name ?? "Unknown Customer",
            due_date: invoice?.due_date ?? null,
            amount_due: amount(record.amount_due),
            amount_paid: amount(record.amount_paid),
            balance: amount(record.balance),
            status: record.status ?? "Unpaid",
          };
        }
      );

      setRecords(rows);
      setPage(1);
    } catch (err) {
      setError(
        err instanceof Error
          ? err.message
          : "Failed to load accounts receivable records."
      );
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    void loadData();
  }, [loadData]);

  const filteredRecords = useMemo(() => {
    const keyword = search.trim().toLowerCase();

    if (!keyword) return records;

    return records.filter((record) => {
      const overdue = isOverdue(record, today);

      return (
        String(record.AR_ID).includes(keyword) ||
        String(record.invoice_id).includes(keyword) ||
        record.customer_name.toLowerCase().includes(keyword) ||
        record.status.toLowerCase().includes(keyword) ||
        (overdue && "overdue".includes(keyword)) ||
        (record.due_date ?? "").toLowerCase().includes(keyword)
      );
    });
  }, [records, search, today]);

  const totals = useMemo(
    () => ({
      amountDue: records.reduce(
        (sum, record) => sum + record.amount_due,
        0
      ),
      amountPaid: records.reduce(
        (sum, record) => sum + record.amount_paid,
        0
      ),
      balance: records.reduce(
        (sum, record) => sum + record.balance,
        0
      ),
      unpaid: records.filter(
        (record) => record.balance > 0
      ).length,
      overdue: records.filter(
        (record) => isOverdue(record, today)
      ).length,
    }),
    [records, today]
  );

  const totalPages = Math.max(
    1,
    Math.ceil(filteredRecords.length / PAGE_SIZE)
  );

  const currentPage = Math.min(page, totalPages);

  const startIndex = (currentPage - 1) * PAGE_SIZE;

  const paginatedRecords = useMemo(() => {
    return filteredRecords.slice(
      startIndex,
      startIndex + PAGE_SIZE
    );
  }, [filteredRecords, startIndex]);

  const statusStyle = (status: string, overdue: boolean) => {
    if (overdue) {
      return "bg-red-100 text-red-700";
    }

    switch (status) {
      case "Paid":
        return "bg-green-100 text-green-700";
      case "Partially Paid":
        return "bg-amber-100 text-amber-800";
      default:
        return "bg-slate-100 text-slate-700";
    }
  };

  const getDisplayStatus = (record: ARRow) => {
    if (isOverdue(record, today)) return "Overdue";
    return record.status;
  };

  const startRecord =
    filteredRecords.length === 0 ? 0 : startIndex + 1;

  const endRecord = Math.min(
    startIndex + PAGE_SIZE,
    filteredRecords.length
  );

  return (
    <div className="min-w-0 space-y-5 bg-green-50 text-slate-800">
      {error && (
        <div
          role="alert"
          className="rounded-lg border border-red-200 bg-red-50 p-3 text-sm text-red-700"
        >
          {error}
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Summary Cards */}
      <section className="grid gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2 xl:grid-cols-5">
        <SummaryCard
          title="Total Receivables"
          value={money(totals.amountDue)}
          color="text-blue-700"
        />

        <SummaryCard
          title="Amount Collected"
          value={money(totals.amountPaid)}
          color="text-green-700"
        />

        <SummaryCard
          title="Outstanding Balance"
          value={money(totals.balance)}
          color="text-red-700"
        />

        <SummaryCard
          title="Unpaid Accounts"
          value={String(totals.unpaid)}
          color="text-amber-700"
        />

        <SummaryCard
          title="Overdue Accounts"
          value={String(totals.overdue)}
          color="text-red-700"
        />
      </section>

      {/* Receivable Records */}
      <section className="overflow-hidden rounded-xl border border-[#D1D5DB] bg-[#E5E7EB] shadow-sm">
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-3 border-b border-[#D1D5DB] p-5 sm:flex-row sm:items-center sm:justify-between">
          <div>
            <h3 className="font-semibold text-slate-900">
              Receivable Records
            </h3>

            <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-slate-600">
              View invoice balances and payment status.
            </p>
          </div>

          <input
            type="text"
            placeholder="Search AR, invoice, customer, or status..."
            value={search}
            onChange={(event) => {
              setSearch(event.target.value);
              setPage(1);
            }}
            className="w-full rounded-lg border border-slate-300 bg-white px-4 py-2.5 text-sm text-slate-800 outline-none placeholder:text-slate-400 focus:border-blue-500 focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-100 sm:max-w-sm"
          />
        </div>

        {loading ? (
          <div className="py-12 text-center text-sm text-slate-600">
            Loading accounts receivable...
          </div>
        ) : (
          <>
            <div className="overflow-x-auto">
              <table className="w-full min-w-[1050px] text-left text-sm">
                <thead className="bg-[#D1D5DB] text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wide text-slate-700">
                  <tr>
                    <th className="px-4 py-3 text-center">AR No.</th>
                    <th className="px-4 py-3 text-center">Invoice No.</th>
                    <th className="px-4 py-3 text-center">Customer</th>
                    <th className="px-4 py-3 text-center">Due Date</th>
                    <th className="px-4 py-3 text-center">Amount Due</th>
                    <th className="px-4 py-3 text-center">Amount Paid</th>
                    <th className="px-4 py-3 text-center">Balance</th>
                    <th className="px-4 py-3 text-center">Status</th>
                  </tr>
                </thead>

                <tbody className="divide-y divide-[#D1D5DB]">
                  {paginatedRecords.map((record) => {
                    const overdue = isOverdue(record, today);

                    return (
                      <tr
                        key={record.AR_ID}
                        className="text-center transition-colors hover:bg-[#DDE1E5]"
                      >
                        <td className="px-4 py-3 font-semibold text-slate-700">
                          AR-{record.AR_ID}
                        </td>

                        <td className="px-4 py-3 font-medium text-blue-700">
                          INV-{record.invoice_id}
                        </td>

                        <td className="px-4 py-3 font-medium text-slate-800">
                          {record.customer_name}
                        </td>

                        <td className="whitespace-nowrap px-4 py-3 text-slate-600">
                          {formatDate(record.due_date)}
                        </td>

                        <td className="px-4 py-3 tabular-nums text-slate-700">
                          {money(record.amount_due)}
                        </td>

                        <td className="px-4 py-3 tabular-nums text-green-700">
                          {money(record.amount_paid)}
                        </td>

                        <td className="px-4 py-3 font-semibold tabular-nums text-slate-900">
                          {money(record.balance)}
                        </td>

                        <td className="px-4 py-3">
                          <span
                            className={`inline-flex whitespace-nowrap rounded-full px-3 py-1 text-xs font-semibold ${statusStyle(
                              record.status,
                              overdue
                            )}`}
                          >
                            {getDisplayStatus(record)}
                          </span>
                        </td>
                      </tr>
                    );
                  })}

                  {paginatedRecords.length === 0 && (
                    <tr>
                      <td
                        colSpan={8}
                        className="px-4 py-12 text-center text-slate-600"
                      >
                        {search
                          ? "No matching receivable records found."
                          : "No accounts receivable records yet."}
                      </td>
                    </tr>
                  )}
                </tbody>
              </table>
            </div>

            {/* Pagination */}
            <div className="flex flex-col gap-3 border-t border-[#D1D5DB] bg-gray px-5 py-4 sm:flex-row sm:items-center sm:justify-between">
              <p className="text-sm text-slate-500">
                Showing{" "}
                <span className="font-semibold text-slate-700">
                  {startRecord}
                </span>
                {" "}to{" "}
                <span className="font-semibold text-slate-700">
                  {endRecord}
                </span>
                {" "}of{" "}
                <span className="font-semibold text-slate-700">
                  {filteredRecords.length}
                </span>
                {" "}records
              </p>

              <div className="flex items-center justify-end gap-2">
                <button
                  type="button"
                  disabled={currentPage === 1}
                  onClick={() => setPage(currentPage - 1)}
                  className="rounded-lg border border-slate-300 px-3 py-2 text-sm font-medium text-slate-700 transition hover:bg-slate-50 disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-40"
                >
                  Previous
                </button>

                <span className="px-2 text-sm font-medium text-slate-600">
                  Page {currentPage} of {totalPages}
                </span>

                <button
                  type="button"
                  disabled={currentPage === totalPages}
                  onClick={() => setPage(currentPage + 1)}
                  className="rounded-lg border border-slate-300 px-3 py-2 text-sm font-medium text-slate-700 transition hover:bg-slate-50 disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-40"
                >
                  Next
                </button>
              </div>
            </div>
          </>
        )}
      </section>
    </div>
  );
}

function SummaryCard({
  title,
  value,
  color,
}: {
  title: string;
  value: string;
  color: string;
}) {
  return (
    <div className="rounded-xl border border-[#D1D5DB] bg-[#E5E7EB] p-5 shadow-sm">
      <p className="text-sm font-medium text-slate-600">
        {title}
      </p>

      <p className={`mt-2 text-xl font-bold ${color}`}>
        {value}
      </p>
    </div>
  );
}
