"use client";

import { useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useState } from "react";
import {
  paymentCollectionAPI,
  accountsReceivableAPI,
  invoiceAPI,
  salesOrderAPI,
  customerAPI,
} from "../../lib/api";

import type { Collection, CollectionCreate } from "../../types/payment";
import type { AccountsReceivable } from "../../types/accounts-receivable";
import type { SalesInvoice } from "../../types/invoice";
import type { SalesOrder } from "../../types/order";
import type { Customer } from "../../types/customer";

type CollectionRow = Collection & {
  customer_name: string;
  invoice_id: number | null;
};

const PAGE_SIZE = 10;

const money = (value: string | number) =>
  new Intl.NumberFormat("en-PH", {
    style: "currency",
    currency: "PHP",
  }).format(Number(value) || 0);

const getToday = () => {
  const date = new Date();
  const year = date.getFullYear();
  const month = String(date.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, "0");
  const day = String(date.getDate()).padStart(2, "0");

  return `${year}-${month}-${day}`;
};

const formatDate = (value: string | null | undefined) => {
  if (!value) return "â€”";

  const date = new Date(`${value.slice(0, 10)}T00:00:00`);

  if (Number.isNaN(date.getTime())) return "â€”";

  return date.toLocaleDateString("en-PH", {
    day: "2-digit",
    month: "long",
    year: "numeric",
  });
};

export default function CollectionsPanel() {
  const [records, setRecords] = useState<CollectionRow[]>([]);
  const [receivables, setReceivables] = useState<AccountsReceivable[]>([]);
  const [search, setSearch] = useState("");
  const [page, setPage] = useState(1);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState("");
  const [success, setSuccess] = useState("");

  const [form, setForm] = useState<CollectionCreate>({
    AR_ID: 0,
    payment_date: getToday(),
    payment_method: "Cash",
    amount_paid: 0,
  });

  const loadData = useCallback(async () => {
    try {
      setLoading(true);
      setError("");

      const [
        paymentData,
        arData,
        invoiceData,
        orderData,
        customerData,
      ] = await Promise.all([
        paymentCollectionAPI.getAll(),
        accountsReceivableAPI.getAll(),
        invoiceAPI.getAll(),
        salesOrderAPI.getAll(),
        customerAPI.getAll(),
      ]);

      const invoiceMap = new Map<number, SalesInvoice>(
        invoiceData.map((item: SalesInvoice) => [
          item.invoice_id,
          item,
        ])
      );

      const orderMap = new Map<number, SalesOrder>(
        orderData.map((item: SalesOrder) => [
          item.order_id,
          item,
        ])
      );

      const customerMap = new Map<number, Customer>(
        customerData.map((item: Customer) => [
          item.customer_id,
          item,
        ])
      );

      const arMap = new Map<number, AccountsReceivable>(
        arData.map((item: AccountsReceivable) => [
          item.AR_ID,
          item,
        ])
      );

      const rows: CollectionRow[] = paymentData.map(
        (payment: Collection) => {
          const ar = arMap.get(payment.AR_ID);
          const invoice = ar
            ? invoiceMap.get(ar.invoice_id)
            : undefined;
          const order = invoice
            ? orderMap.get(invoice.order_id)
            : undefined;
          const customer = order
            ? customerMap.get(order.customer_id)
            : undefined;

          return {
            ...payment,
            invoice_id: ar?.invoice_id ?? null,
            customer_name: customer?.name ?? "Unknown Customer",
          };
        }
      );

      setRecords(rows);
      setReceivables(arData);
    } catch (err) {
      setError(
        err instanceof Error
          ? err.message
          : "Failed to load payment collections."
      );
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    loadData();
  }, [loadData]);

  const filteredRecords = useMemo(() => {
    const keyword = search.trim().toLowerCase();

    if (!keyword) return records;

    return records.filter(
      (record) =>
        String(record.payment_id).includes(keyword) ||
        String(record.AR_ID).includes(keyword) ||
        String(record.invoice_id ?? "").includes(keyword) ||
        record.customer_name.toLowerCase().includes(keyword) ||
        (record.payment_method ?? "").toLowerCase().includes(keyword) ||
        String(record.payment_date ?? "").toLowerCase().includes(keyword)
    );
  }, [records, search]);

  const totals = useMemo(
    () => ({
      collected: records.reduce(
        (sum, record) => sum + Number(record.amount_paid),
        0
      ),
      count: records.length,
      cash: records.filter(
        (record) => record.payment_method === "Cash"
      ).length,
    }),
    [records]
  );

  const unpaidReceivables = useMemo(
    () =>
      receivables.filter(
        (record) => Number(record.balance) > 0
      ),
    [receivables]
  );

  const selectedAR = receivables.find(
    (record) => record.AR_ID === form.AR_ID
  );

  const handleSubmit = async (
    event: React.FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>
  ) => {
    event.preventDefault();
    setError("");
    setSuccess("");

    if (!form.AR_ID) {
      setError("Please select an unpaid or partially paid account.");
      return;
    }

    if (!Number.isFinite(form.amount_paid) || form.amount_paid <= 0) {
      setError("Payment amount must be greater than zero.");
      return;
    }

    const currentAR = receivables.find(
      (record) => record.AR_ID === form.AR_ID
    );

    if (!currentAR) {
      setError("Selected accounts receivable record was not found.");
      return;
    }

    const remainingBalance = Number(currentAR.balance);

    if (form.amount_paid > remainingBalance) {
      setError(
        `Payment cannot exceed the remaining balance of ${money(remainingBalance)}.`
      );
      return;
    }

    try {
      setSaving(true);

      await paymentCollectionAPI.create(form);

      setSuccess("Payment successfully recorded.");

      setForm({
        AR_ID: 0,
        payment_date: getToday(),
        payment_method: "Cash",
        amount_paid: 0,
      });

      setPage(1);
      await loadData();
    } catch (err) {
      setError(
        err instanceof Error
          ? err.message
          : "Failed to record payment."
      );
    } finally {
      setSaving(false);
    }
  };

  const totalPages = Math.max(
    1,
    Math.ceil(filteredRecords.length / PAGE_SIZE)
  );

  const currentPage = Math.min(page, totalPages);

  const paginatedRecords = useMemo(() => {
    const startIndex = (currentPage - 1) * PAGE_SIZE;

    return filteredRecords.slice(
      startIndex,
      startIndex + PAGE_SIZE
    );
  }, [filteredRecords, currentPage]);

  const startRecord =
    filteredRecords.length === 0
      ? 0
      : (currentPage - 1) * PAGE_SIZE + 1;

  const endRecord = Math.min(
    currentPage * PAGE_SIZE,
    filteredRecords.length
  );

  return (
    <div className="space-y-5 text-slate-800">

      <section className="grid gap-4 sm:grid-cols-3">
        <SummaryCard
          title="Total Collected"
          value={money(totals.collected)}
          color="text-green-700"
        />
        <SummaryCard
          title="Payment Transactions"
          value={String(totals.count)}
          color="text-blue-700"
        />
        <SummaryCard
          title="Cash Transactions"
          value={String(totals.cash)}
          color="text-amber-700"
        />
      </section>

      <section className="rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-5 shadow-sm md:p-6">
        <div className="mb-5">
          <h3 className="font-semibold text-slate-900">
            Record New Payment
          </h3>
          <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-slate-500">
            Select an outstanding account and enter the payment details.
          </p>
        </div>

        {error && (
          <div className="mb-4 rounded-lg border border-red-200 bg-red-50 p-3 text-sm text-red-700">
            {error}
          </div>
        )}

        {success && (
          <div className="mb-4 rounded-lg border border-green-200 bg-green-50 p-3 text-sm text-green-700">
            {success}
          </div>
        )}

        <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="grid gap-4 md:grid-cols-2">
          <div>
            <label className="mb-1.5 block text-sm font-medium text-slate-700">
              Accounts Receivable
            </label>
            <select
              value={form.AR_ID || ""}
              onChange={(event) => {
                setForm({
                  ...form,
                  AR_ID: Number(event.target.value),
                });
                setError("");
                setSuccess("");
              }}
              required
              disabled={loading || unpaidReceivables.length === 0}
              className="w-full rounded-lg border border-slate-300 bg-white px-3 py-2.5 text-sm text-slate-800 outline-none focus:border-blue-500 focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-100 disabled:bg-slate-100"
            >
              <option value="">
                {loading
                  ? "Loading accounts..."
                  : unpaidReceivables.length === 0
                    ? "No outstanding accounts"
                    : "Select an account"}
              </option>

              {unpaidReceivables.map((record) => (
                <option key={record.AR_ID} value={record.AR_ID}>
                  AR-{record.AR_ID} Â· Invoice #{record.invoice_id} Â· Balance:{" "}
                  {money(record.balance)}
                </option>
              ))}
            </select>
          </div>

          <div>
            <label className="mb-1.5 block text-sm font-medium text-slate-700">
              Payment Date
            </label>
            <input
              type="date"
              value={form.payment_date}
              max={getToday()}
              onChange={(event) => {
                setForm({
                  ...form,
                  payment_date: event.target.value,
                });
                setError("");
                setSuccess("");
              }}
              required
              className="w-full rounded-lg border border-slate-300 bg-white px-3 py-2.5 text-sm text-slate-800 outline-none focus:border-blue-500 focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-100"
            />
          </div>

          <div>
            <label className="mb-1.5 block text-sm font-medium text-slate-700">
              Payment Method
            </label>
            <select
              value={form.payment_method ?? "Cash"}
              onChange={(event) =>
                setForm({
                  ...form,
                  payment_method: event.target.value,
                })
              }
              className="w-full rounded-lg border border-slate-300 bg-white px-3 py-2.5 text-sm text-slate-800 outline-none focus:border-blue-500 focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-100"
            >
              <option value="Cash">Cash</option>
              <option value="E-Wallet">E-Wallet</option>
              <option value="Other">Other</option>
            </select>
          </div>

          <div>
            <label className="mb-1.5 block text-sm font-medium text-slate-700">
              Amount Received (â‚±)
            </label>
            <input
              type="number"
              min="0.01"
              step="0.01"
              max={selectedAR ? Number(selectedAR.balance) : undefined}
              value={form.amount_paid || ""}
              onChange={(event) => {
                setForm({
                  ...form,
                  amount_paid: Number(event.target.value),
                });
                setError("");
                setSuccess("");
              }}
              required
              placeholder="Enter payment amount"
              className="w-full rounded-lg border border-slate-300 bg-white px-3 py-2.5 text-sm text-slate-800 outline-none placeholder:text-slate-400 focus:border-blue-500 focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-100"
            />

            {selectedAR && (
              <p className="mt-1.5 text-xs text-slate-500">
                Remaining balance:{" "}
                <span className="font-semibold text-slate-700">
                  {money(selectedAR.balance)}
                </span>
              </p>
            )}
          </div>

          <div className="md:col-span-2">
            <button
              type="submit"
              disabled={
                saving ||
                loading ||
                unpaidReceivables.length === 0
              }
              className="rounded-lg bg-blue-600 px-5 py-2.5 text-sm font-semibold text-white transition hover:bg-blue-700 disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-50"
            >
              {saving ? "Recording Payment..." : "Record Payment"}
            </button>
          </div>
        </form>
      </section>

      <section className="overflow-hidden rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-white shadow-sm">
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-3 border-b border-slate-200 p-5 sm:flex-row sm:items-center sm:justify-between">
          <div>
            <h3 className="font-semibold text-slate-900">
              Payment History
            </h3>
            <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-slate-500">
              View recorded customer payments and transaction details.
            </p>
          </div>

          <input
            type="text"
            placeholder="Search payment, invoice, or customer..."
            value={search}
            onChange={(event) => {
              setSearch(event.target.value);
              setPage(1);
            }}
            className="w-full rounded-lg border border-slate-300 bg-white px-4 py-2.5 text-sm text-slate-800 outline-none placeholder:text-slate-400 focus:border-blue-500 focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-100 sm:max-w-sm"
          />
        </div>

        {loading ? (
          <div className="py-12 text-center text-sm text-slate-500">
            Loading payment history...
          </div>
        ) : (
          <>
            <div className="overflow-x-auto">
              <table className="w-full min-w-[950px] text-left text-sm">
                <thead className="bg-slate-50 text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wide text-slate-600">
                  <tr>
                    <th className="px-4 py-3 text-center">Payment No.</th>
                    <th className="px-4 py-3 text-center">AR No.</th>
                    <th className="px-4 py-3 text-center">Invoice No.</th>
                    <th className="px-4 py-3">Customer</th>
                    <th className="px-4 py-3">Payment Date</th>
                    <th className="px-4 py-3 text-right">Amount</th>
                    <th className="px-4 py-3">Method</th>
                  </tr>
                </thead>

                <tbody className="divide-y divide-slate-100">
                  {paginatedRecords.map((record) => (
                    <tr
                      key={record.payment_id}
                      className="transition-colors hover:bg-slate-50"
                    >
                      <td className="px-4 py-3 text-center font-semibold text-slate-700">
                        PAY-{record.payment_id}
                      </td>

                      <td className="px-4 py-3 text-center text-slate-700">
                        AR-{record.AR_ID}
                      </td>

                      <td className="px-4 py-3 text-center font-medium text-blue-700">
                        {record.invoice_id
                          ? `INV-${record.invoice_id}`
                          : "â€”"}
                      </td>

                      <td className="px-4 py-3 font-medium text-slate-800">
                        {record.customer_name}
                      </td>

                      <td className="px-4 py-3 whitespace-nowrap text-slate-600">
                        {formatDate(record.payment_date)}
                      </td>

                      <td className="px-4 py-3 text-right font-semibold tabular-nums text-green-700">
                        {money(record.amount_paid)}
                      </td>

                      <td className="px-4 py-3">
                        <span className="inline-flex rounded-full bg-slate-100 px-3 py-1 text-xs font-medium text-slate-700">
                          {record.payment_method ?? "â€”"}
                        </span>
                      </td>
                    </tr>
                  ))}

                  {paginatedRecords.length === 0 && (
                    <tr>
                      <td
                        colSpan={7}
                        className="px-4 py-12 text-center text-slate-500"
                      >
                        {search
                          ? "No matching payment records found."
                          : "No payment records yet."}
                      </td>
                    </tr>
                  )}
                </tbody>
              </table>
            </div>

            <div className="flex flex-col gap-3 border-t border-slate-200 px-5 py-4 sm:flex-row sm:items-center sm:justify-between">
              <p className="text-sm text-slate-500">
                Showing{" "}
                <span className="font-semibold text-slate-700">
                  {startRecord}-{endRecord}
                </span>{" "}
                of{" "}
                <span className="font-semibold text-slate-700">
                  {filteredRecords.length}
                </span>{" "}
                payments
              </p>

              <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() =>
                    setPage((current) => Math.max(1, current - 1))
                  }
                  disabled={currentPage === 1}
                  className="rounded-lg border border-slate-300 px-3 py-2 text-sm font-medium text-slate-700 hover:bg-slate-50 disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-40"
                >
                  Previous
                </button>

                <span className="min-w-20 text-center text-sm font-medium text-slate-600">
                  Page {currentPage} of {totalPages}
                </span>

                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() =>
                    setPage((current) =>
                      Math.min(totalPages, current + 1)
                    )
                  }
                  disabled={currentPage === totalPages}
                  className="rounded-lg border border-slate-300 px-3 py-2 text-sm font-medium text-slate-700 hover:bg-slate-50 disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-40"
                >
                  Next
                </button>
              </div>
            </div>
          </>
        )}
      </section>
    </div>
  );
}

function SummaryCard({
  title,
  value,
  color,
}: {
  title: string;
  value: string;
  color: string;
}) {
  return (
    <div className="rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-5 shadow-sm">
      <p className="text-sm font-medium text-slate-500">{title}</p>
      <p className={`mt-2 text-xl font-bold ${color}`}>
        {value}
      </p>
    </div>
  );
}

"use client";

import { useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useState } from "react";
import {
  paymentCollectionAPI,
  accountsReceivableAPI,
  invoiceAPI,
  salesOrderAPI,
  customerAPI,
} from "../../lib/api";

import type { Collection, CollectionCreate } from "../../types/payment";
import type { AccountsReceivable } from "../../types/accounts-receivable";
import type { SalesInvoice } from "../../types/invoice";
import type { SalesOrder } from "../../types/order";
import type { Customer } from "../../types/customer";

type CollectionRow = Collection & {
  customer_name: string;
  invoice_id: number | null;
};

const PAGE_SIZE = 10;

const money = (value: string | number) =>
  new Intl.NumberFormat("en-PH", {
    style: "currency",
    currency: "PHP",
  }).format(Number(value) || 0);

const getToday = () => {
  const date = new Date();
  const year = date.getFullYear();
  const month = String(date.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, "0");
  const day = String(date.getDate()).padStart(2, "0");

  return `${year}-${month}-${day}`;
};

const formatDate = (value: string) => {
  if (!value) return "â€”";

  const dateOnly = value.slice(0, 10);
  const date = new Date(`${dateOnly}T00:00:00`);

  if (Number.isNaN(date.getTime())) return "â€”";

  return date.toLocaleDateString("en-PH", {
    day: "2-digit",
    month: "long",
    year: "numeric",
  });
};

const createEmptyForm = (): CollectionCreate => ({
  AR_ID: 0,
  payment_date: getToday(),
  payment_method: "Cash",
  transaction_number: null,
  amount_paid: 0,
});

export default function CollectionsPage() {
  const [records, setRecords] = useState<CollectionRow[]>([]);
  const [receivables, setReceivables] = useState<AccountsReceivable[]>([]);
  const [search, setSearch] = useState("");
  const [page, setPage] = useState(1);

  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);
  const [modalOpen, setModalOpen] = useState(false);

  const [error, setError] = useState("");
  const [success, setSuccess] = useState("");

  const [formError, setFormError] = useState("");

  const [form, setForm] = useState<CollectionCreate>({
    AR_ID: 0,
    payment_date: "",
    payment_method: "Cash",
    transaction_number: null,
    amount_paid: 0,
  });

  useEffect(() => {
    setForm((current) => ({
      ...current,
      payment_date: getToday(),
    }));
  }, []);

  const loadData = useCallback(async () => {
    try {
      setLoading(true);
      setError("");

      const [
        paymentData,
        arData,
        invoiceData,
        orderData,
        customerData,
      ] = await Promise.all([
        paymentCollectionAPI.getAll(),
        accountsReceivableAPI.getAll(),
        invoiceAPI.getAll(),
        salesOrderAPI.getAll(),
        customerAPI.getAll(),
      ]);

      const invoiceMap = new Map<number, SalesInvoice>(
        invoiceData.map((item: SalesInvoice) => [
          item.invoice_id,
          item,
        ])
      );

      const orderMap = new Map<number, SalesOrder>(
        orderData.map((item: SalesOrder) => [
          item.order_id,
          item,
        ])
      );

      const customerMap = new Map<number, Customer>(
        customerData.map((item: Customer) => [
          item.customer_id,
          item,
        ])
      );

      const arMap = new Map<number, AccountsReceivable>(
        arData.map((item: AccountsReceivable) => [
          item.AR_ID,
          item,
        ])
      );

      const rows: CollectionRow[] = paymentData.map(
        (payment: Collection) => {
          const ar = arMap.get(payment.AR_ID);

          const invoice = ar
            ? invoiceMap.get(ar.invoice_id)
            : undefined;

          const order = invoice
            ? orderMap.get(invoice.order_id)
            : undefined;

          const customer = order
            ? customerMap.get(order.customer_id)
            : undefined;

          return {
            ...payment,
            invoice_id: ar?.invoice_id ?? null,
            customer_name: customer?.name ?? "Unknown Customer",
          };
        }
      );

      setRecords(rows);
      setReceivables(arData);
    } catch (err) {
      setError(
        err instanceof Error
          ? err.message
          : "Failed to load payment collections."
      );
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    void loadData();
  }, [loadData]);

  const filteredRecords = useMemo(() => {
    const keyword = search.trim().toLowerCase();

    return records.filter(
      (record) =>
        String(record.payment_id).includes(keyword) ||
        String(record.invoice_id ?? "").includes(keyword) ||
        String(record.AR_ID).includes(keyword) ||
        record.customer_name.toLowerCase().includes(keyword) ||
        (record.payment_method ?? "").toLowerCase().includes(keyword) ||
        (record.transaction_number ?? "").toLowerCase().includes(keyword)
    );
  }, [records, search]);

  const totals = useMemo(
    () => ({
      collected: records.reduce(
        (sum, record) => sum + Number(record.amount_paid),
        0
      ),
      count: records.length,
      cash: records.filter(
        (record) => record.payment_method === "Cash"
      ).length,
    }),
    [records]
  );

  const totalPages = Math.max(
    1,
    Math.ceil(filteredRecords.length / PAGE_SIZE)
  );

  const currentPage = Math.min(page, totalPages);

  const startIndex = (currentPage - 1) * PAGE_SIZE;

  const paginatedRecords = useMemo(
    () =>
      filteredRecords.slice(
        startIndex,
        startIndex + PAGE_SIZE
      ),
    [filteredRecords, startIndex]
  );

  const startRecord =
    filteredRecords.length === 0 ? 0 : startIndex + 1;

  const endRecord = Math.min(
    startIndex + PAGE_SIZE,
    filteredRecords.length
  );

  const hasOutstandingAccounts = receivables.some(
    (record) => Number(record.balance) > 0
  );

  const openModal = () => {
    setForm(createEmptyForm());
    setFormError("");
    setModalOpen(true);
  };

  const closeModal = () => {
    if (saving) return;

    setModalOpen(false);
    setFormError("");
  };

  const handleSubmit = async (
    event: React.FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>
  ) => {
    event.preventDefault();
    setFormError("");
    setError("");
    setSuccess("");

    if (!form.AR_ID) {
      setFormError(
        "Please select an unpaid or partially paid account."
      );
      return;
    }

    if (form.amount_paid <= 0) {
      setFormError("Payment amount must be greater than zero.");
      return;
    }

    if (!form.payment_date) {
      setFormError("Please select a payment date.");
      return;
    }

    if (
      form.payment_method === "E-Wallet" &&
      !form.transaction_number?.trim()
    ) {
      setFormError(
        "Please enter the e-wallet transaction number."
      );
      return;
    }

    const selectedAR = receivables.find(
      (record) => record.AR_ID === form.AR_ID
    );

    if (!selectedAR) {
      setFormError(
        "Selected accounts receivable record was not found."
      );
      return;
    }

    if (form.amount_paid > Number(selectedAR.balance)) {
      setFormError(
        "Payment cannot exceed the remaining balance."
      );
      return;
    }

    try {
      setSaving(true);

      const paymentData: CollectionCreate = {
        ...form,
        transaction_number:
          form.payment_method === "E-Wallet"
            ? form.transaction_number?.trim() || null
            : null,
      };

      await paymentCollectionAPI.create(paymentData);

      setModalOpen(false);
      setSuccess("Payment successfully recorded.");
      setForm(createEmptyForm());
      setPage(1);

      await loadData();
    } catch (err) {
      setFormError(
        err instanceof Error
          ? err.message
          : "Failed to record payment."
      );
    } finally {
      setSaving(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <main className="min-h-full bg-green-50 p-4 text-slate-800 sm:p-6 md:p-8">
      <div className="mx-auto max-w-7xl space-y-6">
        {/* Notifications */}
        {error && (
          <div
            role="alert"
            className="rounded-lg border border-red-200 bg-red-50 p-3 text-sm text-red-700"
          >
            {error}
          </div>
        )}

        {success && (
          <div
            role="status"
            className="rounded-lg border border-green-200 bg-green-50 p-3 text-sm text-green-700"
          >
            {success}
          </div>
        )}

        {/* Summary Cards */}
        <section className="grid gap-4 sm:grid-cols-3">
          <SummaryCard
            title="Total Collected"
            value={money(totals.collected)}
            color="text-green-600"
          />

          <SummaryCard
            title="Payment Transactions"
            value={String(totals.count)}
            color="text-blue-600"
          />

          <SummaryCard
            title="Cash Transactions"
            value={String(totals.cash)}
            color="text-orange-600"
          />
        </section>

        {/* Payment History */}
        <section className="overflow-hidden rounded-xl border border-[#D1D5DB] bg-[#E5E7EB] shadow-sm">
          <div className="flex flex-col gap-3 border-b border-[#D1D5DB] p-5 sm:flex-row sm:items-center sm:justify-between">
            <div>
              <h2 className="text-lg font-semibold text-slate-800">
                Payment History
              </h2>

              <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-slate-600">
                View and manage recorded customer payments.
              </p>
            </div>

            <div className="flex flex-col gap-2 sm:flex-row">
              <input
                type="text"
                placeholder="Search payment, customer, or transaction..."
                value={search}
                onChange={(event) => {
                  setSearch(event.target.value);
                  setPage(1);
                }}
                className="w-full rounded-lg border border-[#D1D5DB] bg-white px-4 py-2.5 text-sm text-slate-800 outline-none focus:border-blue-500 focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-100 sm:w-72"
              />

              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={openModal}
                disabled={loading || !hasOutstandingAccounts}
                className="inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-2 whitespace-nowrap rounded-lg bg-green-700 px-4 py-2.5 text-sm font-medium text-white transition hover:bg-blue-700 disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-50"
              >
                <span className="text-lg leading-none">+</span>
                Add New Payment
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>

          {loading ? (
            <p className="py-12 text-center text-sm text-slate-500">
              Loading payment history...
            </p>
          ) : (
            <>

                <table className="w-full min-w-[1100px] text-left text-sm">
                  <thead className="border-b border-[#D1D5DB] bg-[#D1D5DB] text-xs uppercase text-slate-600">

                      <th className="px-4 py-3 text-center">
                        Payment No.
                      </th>
                      <th className="px-4 py-3 text-center">
                        AR No.
                      </th>
                      <th className="px-4 py-3 text-center">
                        Invoice No.
                      </th>
                      <th className="px-4 py-3 text-center">
                        Customer
                      </th>
                      <th className="px-4 py-3 text-center">
                        Payment Date
                      </th>
                      <th className="px-4 py-3 text-center">
                        Amount
                      </th>
                      <th className="px-4 py-3 text-center">
                        Method
                      </th>
                      <th className="px-4 py-3 text-center">
                        Transaction No.
                      </th>
                    </tr>
                  </thead>

                    {paginatedRecords.map((record) => (
                      <tr
                        key={record.payment_id}
                        className="text-center transition hover:bg-[#DDE1E5]"
                      >
                        <td className="px-4 py-3 font-medium text-slate-800">
                          PAY-{record.payment_id}
                        </td>

                        <td className="px-4 py-3">
                          AR-{record.AR_ID}
                        </td>

                        <td className="px-4 py-3">
                          {record.invoice_id
                            ? `INV-${record.invoice_id}`
                            : "â€”"}
                        </td>

                        <td className="px-4 py-3">
                          {record.customer_name}
                        </td>

                        <td className="whitespace-nowrap px-4 py-3">

                        </td>

                        <td className="px-4 py-3 font-semibold tabular-nums">
                          {money(record.amount_paid)}
                        </td>

                        <td className="px-4 py-3">
                          {record.payment_method ?? "â€”"}
                        </td>

                        <td className="px-4 py-3">
                          {record.transaction_number || "â€”"}
                        </td>

                    ))}

                    {paginatedRecords.length === 0 && (
                      <tr>
                        <td
                          colSpan={8}

                        >
                          {search
                            ? "No matching payment records found."
                            : "No payment records yet."}
                        </td>
                      </tr>
                    )}
                  </tbody>
                </table>
              </div>

              <div className="flex flex-col gap-3 border-t border-[#D1D5DB] bg-gray px-5 py-4 sm:flex-row sm:items-center sm:justify-between">

                  <span className="font-semibold text-slate-700">
                    {startRecord}

                  {" "}to{" "}
                  <span className="font-semibold text-slate-700">
                    {endRecord}

                  {" "}of{" "}
                  <span className="font-semibold text-slate-700">
                    {filteredRecords.length}
                  </span>
                  {" "}payments
                </p>

                <div className="flex items-center justify-end gap-2">
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    disabled={currentPage === 1}
                    onClick={() => setPage(currentPage - 1)}

                  >
                    Previous
                  </button>

                  <span className="px-2 text-sm font-medium text-slate-600">
                    Page {currentPage} of {totalPages}
                  </span>

                  <button
                    type="button"
                    disabled={currentPage === totalPages}
                    onClick={() => setPage(currentPage + 1)}
                    className="rounded-lg border border-slate-300 px-3 py-2 text-sm font-medium text-slate-700 transition hover:bg-slate-50 disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-40"
                  >
                    Next
                  </button>
                </div>
              </div>
            </>
          )}
        </section>
      </div>

      {/* Add Payment Modal */}
      {modalOpen && (
        <div
          className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center overflow-y-auto bg-black/50 p-4"
          onMouseDown={(event) => {
            if (event.target === event.currentTarget) {
              closeModal();
            }
          }}
        >
          <section
            role="dialog"
            aria-modal="true"
            aria-labelledby="payment-modal-title"
            className="my-auto w-full max-w-2xl overflow-hidden rounded-xl border border-[#D1D5DB] bg-white shadow-xl"
          >
            {/* Modal Header */}
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between border-b border-[#D1D5DB] px-6 py-4">
              <div>
                <h2
                  id="payment-modal-title"
                  className="text-lg font-semibold text-slate-900"
                >
                  Record New Payment
                </h2>

                <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-slate-500">
                  Enter the customer's payment information.
                </p>
              </div>

              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={closeModal}
                disabled={saving}
                aria-label="Close modal"
                className="rounded-lg p-2 text-slate-500 transition hover:bg-slate-100 hover:text-slate-800 disabled:opacity-50"
              >
                <span className="text-xl leading-none">Ã—</span>
              </button>
            </div>

            {/* Modal Form */}
            <form onSubmit={handleSubmit}>
              <div className="max-h-[70vh] space-y-4 overflow-y-auto p-6">
                {/* Accounts Receivable */}
                <div>
                  <label
                    htmlFor="payment-ar"
                    className="mb-1.5 block text-sm font-medium text-slate-700"
                  >
                    Accounts Receivable
                  </label>

                  <select
                    id="payment-ar"
                    value={form.AR_ID || ""}
                    onChange={(event) =>
                      setForm({
                        ...form,
                        AR_ID: Number(event.target.value),
                      })
                    }
                    required
                    className="w-full rounded-lg border border-[#D1D5DB] bg-white px-3 py-2.5 text-sm text-slate-800 outline-none focus:border-blue-500 focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-100"
                  >
                    <option value="">Select an account</option>

                    {receivables
                      .filter(
                        (record) => Number(record.balance) > 0
                      )
                      .map((record) => (
                        <option
                          key={record.AR_ID}
                          value={record.AR_ID}
                        >
                          AR-{record.AR_ID} Â· Invoice #
                          {record.invoice_id} Â· Balance:{" "}
                          {money(record.balance)}
                        </option>
                      ))}
                  </select>
                </div>

                {/* Payment Date */}
                <div>
                  <label
                    htmlFor="payment-date"
                    className="mb-1.5 block text-sm font-medium text-slate-700"
                  >
                    Payment Date
                  </label>

                  <input
                    id="payment-date"
                    type="date"
                    value={form.payment_date}
                    onChange={(event) =>
                      setForm({
                        ...form,
                        payment_date: event.target.value,
                      })
                    }
                    required
                    className="w-full rounded-lg border border-[#D1D5DB] bg-white px-3 py-2.5 text-sm text-slate-800 outline-none focus:border-blue-500 focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-100"
                  />
                </div>

                {/* Payment Method */}
                <div>
                  <label
                    htmlFor="payment-method"
                    className="mb-1.5 block text-sm font-medium text-slate-700"
                  >
                    Payment Method
                  </label>

                  <select
                    id="payment-method"
                    value={form.payment_method ?? "Cash"}
                    onChange={(event) =>
                      setForm({
                        ...form,
                        payment_method: event.target.value,
                        transaction_number: null,
                      })
                    }
                    className="w-full rounded-lg border border-[#D1D5DB] bg-white px-3 py-2.5 text-sm text-slate-800 outline-none focus:border-blue-500 focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-100"
                  >
                    <option value="Cash">Cash</option>
                    <option value="E-Wallet">E-Wallet</option>
                    <option value="Other">Other</option>
                  </select>
                </div>

                {/* E-Wallet Transaction Number */}
                {form.payment_method === "E-Wallet" && (
                  <div>
                    <label
                      htmlFor="transaction-number"
                      className="mb-1.5 block text-sm font-medium text-slate-700"
                    >
                      Transaction Number
                    </label>

                    <input
                      id="transaction-number"
                      type="text"
                      value={form.transaction_number ?? ""}
                      onChange={(event) =>
                        setForm({
                          ...form,
                          transaction_number: event.target.value,
                        })
                      }
                      required
                      maxLength={100}
                      placeholder="Enter e-wallet transaction number"
                      className="w-full rounded-lg border border-[#D1D5DB] bg-white px-3 py-2.5 text-sm text-slate-800 outline-none focus:border-blue-500 focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-100"
                    />
                  </div>
                )}

                {/* Amount Received */}
                <div>
                  <label
                    htmlFor="payment-amount"
                    className="mb-1.5 block text-sm font-medium text-slate-700"
                  >
                    Amount Received (â‚±)
                  </label>

                  <input
                    id="payment-amount"
                    type="number"
                    min="0.01"
                    step="0.01"
                    value={form.amount_paid || ""}
                    onChange={(event) =>
                      setForm({
                        ...form,
                        amount_paid: Number(event.target.value),
                      })
                    }
                    required
                    placeholder="Enter payment amount"
                    className="w-full rounded-lg border border-[#D1D5DB] bg-white px-3 py-2.5 text-sm text-slate-800 outline-none focus:border-blue-500 focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-100"
                  />
                </div>

                {/* Selected Account Balance */}
                {form.AR_ID !== 0 && (
                  <div className="rounded-lg border border-blue-200 bg-blue-50 p-3">
                    {(() => {
                      const selectedAR = receivables.find(
                        (record) => record.AR_ID === form.AR_ID
                      );

                      return selectedAR ? (
                        <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-3 text-sm">
                          <span className="font-medium text-blue-800">
                            Remaining Balance
                          </span>

                          <span className="font-bold text-blue-900">
                            {money(selectedAR.balance)}
                          </span>
                        </div>
                      ) : null;
                    })()}
                  </div>
                )}

                {/* Form Error */}
                {formError && (
                  <div
                    role="alert"
                    className="rounded-lg border border-red-200 bg-red-50 p-3 text-sm text-red-700"
                  >
                    {formError}
                  </div>
                )}
              </div>

              {/* Modal Footer */}
              <div className="flex flex-col-reverse gap-2 border-t border-[#D1D5DB] bg-slate-50 px-6 py-4 sm:flex-row sm:justify-end">
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={closeModal}
                  disabled={saving}
                  className="rounded-lg border border-slate-300 bg-white px-5 py-2.5 text-sm font-medium text-slate-700 transition hover:bg-slate-100 disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-50"
                >
                  Cancel
                </button>

                <button
                  type="submit"
                  disabled={saving || !hasOutstandingAccounts}
                  className="rounded-lg bg-blue-600 px-5 py-2.5 text-sm font-medium text-white transition hover:bg-blue-700 disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-50"
                >
                  {saving ? "Recording Payment..." : "Save Payment"}
                </button>
              </div>
            </form>
          </section>
        </div>
      )}
    </main>
  );
}

function SummaryCard({
  title,
  value,
  color,
}: {
  title: string;
  value: string;
  color: string;
}) {
  return (
    <div className="rounded-xl border border-[#D1D5DB] bg-[#E5E7EB] p-5 shadow-sm">
      <p className="text-sm font-medium text-slate-500">
        {title}
      </p>

      <p className={`mt-2 text-2xl font-bold ${color}`}>
        {value}
      </p>
    </div>
  );
}

"use client";

import { useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useState } from "react";

import {
  invoiceAPI,
  salesOrderAPI,
  accountsReceivableAPI,
} from "../../lib/api";

import type {
  SalesInvoice,
  SalesInvoiceCreate,
  InvoiceTaxType,
} from "../../types/invoice";

import type {
  SalesOrder,
  SalesOrderItem,
} from "../../types/order";

import type { AccountsReceivable } from "../../types/accounts-receivable";

const PAGE_SIZE = 10;

const INTEREST_RATES: Record<number, number> = {
  1: 0,
  3: 3,
  6: 5,
  12: 10,
};

const STATUS_STYLES: Record<string, string> = {
  Paid: "bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-700",
  "Partially Paid": "bg-amber-100 text-amber-700",
  Unpaid: "bg-slate-100 text-slate-700",
  Overdue: "bg-red-100 text-red-700",
  "AR Not Found": "bg-gray-100 text-gray-600",
};

const money = (amount: string | number) =>
  `â‚±${Number(amount).toLocaleString("en-PH", {
    minimumFractionDigits: 2,
    maximumFractionDigits: 2,
  })}`;

function escapeHtml(value: string | number | null | undefined) {
  return String(value ?? "â€”").replace(/[&<>"']/g, (character) => {
    const entities: Record<string, string> = {
      "&": "&amp;",
      "<": "&lt;",
      ">": "&gt;",
      '"': "&quot;",
      "'": "&#39;",
    };

    return entities[character];
  });
}

function getLocalDate() {
  const parts = new Intl.DateTimeFormat("en-CA", {
    timeZone: "Asia/Manila",
    year: "numeric",
    month: "2-digit",
    day: "2-digit",
  }).formatToParts(new Date());

  const year = parts.find((part) => part.type === "year")?.value;
  const month = parts.find((part) => part.type === "month")?.value;
  const day = parts.find((part) => part.type === "day")?.value;

  return year && month && day ? `${year}-${month}-${day}` : "";
}

function formatDate(value: string | null) {
  if (!value) return "â€”";

  const [year, month, day] = value.slice(0, 10).split("-").map(Number);

  if (
    !year ||
    !month ||
    !day ||
    month < 1 ||
    month > 12 ||
    day < 1 ||
    day > 31
  ) {
    return "â€”";
  }

  const date = new Date(year, month - 1, day);

  if (
    date.getFullYear() !== year ||
    date.getMonth() !== month - 1 ||
    date.getDate() !== day
  ) {
    return "â€”";
  }

  return date.toLocaleDateString("en-PH", {
    day: "2-digit",
    month: "long",
    year: "numeric",
  });
}

function calculateDueDate(invoiceDate: string, months: number) {
  if (!invoiceDate) return "";

  const [year, month, day] = invoiceDate.split("-").map(Number);

  if (
    !year ||
    !month ||
    !day ||
    month < 1 ||
    month > 12 ||
    day < 1 ||
    day > 31
  ) {
    return "";
  }

  const date = new Date(year, month - 1, 1);

  if (
    date.getFullYear() !== year ||
    date.getMonth() !== month - 1
  ) {
    return "";
  }

  const targetYear = year + Math.floor((month - 1 + months) / 12);
  const targetMonth = (month - 1 + months) % 12;

  const lastDay = new Date(targetYear, targetMonth + 1, 0).getDate();
  const dueDay = Math.min(day, lastDay);

  return `${targetYear}-${String(targetMonth + 1).padStart(2, "0")}-${String(
    dueDay
  ).padStart(2, "0")}`;
}

function SummaryCard({
  label,
  value,
  description,
  valueColor = "text-slate-900",
}: {
  label: string;
  value: string | number;
  description: string;
  valueColor?: string;
}) {
  return (
    <div className="rounded-xl border border-[#D1D5DB] bg-[#E5E7EB] p-5 shadow-sm">
      <p className="text-sm font-medium text-slate-600">{label}</p>

      <p className={`mt-2 text-2xl font-bold tracking-tight ${valueColor}`}>
        {value}
      </p>

      <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-slate-600">{description}</p>
    </div>
  );
}

export default function InvoicePanel() {

  const [orders, setOrders] = useState<SalesOrder[]>([]);
  const [receivables, setReceivables] = useState<AccountsReceivable[]>([]);

  const [currentPage, setCurrentPage] = useState(1);

  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);
  const [printingInvoiceId, setPrintingInvoiceId] = useState<number | null>(
    null
  );

  const [error, setError] = useState("");

  const [invoiceDate, setInvoiceDate] = useState("");
  const [today, setToday] = useState("");
  const [installmentMonths, setInstallmentMonths] = useState("1");

  const [taxType, setTaxType] = useState<InvoiceTaxType>("VAT");
  const [customTaxRate, setCustomTaxRate] = useState("0");

  useEffect(() => {
    const currentDate = getLocalDate();

    setToday(currentDate);
    setInvoiceDate(currentDate);
  }, []);

  const selectedMonths = Number(installmentMonths);
  const interestRate = INTEREST_RATES[selectedMonths] ?? 0;

  const taxRate =
    taxType === "VAT"
      ? 12
      : taxType === "Non-VAT" || taxType === "Tax-Exempt"
        ? 0
        : Number(customTaxRate);

  const selectedOrderAmount = () => {
    const order = orders.find(
      (item) => item.order_id === Number(orderId)
    );

    return order ? Number(order.total_amount) || 0 : 0;
  };

  const orderAmount = selectedOrderAmount();

  const interestAmount = Number(
    ((orderAmount * interestRate) / 100).toFixed(2)
  );

  const taxAmount = Number(
    ((orderAmount * taxRate) / 100).toFixed(2)
  );

  const finalInvoiceAmount =
    orderAmount + interestAmount + taxAmount;

  const getInvoiceStatus = (invoice: SalesInvoice) => {
    const ar = receivables.find(
      (record) => record.invoice_id === invoice.invoice_id
    );

    if (!ar) return "AR Not Found";

    const amountPaid = Number(ar.amount_paid) || 0;
    const balance = Number(ar.balance) || 0;

    if (balance <= 0) return "Paid";

    if (
      invoice.due_date &&
      today &&
      invoice.due_date.slice(0, 10) < today
    ) {
      return "Overdue";
    }

    if (amountPaid > 0) return "Partially Paid";

    return "Unpaid";
  };

  const loadData = useCallback(async () => {
    setLoading(true);
    setError("");

      const [invoiceData, orderData, arData] = await Promise.all([
        invoiceAPI.getAll(),
        salesOrderAPI.getAll(),
        accountsReceivableAPI.getAll(),
      ]);

      setInvoices(invoiceData);
      setOrders(orderData);
      setReceivables(arData);
    } catch (err) {
      setError(
        err instanceof Error
          ? err.message
          : "Unable to load invoices."
      );
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    void loadData();
  }, [loadData]);

  const invoicedOrderIds = useMemo(
    () => new Set(invoices.map((invoice) => invoice.order_id)),
    [invoices]
  );

  const availableOrders = useMemo(
    () =>
      orders.filter(
        (order) =>
          !invoicedOrderIds.has(order.order_id) &&
          order.status !== "Cancelled"
      ),
    [orders, invoicedOrderIds]
  );

  const totalPages = Math.max(
    1,
    Math.ceil(invoices.length / PAGE_SIZE)
  );

  const paginatedInvoices = useMemo(() => {
    const startIndex = (currentPage - 1) * PAGE_SIZE;

    return invoices.slice(startIndex, startIndex + PAGE_SIZE);
  }, [invoices, currentPage]);

  const totalInvoiced = invoices.reduce(
    (sum, invoice) => sum + Number(invoice.total_due),
    0
  );

  const totalTax = invoices.reduce(
    (sum, invoice) => sum + Number(invoice.tax_amount ?? 0),
    0
  );

  const overdueInvoices = invoices.filter(
    (invoice) => getInvoiceStatus(invoice) === "Overdue"
  ).length;

  useEffect(() => {
    if (currentPage > totalPages) {
      setCurrentPage(totalPages);
    }
  }, [currentPage, totalPages]);

  function resetForm() {
    if (saving) return;

    setOrderId("");
    setInvoiceDate(today);
    setInstallmentMonths("1");
    setTaxType("VAT");
    setCustomTaxRate("0");
    setShowForm(false);
    setError("");
  }

  async function handleCreateInvoice() {
    setError("");

    if (!orderId) {
      setError("Please select a sales order.");
      return;
    }

    if (!invoiceDate) {
      setError("Please select an invoice date.");
      return;
    }

    if (
      taxType === "Custom" &&
      (
        !Number.isFinite(taxRate) ||
        taxRate < 0 ||
        taxRate > 100
      )
    ) {
      setError("Custom tax rate must be between 0% and 100%.");
      return;
    }

    const calculatedDueDate = calculateDueDate(
      invoiceDate,
      selectedMonths
    );

    if (!calculatedDueDate) {
      setError("Unable to calculate the invoice due date.");
      return;
    }

    const data: SalesInvoiceCreate = {
      order_id: Number(orderId),
      invoice_date: invoiceDate,
      due_date: calculatedDueDate,
      installment_months: selectedMonths as 1 | 3 | 6 | 12,
      tax_type: taxType,
      tax_rate: taxRate,
    };

    setSaving(true);

    try {
      await invoiceAPI.create(data);

      setOrderId("");
      setInvoiceDate(today);
      setInstallmentMonths("1");
      setTaxType("VAT");
      setCustomTaxRate("0");
      setShowForm(false);

      await loadData();
    } catch (err) {
      setError(
        err instanceof Error
          ? err.message
          : "Unable to create invoice."
      );

    }
  }

                  <span>Tax Rate</span>
                  <strong>${escapeHtml(invoiceTaxRate.toFixed(2))}%</strong>
                </div>

                <div class="total-row">
                  <span>Tax Amount</span>
                  <strong>${escapeHtml(money(invoiceTax))}</strong>
                </div>

                <div class="total-row">
                  <span>Interest Rate</span>
                  <strong>${escapeHtml(Number(invoice.interest_rate).toFixed(2))}%</strong>
                </div>

                <div class="total-row">
                  <span>Interest Amount</span>
                  <strong>${escapeHtml(money(interest))}</strong>
                </div>

                <div class="total-row grand-total">
                  <span>Total Due</span>
                  <span>${escapeHtml(money(total))}</span>
                </div>
              </section>

              <footer class="footer">
                <p>Thank you for your purchase!</p>
                <p>This document was generated by Sales ERP.</p>
              </footer>
            </main>
          </body>
        </html>
      `;

      printWindow.document.open();
      printWindow.document.write(html);
      printWindow.document.close();
      printWindow.focus();
    } catch (err) {
      printWindow.close();

      setError(
        err instanceof Error
          ? `Unable to prepare invoice for printing: ${err.message}`
          : "Unable to prepare invoice for printing."
      );
    } finally {
      setPrintingInvoiceId(null);
    }
  }

  return (
    <div className="min-h-full space-y-5 bg-green-50 text-slate-800">
      {error && (
        <div
          role="alert"
          className="rounded-lg border border-red-200 bg-red-50 p-4 text-sm text-red-700"
        >
          {error}
        </div>
      )}

      <section className="grid gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4">
        <SummaryCard
          label="Total Invoices"
          value={invoices.length}
          description="Recorded sales invoices"
        />

        <SummaryCard
          label="Total Invoiced Amount"
          value={money(totalInvoiced)}
          description="Combined invoice value including tax and interest"
        />

        <SummaryCard
          label="Total Tax Amount"
          value={money(totalTax)}
          description="Tax recorded on sales invoices"
        />

        <SummaryCard
          label="Overdue Invoices"
          value={overdueInvoices}
          description="Invoices past their due date"
          valueColor="text-red-600"
        />
      </section>

      <section className="overflow-hidden rounded-xl border border-[#D1D5DB] bg-[#E5E7EB] shadow-sm">
        <div className="flex flex-col justify-between gap-4 border-b border-[#D1D5DB] p-5 sm:flex-row sm:items-center">
          <div>

            </h3>

            <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-slate-600">
              View and manage invoices generated from sales orders.
            </p>
          </div>

          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => {
              setError("");
              setShowForm(true);
            }}
            disabled={availableOrders.length === 0 || loading || !today}
            className="whitespace-nowrap rounded-lg bg-green-900 px-5 py-2.5 text-sm font-semibold text-white transition hover:bg-blue-800 disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-50"
          >
            + Add Sales Invoice
          </button>
        </div>

        <div className="overflow-x-auto">

            <thead className="bg-[#D1D5DB] text-slate-700">

                </th>

              {loading ? (

                      </span>
                      <span className="font-medium text-slate-800">

                      </span>
                    </div>

                      <span className="text-slate-600">
                        Interest Rate
                      </span>
                      <span className="font-medium text-slate-800">
                        {interestRate}%

                    </div>

                    <div className="flex justify-between gap-4">

                        <span className="font-bold text-slate-900">
                          Final Invoice Total
                        </span>
                        <span className="text-lg font-bold text-blue-900">
                          {money(finalInvoiceAmount)}
                        </span>
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                </div>
              )}
            </div>

            <div className="mt-5 flex flex-col-reverse justify-end gap-3 border-t border-[#D1D5DB] pt-5 sm:flex-row">
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={resetForm}
                disabled={saving}
                className="rounded-lg border border-[#D1D5DB] bg-white px-5 py-2.5 text-sm font-semibold text-slate-700 hover:bg-[#DDE1E5] disabled:opacity-50"
              >
                Cancel
              </button>

              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => void handleCreateInvoice()}
                disabled={
                  saving ||
                  availableOrders.length === 0 ||
                  !orderId ||
                  !invoiceDate ||
                  (
                    taxType === "Custom" &&
                    (
                      !Number.isFinite(taxRate) ||
                      taxRate < 0 ||
                      taxRate > 100
                    )
                  )
                }
                className="rounded-lg bg-slate-900 px-5 py-2.5 text-sm font-semibold text-white hover:bg-slate-700 disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-50"
              >
                {saving ? "Creating..." : "Add Sales Invoice"}
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

"use client";

import { useState } from "react";
import type { Customer } from "../../types/customer";

type CustomerTableProps = {
  customers: Customer[];
  loading: boolean;
  onEdit: (customer: Customer) => void;
  onToggleStatus: (customer: Customer) => void;
};

const PAGE_SIZE = 10;

// Safely extracts contact numbers from strings, arrays, or objects.
function extractContactNumbers(value: unknown): string[] {
  if (value === null || value === undefined || value === "") {
    return [];
  }

  if (typeof value === "string" || typeof value === "number") {
    return [String(value).trim()].filter(Boolean);
  }

  if (Array.isArray(value)) {
    return value.flatMap(extractContactNumbers);
  }

  if (typeof value === "object") {
    const contactObject = value as Record<string, unknown>;

    const preferredKeys = [
      "contact",
      "contact_number",
      "phone",
      "phone_number",
      "mobile",
      "landline",
      "number",
      "value",
    ];

    for (const key of preferredKeys) {
      if (contactObject[key] !== undefined) {
        return extractContactNumbers(contactObject[key]);
      }
    }

    return Object.values(contactObject).flatMap((item) =>
      typeof item === "string" || typeof item === "number"
        ? extractContactNumbers(item)
        : []
    );
  }

  return [];
}

function getContactType(contact: string): string {
  const digits = contact.replace(/\D/g, "");

  // Philippine mobile number formats: 09XXXXXXXXX or 639XXXXXXXXX.
  if (/^09\d{9}$/.test(digits) || /^639\d{9}$/.test(digits)) {
    return "Mobile Phone";
  }

  // Philippine landline numbers commonly use area codes,
  // including 02 for Metro Manila.
  if (
    /^02\d{7,8}$/.test(digits) ||
    /^0[3-9]\d{6,8}$/.test(digits) ||
    /^632\d{7,8}$/.test(digits)
  ) {
    return "Landline";
  }

  return "Contact Number";
}

export default function CustomerTable({
  customers,
  loading,
  onEdit,
  onToggleStatus,
}: CustomerTableProps) {
  const [currentPage, setCurrentPage] = useState(1);

  const totalPages = Math.max(
    1,
    Math.ceil(customers.length / PAGE_SIZE)
  );

  const safePage = Math.min(currentPage, totalPages);

  const startIndex = (safePage - 1) * PAGE_SIZE;
  const paginatedCustomers = customers.slice(
    startIndex,
    startIndex + PAGE_SIZE
  );

  if (loading) {
    return (
      <div className="p-12 text-center text-sm text-slate-500">
        Loading customers...
      </div>
    );
  }

  if (customers.length === 0) {
    return (
      <div className="p-12 text-center">
        <p className="font-medium text-slate-700">
          No customers found.
        </p>
        <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-slate-500">
          Add a customer or change your search.
        </p>
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div>
      <div className="overflow-x-auto">
        <table className="w-full min-w-[1050px] text-left text-sm">
          <thead className="text-slate-600 bg-[#D1D5DB]">
            
            <tr>
              <th className="px-5 py-4 text-center font-semibold">
                Customer ID
              </th>
              <th className="px-5 text-center py-4 font-semibold">
                Customer Name
              </th>
              <th className="px-5 text-center py-4 font-semibold">
                Email
              </th>
              <th className="px-5 text-center py-4 font-semibold">
                Contact
              </th>
              <th className="px-5 text-center py-4 font-semibold">
                Type
              </th>
              <th className="px-5 text-center py-4 font-semibold">
                Status
              </th>
              <th className="px-5 text-center py-4 font-semibold">
                Address
              </th>
              <th className="px-5 py-4 text-center font-semibold">
                Actions
              </th>
            </tr>
          </thead>

          <tbody className="divide-y divide-slate-100">
            {paginatedCustomers.map((customer) => {
              const contactNumbers = extractContactNumbers(
                customer.contact
              );

              return (
                <tr
                  key={customer.customer_id}
                  className="transition-colors hover:bg-[#DDE1E5]"
                >
                  <td className="px-5 py-4 text-center font-semibold text-slate-700">
                    #{customer.customer_id}
                  </td>

                  <td className="px-5 py-4 font-medium text-slate-900">
                    {customer.name}
                  </td>

                  <td className="px-5 py-4 text-slate-600">
                    {customer.email || "â€”"}
                  </td>

                  <td className="px-5 py-4 text-slate-600">
                    {contactNumbers.length > 0 ? (
                      <div className="space-y-1">
                        {contactNumbers.map((contact, index) => (
                          <div key={`${contact}-${index}`}>
                            <p className="font-medium text-slate-800">
                              {contact}
                            </p>
                            <span className="text-xs text-slate-500">
                              {getContactType(contact)}
                            </span>
                          </div>
                        ))}
                      </div>
                    ) : (
                      "â€”"
                    )}
                  </td>

                  <td className="px-5 py-4 text-slate-600">
                    {customer.customer_type}
                  </td>

                  <td className="px-5 py-4">
                    <span
                      className={`inline-flex rounded-full px-3 py-1 text-xs font-semibold ${
                        customer.status
                          ? "bg-green-100 text-green-700"
                          : "bg-slate-100 text-slate-600"
                      }`}
                    >
                      {customer.status ? "Active" : "Inactive"}
                    </span>
                  </td>

                  <td className="px-5 py-4 text-slate-600">
                    {customer.address || "â€”"}
                  </td>

                  <td className="px-5 py-4">
                    <div className="flex justify-end gap-2">
                      <button
                        type="button"
                        onClick={() => onEdit(customer)}
                        className="rounded-lg border border-blue-200 bg-blue-50 px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-semibold text-blue-700 transition hover:bg-blue-100"
                      >
                        Edit
                      </button>

                      <button
                        type="button"
                        onClick={() => onToggleStatus(customer)}
                        className={`rounded-lg border px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-semibold transition ${
                          customer.status
                            ? "border-red-200 bg-red-50 text-red-700 hover:bg-red-100"
                            : "border-green-200 bg-green-50 text-green-700 hover:bg-green-100"
                        }`}
                      >
                        {customer.status ? "Deactivate" : "Activate"}
                      </button>
                    </div>
                  </td>
                </tr>
              );
            })}
          </tbody>
        </table>
      </div>

      {/* Pagination */}
      <div className="flex flex-col gap-3 border-t border-slate-200 bg-gray px-5 py-4 sm:flex-row sm:items-center sm:justify-between">
        <p className="text-sm text-slate-500">
          Showing{" "}
          <span className="font-semibold text-slate-700">
            {startIndex + 1}
          </span>
          {" "}to{" "}
          <span className="font-semibold text-slate-700">
            {Math.min(startIndex + PAGE_SIZE, customers.length)}
          </span>
          {" "}of{" "}
          <span className="font-semibold text-slate-700">
            {customers.length}
          </span>
          {" "}customers
        </p>

        <div className="flex items-center justify-end gap-2">
          <button
            type="button"
            disabled={safePage === 1}
            onClick={() => setCurrentPage(safePage - 1)}
            className="rounded-lg border border-slate-300 px-3 py-2 text-sm font-medium text-slate-700 transition hover:bg-slate-50 disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-40"
          >
            Previous
          </button>

          <span className="px-2 text-sm font-medium text-slate-600">
            Page {safePage} of {totalPages}
          </span>

          <button
            type="button"
            disabled={safePage === totalPages}
            onClick={() => setCurrentPage(safePage + 1)}
            className="rounded-lg border border-slate-300 px-3 py-2 text-sm font-medium text-slate-700 transition hover:bg-slate-50 disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-40"
          >
            Next
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
