"use client";

import { useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useState } from "react";
import {
  customerAPI,
  deliveryAPI,
  salesOrderAPI,
} from "../../lib/api";

import type { Customer } from "../../types/customer";
import type {
  Delivery,
  DeliveryCreate,
  DeliveryStatus,
  Warehouse,
} from "../../types/delivery";
import type { SalesOrder } from "../../types/order";

const statuses: DeliveryStatus[] = [
  "Pending",
  "Preparing",
  "Released by Inventory",
  "Delivered",
  "Cancelled",
];


const statusStyles: Record<string, string> = {
  Pending:
    "border-amber-200 bg-amber-50 text-amber-800",
  Preparing:
    "border-blue-200 bg-blue-50 text-blue-800",
  "Released by Inventory":
    "border-teal-200 bg-teal-50 text-teal-800",
  Delivered:
    "border-green-200 bg-green-50 text-green-800",
  Cancelled:
    "border-red-200 bg-red-50 text-red-800",
  Shipped:
    "border-teal-200 bg-teal-50 text-teal-800",
};


const allowedTransitions: Record<string, DeliveryStatus[]> = {
  Pending: ["Preparing", "Cancelled"],
  Preparing: ["Released by Inventory", "Cancelled"],
  "Released by Inventory": ["Delivered", "Cancelled"],
  Delivered: [],
  Cancelled: [],
  Shipped: ["Delivered", "Cancelled"],
};

const displayStatus = (status: DeliveryStatus): string =>
  status === "Shipped" ? "Released by Inventory" : status;

const formatDate = (date: string | null) => {
  if (!date) return "â€”";

  const parsedDate = new Date(`${date}T00:00:00`);

  if (Number.isNaN(parsedDate.getTime())) return "â€”";

  return parsedDate.toLocaleDateString("en-PH", {
    day: "2-digit",
    month: "long",
    year: "numeric",
  });
};

export default function DeliveryPanel() {
  const [deliveries, setDeliveries] = useState<Delivery[]>([]);
  const [orders, setOrders] = useState<SalesOrder[]>([]);
  const [customers, setCustomers] = useState<Customer[]>([]);
  const [warehouses, setWarehouses] = useState<Warehouse[]>([]);

  const [search, setSearch] = useState("");
  const [currentPage, setCurrentPage] = useState(1);
  const [pageSize] = useState(10);

  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);
  const [updatingId, setUpdatingId] = useState<number | null>(null);
  const [error, setError] = useState("");

  const [showForm, setShowForm] = useState(false);

  const [orderId, setOrderId] = useState("");
  const [warehouseId, setWarehouseId] = useState("");
  const [pickupDate, setPickupDate] = useState("");

  const loadData = useCallback(async () => {
    setLoading(true);
    setError("");

    try {
      const [
        deliveryData,
        orderData,
        customerData,
        warehouseData,
      ] = await Promise.all([
        deliveryAPI.getAll(),
        salesOrderAPI.getAll(),
        customerAPI.getAll(),
        deliveryAPI.getWarehouses(),
      ]);

      setDeliveries(deliveryData);
      setOrders(orderData);
      setCustomers(customerData);
      setWarehouses(warehouseData);
    } catch (err) {
      setError(
        err instanceof Error
          ? err.message
          : "Unable to load pickup information."
      );
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    void loadData();
  }, [loadData]);

  useEffect(() => {
    setCurrentPage(1);
  }, [search]);

  const customerMap = useMemo(
    () =>
      new Map(
        customers.map((customer) => [
          customer.customer_id,
          customer.name,
        ])
      ),
    [customers]
  );

  const warehouseMap = useMemo(
    () =>
      new Map(
        warehouses.map((warehouse) => [
          warehouse.WH_ID,
          warehouse.Warehouse_Name,
        ])
      ),
    [warehouses]
  );

  const orderMap = useMemo(
    () => new Map(orders.map((order) => [order.order_id, order])),
    [orders]
  );

  const filteredDeliveries = useMemo(() => {
    const term = search.trim().toLowerCase();

    if (!term) return deliveries;

    return deliveries.filter((delivery) => {
      const order = orderMap.get(delivery.order_id);

      const customerName = order
        ? customerMap.get(order.customer_id) ?? ""
        : "";

      const warehouseName =
        warehouseMap.get(delivery.WH_ID) ?? "";

      return (
        String(delivery.delivery_id).includes(term) ||
        String(delivery.order_id).includes(term) ||
        customerName.toLowerCase().includes(term) ||
        warehouseName.toLowerCase().includes(term) ||
        displayStatus(delivery.status).toLowerCase().includes(term)
      );
    });
  }, [
    deliveries,
    search,
    orderMap,
    customerMap,
    warehouseMap,
  ]);

  const totalPages = Math.max(
    1,
    Math.ceil(filteredDeliveries.length / pageSize)
  );

  const safeCurrentPage = Math.min(currentPage, totalPages);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (currentPage > totalPages) {
      setCurrentPage(totalPages);
    }
  }, [currentPage, totalPages]);

  const startIndex =
    filteredDeliveries.length === 0
      ? 0
      : (safeCurrentPage - 1) * pageSize;

  const endIndex = Math.min(
    startIndex + pageSize,
    filteredDeliveries.length
  );

  const paginatedDeliveries = useMemo(
    () =>
      filteredDeliveries.slice(
        startIndex,
        startIndex + pageSize
      ),
    [filteredDeliveries, startIndex, pageSize]
  );

  const pendingCount = deliveries.filter(
    (delivery) => displayStatus(delivery.status) === "Pending"
  ).length;

  const preparingCount = deliveries.filter(
    (delivery) => displayStatus(delivery.status) === "Preparing"
  ).length;

  const readyForPickupCount = deliveries.filter(
    (delivery) =>
      displayStatus(delivery.status) === "Released by Inventory"
  ).length;

  const deliveredCount = deliveries.filter(
    (delivery) => displayStatus(delivery.status) === "Delivered"
  ).length;

  function resetForm() {
    setOrderId("");
    setWarehouseId("");
    setPickupDate("");
    setShowForm(false);
  }

  async function handleCreateDelivery() {
    setError("");

    if (!orderId || !warehouseId) {
      setError("Please select a sales order and warehouse.");
      return;
    }

    const selectedOrder = orders.find(
      (order) => order.order_id === Number(orderId)
    );

    if (!selectedOrder || selectedOrder.status !== "Approved") {
      setError(
        "Only approved sales orders can be added to customer pickup."
      );
      return;
    }

    const alreadyHasPickup = deliveries.some(
      (delivery) => delivery.order_id === Number(orderId)
    );

    if (alreadyHasPickup) {
      setError(
        "A pickup record already exists for this sales order."
      );
      return;
    }

    const data: DeliveryCreate = {
      order_id: Number(orderId),
      WH_ID: Number(warehouseId),
      delivery_date: pickupDate || null,
      delivery_address: "",
    };

    setSaving(true);

    try {
      await deliveryAPI.create(data);
      resetForm();
      setCurrentPage(1);
      await loadData();
    } catch (err) {
      setError(
        err instanceof Error
          ? err.message
          : "Unable to create pickup record."
      );
    } finally {
      setSaving(false);
    }
  }

  async function handleStatusChange(
    delivery: Delivery,
    newStatus: DeliveryStatus
  ) {
    const currentStatus = displayStatus(delivery.status);

    if (
      currentStatus === newStatus ||
      updatingId !== null
    ) {
      return;
    }

    const permittedStatuses =
      allowedTransitions[delivery.status] ??
      allowedTransitions[currentStatus] ??
      [];

    if (!permittedStatuses.includes(newStatus)) {
      setError(
        `The status cannot be changed from ${currentStatus} to ${newStatus}.`
      );
      return;
    }

    let receivedBy: string | undefined;

    if (newStatus === "Delivered") {
      const customerName = window.prompt(
        "Enter the name of the customer who collected the items:"
      );

      if (customerName === null) return;

      receivedBy = customerName.trim();

      if (!receivedBy) {
        setError(
          "Enter the customer's name to confirm that the items were collected."
        );
        return;
      }
    }

    if (newStatus === "Cancelled") {
      const confirmed = window.confirm(
        "Are you sure you want to cancel this customer pickup?"
      );

      if (!confirmed) return;
    }

    setError("");
    setUpdatingId(delivery.delivery_id);

    try {
      const updated = await deliveryAPI.updateStatus(
        delivery.delivery_id,
        newStatus,
        receivedBy
      );

      setDeliveries((current) =>
        current.map((item) =>
          item.delivery_id === updated.delivery_id
            ? updated
            : item
        )
      );
    } catch (err) {
      setError(
        err instanceof Error
          ? err.message
          : "Unable to update pickup status."
      );
    } finally {
      setUpdatingId(null);
    }
  }

  function goToPage(page: number) {
    setCurrentPage(
      Math.max(1, Math.min(page, totalPages))
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="min-w-0 space-y-5 bg-gray text-slate-800">
      {error && (
        <div
          role="alert"
          className="rounded-lg border border-red-200 bg-red-50 p-4 text-sm text-red-700"
        >
          {error}
        </div>
      )}

      <section className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2 xl:grid-cols-4">
        <SummaryCard
          title="Total Pickups"
          value={deliveries.length.toString()}
        />

        <SummaryCard
          title="Pending"
          value={pendingCount.toString()}
          valueStyle="text-amber-700"
        />

        <SummaryCard
          title="Ready for Pickup"
          value={readyForPickupCount.toString()}
          valueStyle="text-teal-700"
        />

        <SummaryCard
          title="Collected"
          value={deliveredCount.toString()}
          valueStyle="text-green-700"
        />
      </section>

      <section className="overflow-hidden rounded-xl border border-[#D1D5DB] bg-[#E5E7EB] shadow-sm">
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-4 border-b border-[#D1D5DB] p-5 lg:flex-row lg:items-center lg:justify-between">
          <div className="min-w-0">
            <h2 className="font-bold text-slate-900">
              Customer Pickup Records
            </h2>

            <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-slate-600">
              Prepare orders, release items from inventory, and confirm customer collection.
            </p>
          </div>

          <div className="flex w-full min-w-0 flex-col gap-3 sm:flex-row lg:w-auto lg:flex-1 lg:justify-end">
            <div className="relative min-w-0 flex-1 lg:max-w-md">
              <input
                type="search"
                value={search}
                onChange={(e) => setSearch(e.target.value)}
                placeholder="Search pickup, order, customer, warehouse, or status..."
                className="w-full rounded-lg border border-slate-300 bg-white px-4 py-2.5 text-sm text-slate-900 outline-none transition placeholder:text-slate-400 focus:border-[#4F8585] focus:ring-2 focus:ring-[#4F8585]/20"
              />
            </div>

            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => {
                setError("");
                setShowForm(true);
              }}
              className="shrink-0 whitespace-nowrap rounded-lg bg-[#4F8585] px-4 py-2.5 text-sm font-semibold text-white transition hover:bg-[#3D6D6D]"
            >
              + Create Pickup
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="overflow-x-auto">
          <table className="w-full min-w-[1050px] text-left text-sm">
            <thead className="bg-[#D1D5DB] text-xs uppercase tracking-wide text-slate-700">
              <tr>
                <th className="whitespace-nowrap px-5 py-4 text-center font-semibold">
                  Pickup No.
                </th>

                <th className="whitespace-nowrap px-5 py-4 text-center font-semibold">
                  Order No.

                <th className="whitespace-nowrap px-5 py-4 text-center font-semibold">

                  Pickup Status

              {loading ? (
                <tr>
                  <td
                    colSpan={6}
                    className="px-5 py-10 text-center text-slate-600"
                  >
                    Loading pickup records...
                  </td>

                    </option>

                </select>

                <label className="mb-2 block text-sm font-medium text-slate-700">
                  Expected Pickup Date

                <input
                  type="date"

                  onChange={(e) => setPickupDate(e.target.value)}
                  className="w-full rounded-lg border border-slate-300 bg-white px-4 py-3 text-sm text-slate-800 outline-none focus:border-[#4F8585]"
                />
              </div>

              <div className="rounded-lg border border-[#D1D5DB] bg-white p-4">
                <p className="text-sm font-semibold text-slate-800">
                  Customer Pickup
                </p>
                <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-slate-600">
                  The customer will collect the prepared items directly from the physical store. No delivery address is required.
                </p>
              </div>

              <div className="flex flex-col-reverse justify-end gap-3 border-t border-[#D1D5DB] pt-5 sm:flex-row">
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={resetForm}
                  disabled={saving}

                  className="rounded-lg bg-[#4F8585] px-5 py-3 text-sm font-semibold text-white transition hover:bg-[#3D6D6D] disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-50"
                >
                  {saving ? "Creating..." : "Create Pickup"}
                </button>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

function SummaryCard({
  title,
  value,
  valueStyle = "text-slate-900",
}: {
  title: string;
  value: string;
  valueStyle?: string;
}) {
  return (
    <div className="rounded-xl border border-[#D1D5DB] bg-[#E5E7EB] p-5 shadow-sm">
      <p className="text-sm font-semibold text-slate-600">
        {title}
      </p>

      <p className={`mt-2 text-2xl font-bold ${valueStyle}`}>
        {value}
      </p>
    </div>
  );
}

"use client";

import { useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useState } from "react";

import {
  customerAPI,
  invoiceAPI,
  salesOrderAPI,
  salesReturnAPI,
} from "../../lib/api";

import type { Customer } from "../../types/customer";
import type { SalesInvoice } from "../../types/invoice";
import type {
  SalesOrder,
  SalesOrderDetail,
  SalesOrderItem,
} from "../../types/order";
import type { SalesReturn, SalesReturnCreate } from "../../types/return";

const PAGE_SIZE = 10;

const money = (amount: string | number) =>
  `â‚±${Number(amount).toLocaleString("en-PH", {
    minimumFractionDigits: 2,
    maximumFractionDigits: 2,
  })}`;

function getLocalDate() {
  const date = new Date();
  const year = date.getFullYear();
  const month = String(date.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, "0");
  const day = String(date.getDate()).padStart(2, "0");

  return `${year}-${month}-${day}`;
}

function formatDate(value: string) {
  if (!value) return "â€”";

  const date = new Date(`${value.slice(0, 10)}T00:00:00`);

  if (Number.isNaN(date.getTime())) return "â€”";

  return date.toLocaleDateString("en-PH", {
    day: "2-digit",
    month: "long",
    year: "numeric",
  });
}

function SummaryCard({
  label,
  value,
  description,
}: {
  label: string;
  value: string | number;
  description: string;
}) {
  return (
    <div className="rounded-xl border border-[#D1D5DB] bg-[#E5E7EB] p-5 shadow-sm">
      <p className="text-sm font-medium text-slate-600">{label}</p>
      <p className="mt-2 text-2xl font-bold tracking-tight text-slate-900">
        {value}
      </p>
      <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-slate-500">{description}</p>
    </div>
  );
}

export default function ReturnsPanel() {
  const [returns, setReturns] = useState<SalesReturn[]>([]);
  const [orders, setOrders] = useState<SalesOrder[]>([]);
  const [invoices, setInvoices] = useState<SalesInvoice[]>([]);
  const [customers, setCustomers] = useState<Customer[]>([]);
  const [items, setItems] = useState<SalesOrderItem[]>([]);

  const [search, setSearch] = useState("");
  const [currentPage, setCurrentPage] = useState(1);

  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState("");

  const [showForm, setShowForm] = useState(false);
  const [orderDetailId, setOrderDetailId] = useState("");
  const [returnDate, setReturnDate] = useState("");
  const [quantity, setQuantity] = useState("1");
  const [reason, setReason] = useState("");
  const [returnAmount, setReturnAmount] = useState("");

  useEffect(() => {
    setReturnDate(getLocalDate());
  }, []);

  const loadData = useCallback(async () => {
    setLoading(true);
    setError("");

    try {
      const [
        returnData,
        orderData,
        invoiceData,
        customerData,
        itemData,
      ] = await Promise.all([
        salesReturnAPI.getAll(),
        salesOrderAPI.getAll(),
        invoiceAPI.getAll(),
        customerAPI.getAll(),
        salesOrderAPI.getItems(),
      ]);

      setReturns(returnData);
      setOrders(orderData);
      setInvoices(invoiceData);
      setCustomers(customerData);
      setItems(itemData);
    } catch (err) {
      setError(
        err instanceof Error
          ? err.message
          : "Unable to load sales returns."
      );
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    void loadData();
  }, [loadData]);

  const orderMap = useMemo(
    () => new Map(orders.map((order) => [order.order_id, order])),
    [orders]
  );

  const customerMap = useMemo(
    () =>
      new Map(
        customers.map((customer) => [customer.customer_id, customer])
      ),
    [customers]
  );

  const itemMap = useMemo(
    () => new Map(items.map((item) => [item.IM_ID, item])),
    [items]
  );

  const invoiceMap = useMemo(
    () => new Map(invoices.map((invoice) => [invoice.order_id, invoice])),
    [invoices]
  );

  const detailMap = useMemo(() => {
    const map = new Map<
      number,
      {
        order: SalesOrder;
        detail: SalesOrderDetail;
      }
    >();

    orders.forEach((order) => {
      order.details.forEach((detail) => {
        map.set(detail.order_detail_id, {
          order,
          detail,
        });
      });
    });

    return map;
  }, [orders]);

  const returnedQuantities = useMemo(() => {
    const totals = new Map<number, number>();

    returns.forEach((record) => {
      const current = totals.get(record.Order_Detail_ID) ?? 0;

      totals.set(
        record.Order_Detail_ID,
        current + Number(record.quantity)
      );
    });

    return totals;
  }, [returns]);

  const availableDetails = useMemo(
    () =>
      orders
        .filter((order) => invoiceMap.has(order.order_id))
        .filter((order) => order.status !== "Cancelled")
        .flatMap((order) =>
          order.details
            .map((detail) => {
              const returned =
                returnedQuantities.get(detail.order_detail_id) ?? 0;

              return {
                ...detail,
                order,
                remaining: detail.quantity - returned,
              };
            })
            .filter((detail) => detail.remaining > 0)
        ),
    [orders, invoiceMap, returnedQuantities]
  );

  const selectedDetail = availableDetails.find(
    (detail) => detail.order_detail_id === Number(orderDetailId)
  );

  const selectedInvoice = selectedDetail
    ? invoiceMap.get(selectedDetail.order_id)
    : undefined;

  const returnQuantity = Number(quantity);

  const calculatedAmount =
    selectedDetail &&
    Number.isInteger(returnQuantity) &&
    returnQuantity > 0
      ? (Number(selectedDetail.line_total) /
          selectedDetail.quantity) *
        returnQuantity
      : 0;

  const totalReturnedAmount = returns.reduce(
    (sum, record) => sum + Number(record.return_amount ?? 0),
    0
  );

  const totalReturnedItems = returns.reduce(
    (sum, record) => sum + Number(record.quantity),
    0
  );

  const filteredReturns = useMemo(() => {
    const term = search.trim().toLowerCase();

    if (!term) return returns;

    return returns.filter((record) => {
      const related = detailMap.get(record.Order_Detail_ID);
      const relatedOrder = related?.order;
      const relatedDetail = related?.detail;

      const customerName = relatedOrder
        ? customerMap.get(relatedOrder.customer_id)?.name ?? ""
        : "";

      const itemName = relatedDetail
        ? itemMap.get(relatedDetail.IM_ID)?.Item_Name ?? ""
        : "";

      return (
        String(record.return_id).includes(term) ||
        String(record.invoice_id).includes(term) ||
        String(relatedOrder?.order_id ?? "").includes(term) ||
        customerName.toLowerCase().includes(term) ||
        itemName.toLowerCase().includes(term)
      );
    });
  }, [returns, detailMap, customerMap, itemMap, search]);

  const totalPages = Math.max(
    1,
    Math.ceil(filteredReturns.length / PAGE_SIZE)
  );

  const paginatedReturns = useMemo(() => {
    const startIndex = (currentPage - 1) * PAGE_SIZE;

    return filteredReturns.slice(
      startIndex,
      startIndex + PAGE_SIZE
    );
  }, [filteredReturns, currentPage]);

  useEffect(() => {
    setCurrentPage(1);
  }, [search]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (currentPage > totalPages) {
      setCurrentPage(totalPages);
    }
  }, [currentPage, totalPages]);

  function resetForm() {
    setOrderDetailId("");
    setReturnDate(getLocalDate());
    setQuantity("1");
    setReason("");
    setReturnAmount("");
    setShowForm(false);
  }

  async function handleCreateReturn() {
    setError("");

    if (!selectedDetail || !selectedInvoice) {
      setError("Please select an available sales order item.");
      return;
    }

    if (!Number.isInteger(returnQuantity) || returnQuantity <= 0) {
      setError("Return quantity must be a positive whole number.");
      return;
    }

    if (returnQuantity > selectedDetail.remaining) {
      setError(
        `Only ${selectedDetail.remaining} item(s) remain eligible for return.`
      );
      return;
    }

    if (!returnDate) {
      setError("Please select a return date.");
      return;
    }

    if (returnAmount.trim()) {
      const amount = Number(returnAmount);

      if (!Number.isFinite(amount) || amount < 0) {
        setError("Please enter a valid, non-negative return amount.");
        return;
      }
    }

    const data: SalesReturnCreate = {
      Order_Detail_ID: selectedDetail.order_detail_id,
      invoice_id: selectedInvoice.invoice_id,
      return_date: returnDate,
      quantity: returnQuantity,
      reason: reason.trim() || null,
      return_amount: returnAmount.trim()
        ? Number(returnAmount)
        : null,
    };

    setSaving(true);

    try {
      await salesReturnAPI.create(data);
      resetForm();
      await loadData();
    } catch (err) {
      setError(
        err instanceof Error
          ? err.message
          : "Unable to create sales return."
      );
    } finally {
      setSaving(false);
    }
  }

  return (
    <div className="min-w-0 space-y-5 bg-green-50 text-slate-800">
      {error && (
        <div
          role="alert"
          className="rounded-lg border border-red-200 bg-red-50 p-4 text-sm text-red-700"
        >
          {error}
        </div>
      )}

      <section className="grid gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3">
        <SummaryCard
          label="Total Returns"
          value={returns.length}
          description="Recorded return transactions"
        />

        <SummaryCard
          label="Total Returned Amount"
          value={money(totalReturnedAmount)}
          description="Combined recorded return value"
        />

        <SummaryCard
          label="Total Returned Items"
          value={totalReturnedItems}
          description="Total quantity of returned items"
        />
      </section>

      <section className="overflow-hidden rounded-xl border border-[#D1D5DB] bg-[#E5E7EB] shadow-sm">
        <div className="flex flex-col justify-between gap-4 border-b border-[#D1D5DB] p-5 lg:flex-row lg:items-center">
          <div>
            <h3 className="font-semibold text-slate-900">
              Sales Return History
            </h3>
            <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-slate-600">
              View all returned items and their details.
            </p>
          </div>

          <div className="flex flex-col gap-3 sm:flex-row sm:items-center">
            <input
              type="search"
              value={search}
              onChange={(event) => setSearch(event.target.value)}
              placeholder="Search return, order, item, or customer..."
              className="w-full rounded-lg border border-[#D1D5DB] bg-white px-4 py-2.5 text-sm text-slate-900 outline-none placeholder:text-slate-400 focus:border-blue-500 focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-100 sm:w-80"
            />

            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => {
                setError("");
                setShowForm(true);
              }}
              disabled={availableDetails.length === 0 || loading}
              className="whitespace-nowrap rounded-lg bg-green-900 px-5 py-2.5 text-sm font-semibold text-white transition hover:bg-slate-700 disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-50"
            >
              + Create Sales Return
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="overflow-x-auto">
          <table className="w-full min-w-[1050px] text-left text-sm">
            <thead className="bg-[#D1D5DB] text-slate-700">
              <tr>
                <th className="px-5 py-4 text-center font-semibold">
                  Return No.
                </th>
                <th className="px-5 py-4 text-center font-semibold">
                  Order No.
                </th>
                <th className="px-5 py-4 text-center font-semibold">
                  Invoice No.
                </th>
                <th className="px-5 py-4 text-center font-semibold">
                  Customer
                </th>
                <th className="px-5 py-4 text-center font-semibold">
                  Returned Item
                </th>
                <th className="px-5 py-4 text-center font-semibold">
                  Return Date
                </th>
                <th className="px-5 py-4 text-center font-semibold">
                  Quantity
                </th>
                <th className="px-5 py-4 text-center font-semibold">
                  Return Amount
                </th>
              </tr>
            </thead>

            <tbody className="divide-y divide-[#D1D5DB]">
              {loading ? (
                <tr>
                  <td
                    colSpan={8}
                    className="px-5 py-12 text-center text-slate-600"
                  >
                    Loading sales returns...
                  </td>
                </tr>
              ) : paginatedReturns.length === 0 ? (
                <tr>
                  <td
                    colSpan={8}
                    className="px-5 py-12 text-center text-slate-600"
                  >
                    No sales returns found.
                  </td>
                </tr>
              ) : (
                paginatedReturns.map((record) => {
                  const related = detailMap.get(record.Order_Detail_ID);
                  const relatedOrder = related?.order;
                  const relatedDetail = related?.detail;

                  const customer = relatedOrder
                    ? customerMap.get(relatedOrder.customer_id)
                    : undefined;

                  const item = relatedDetail
                    ? itemMap.get(relatedDetail.IM_ID)
                    : undefined;

                  return (
                    <tr
                      key={record.return_id}
                      className="text-center transition hover:bg-[#DDE1E5]"
                    >
                      <td className="whitespace-nowrap px-5 py-4 font-semibold text-slate-900">
                        RET-{String(record.return_id).padStart(4, "0")}
                      </td>

                      <td className="whitespace-nowrap px-5 py-4 text-slate-700">
                        {relatedOrder
                          ? `SO-${String(relatedOrder.order_id).padStart(4, "0")}`
                          : "â€”"}
                      </td>

                      <td className="whitespace-nowrap px-5 py-4 text-slate-700">
                        SI-{String(record.invoice_id).padStart(4, "0")}
                      </td>

                      <td className="px-5 py-4 text-slate-700">
                        {customer?.name ?? "Unknown customer"}
                      </td>

                      <td className="px-5 py-4 text-slate-700">
                        <p className="font-medium text-slate-900">
                          {item?.Item_Name ??
                            `Item #${relatedDetail?.IM_ID ?? "â€”"}`}
                        </p>
                        {item?.SKU && (
                          <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-slate-500">
                            {item.SKU}
                          </p>
                        )}
                      </td>

                      <td className="whitespace-nowrap px-5 py-4 text-slate-700">
                        {formatDate(record.return_date)}
                      </td>

                      <td className="px-5 py-4 text-center font-medium text-slate-700">
                        {record.quantity}
                      </td>

                      <td className="whitespace-nowrap px-5 py-4 text-right font-semibold text-slate-900">
                        {money(record.return_amount ?? 0)}
                      </td>
                    </tr>
                  );
                })
              )}
            </tbody>
          </table>
        </div>

        <div className="flex flex-col justify-between gap-3 border-t border-[#D1D5DB] px-5 py-4 sm:flex-row sm:items-center">
          <p className="text-sm text-slate-600">
            Showing{" "}
            {filteredReturns.length === 0
              ? 0
              : (currentPage - 1) * PAGE_SIZE + 1}
            {" "}to{" "}
            {Math.min(currentPage * PAGE_SIZE, filteredReturns.length)}
            {" "}of {filteredReturns.length} returns
          </p>

          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() =>
                setCurrentPage((page) => Math.max(1, page - 1))
              }
              disabled={currentPage === 1}
              className="rounded-lg border border-[#D1D5DB] bg-white px-3 py-2 text-sm font-medium text-slate-700 hover:bg-[#DDE1E5] disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-40"
            >
              Previous
            </button>

            <span className="px-2 text-sm font-medium text-slate-700">
              Page {currentPage} of {totalPages}
            </span>

            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() =>
                setCurrentPage((page) => Math.min(totalPages, page + 1))
              }
              disabled={currentPage >= totalPages}
              className="rounded-lg border border-[#D1D5DB] bg-white px-3 py-2 text-sm font-medium text-slate-700 hover:bg-[#DDE1E5] disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-40"
            >
              Next
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </section>

      {showForm && (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center overflow-y-auto bg-slate-950/50 p-4">
          <div className="my-auto w-full max-w-lg rounded-xl border border-[#D1D5DB] bg-[#E5E7EB] p-6 shadow-2xl">
            <div className="mb-6 flex items-start justify-between gap-4">
              <div>
                <h2 className="text-xl font-bold text-slate-900">
                  Create Sales Return
                </h2>
                <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-slate-600">
                  Record an item returned by a customer.
                </p>
              </div>

              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={resetForm}
                disabled={saving}
                className="rounded-lg px-2 py-1 text-slate-500 hover:bg-[#D1D5DB] hover:text-slate-900 disabled:opacity-40"
                aria-label="Close form"
              >
                âœ•
              </button>
            </div>

            <div className="space-y-5">
              <div>
                <label className="mb-2 block text-sm font-semibold text-slate-700">
                  Sales Order Item
                </label>

                <select
                  value={orderDetailId}
                  onChange={(event) => {
                    setOrderDetailId(event.target.value);
                    setQuantity("1");
                    setReturnAmount("");
                  }}
                  className="w-full rounded-lg border border-[#D1D5DB] bg-white px-4 py-3 text-sm text-slate-900 outline-none focus:border-blue-500 focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-100"
                >
                  <option value="">Select order item</option>

                  {availableDetails.map((detail) => {
                    const item = itemMap.get(detail.IM_ID);

                    return (
                      <option
                        key={detail.order_detail_id}
                        value={detail.order_detail_id}
                      >
                        SO-{String(detail.order.order_id).padStart(4, "0")}
                        {" â€” "}
                        {item?.Item_Name ?? `Item #${detail.IM_ID}`}
                        {" â€” "}
                        {detail.remaining} remaining
                      </option>
                    );
                  })}
                </select>
              </div>

              {selectedDetail && (
                <div className="rounded-lg border border-[#D1D5DB] bg-[#F3F4F6] p-4">
                  <p className="text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wide text-slate-500">
                    Selected Item
                  </p>

                  <p className="mt-1 font-semibold text-slate-900">
                    {itemMap.get(selectedDetail.IM_ID)?.Item_Name ??
                      `Item #${selectedDetail.IM_ID}`}
                  </p>

                  <div className="mt-3 grid grid-cols-2 gap-3 text-sm">
                    <div>
                      <p className="text-slate-500">Ordered Quantity</p>
                      <p className="mt-1 font-semibold text-slate-900">
                        {selectedDetail.quantity}
                      </p>
                    </div>

                    <div>
                      <p className="text-slate-500">Remaining Quantity</p>
                      <p className="mt-1 font-semibold text-slate-900">
                        {selectedDetail.remaining}
                      </p>
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  <p className="mt-3 text-sm text-slate-600">
                    Invoice: SI-
                    {String(selectedInvoice?.invoice_id ?? "").padStart(4, "0")}
                  </p>
                </div>
              )}

              <div>
                <label className="mb-2 block text-sm font-semibold text-slate-700">
                  Return Quantity
                </label>

                <input
                  type="number"
                  min="1"
                  max={selectedDetail?.remaining ?? 1}
                  step="1"
                  value={quantity}
                  onChange={(event) => setQuantity(event.target.value)}
                  className="w-full rounded-lg border border-[#D1D5DB] bg-white px-4 py-3 text-sm text-slate-900 outline-none focus:border-blue-500 focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-100"
                />
              </div>

              <div>
                <label className="mb-2 block text-sm font-semibold text-slate-700">
                  Return Date
                </label>

                <input
                  type="date"
                  value={returnDate}
                  onChange={(event) => setReturnDate(event.target.value)}
                  className="w-full rounded-lg border border-[#D1D5DB] bg-white px-4 py-3 text-sm text-slate-900 outline-none focus:border-blue-500 focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-100"
                />
              </div>

              <div>
                <label className="mb-2 block text-sm font-semibold text-slate-700">
                  Reason for Return
                </label>

                <textarea
                  value={reason}
                  onChange={(event) => setReason(event.target.value)}
                  rows={3}
                  placeholder="Enter the reason for returning the item..."
                  className="w-full rounded-lg border border-[#D1D5DB] bg-white px-4 py-3 text-sm text-slate-900 outline-none placeholder:text-slate-400 focus:border-blue-500 focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-100"
                />
              </div>

              <div>
                <label className="mb-2 block text-sm font-semibold text-slate-700">
                  Return Amount (Optional)
                </label>

                <input
                  type="number"
                  min="0"
                  step="0.01"
                  value={returnAmount}
                  onChange={(event) => setReturnAmount(event.target.value)}
                  placeholder="Leave blank for automatic calculation"
                  className="w-full rounded-lg border border-[#D1D5DB] bg-white px-4 py-3 text-sm text-slate-900 outline-none placeholder:text-slate-400 focus:border-blue-500 focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-100"
                />

                <p className="mt-2 text-xs text-slate-600">
                  Estimated amount: {money(calculatedAmount)}. Leave blank to
                  let the backend calculate it.
                </p>
              </div>

              <div className="flex flex-col-reverse justify-end gap-3 border-t border-[#D1D5DB] pt-5 sm:flex-row">
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={resetForm}
                  disabled={saving}
                  className="rounded-lg border border-[#D1D5DB] bg-white px-5 py-2.5 text-sm font-semibold text-slate-700 hover:bg-[#DDE1E5] disabled:opacity-50"
                >
                  Cancel
                </button>

                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => void handleCreateReturn()}
                  disabled={saving || availableDetails.length === 0}
                  className="rounded-lg bg-slate-900 px-5 py-2.5 text-sm font-semibold text-white hover:bg-slate-700 disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-50"
                >
                  {saving ? "Saving..." : "Create Return"}
                </button>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

"use client";

import { useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useState } from "react";
import { customerAPI, salesOrderAPI } from "../../lib/api";
import type { Customer } from "../../types/customer";
import type {
  SalesOrder,
  SalesOrderCreate,
  SalesOrderItem,
  SalesOrderStatus,
} from "../../types/order";

interface OrderLine {
  lineId: number;
  IM_ID: number;
  quantity: number;
  discount: number;
}

interface StockIssue {
  IM_ID: number;
  itemName: string;
  requested: number;
  available: number;
}

const PAGE_SIZE = 10;
const statuses = ["Pending", "Completed", "Cancelled"] as const;

type OrderStatusOption = (typeof statuses)[number];

const money = (amount: string | number) =>
  `â‚±${Number(amount || 0).toLocaleString("en-PH", {
    minimumFractionDigits: 2,
    maximumFractionDigits: 2,
  })}`;

const formatDate = (value: string | null | undefined) => {
  if (!value) return "â€”";

  const date = new Date(value);

  if (Number.isNaN(date.getTime())) return "â€”";

  return date.toLocaleDateString("en-PH", {
    day: "2-digit",
    month: "long",
    year: "numeric",
  });
};

const statusStyles: Partial<Record<SalesOrderStatus, string>> = {
  Pending: "border-amber-200 bg-amber-50 text-amber-700",
  Completed: "border-green-200 bg-green-50 text-green-700",
  Cancelled: "border-red-200 bg-red-50 text-red-700",
};

export default function OrdersPage() {
  const [orders, setOrders] = useState<SalesOrder[]>([]);
  const [customers, setCustomers] = useState<Customer[]>([]);
  const [items, setItems] = useState<SalesOrderItem[]>([]);
  const [itemCatalog, setItemCatalog] = useState<
    Record<number, SalesOrderItem>
  >({});

  const [search, setSearch] = useState("");
  const [currentPage, setCurrentPage] = useState(1);

  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [loadingItems, setLoadingItems] = useState(false);
  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);
  const [updatingOrderId, setUpdatingOrderId] = useState<number | null>(null);
  const [error, setError] = useState("");

  const [showForm, setShowForm] = useState(false);
  const [customerId, setCustomerId] = useState("");

  const [nextLineId, setNextLineId] = useState(2);
  const [lines, setLines] = useState<OrderLine[]>([
    { lineId: 1, IM_ID: 0, quantity: 1, discount: 0 },
  ]);

  const [itemQueries, setItemQueries] = useState<Record<number, string>>({});
  const [activeItemLine, setActiveItemLine] = useState<number | null>(null);

  const customerSelected = customerId !== "";

  const loadOrders = useCallback(async () => {
    setLoading(true);
    setError("");

    try {
      const data = await salesOrderAPI.getAll();
      setOrders(data);
    } catch (err) {
      setError(
        err instanceof Error ? err.message : "Unable to load sales orders."
      );
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  }, []);

  const loadCustomers = useCallback(async () => {
    try {
      const data = await customerAPI.getAll();
      setCustomers(data);
    } catch (err) {
      setError(
        err instanceof Error ? err.message : "Unable to load customers."
      );
    }
  }, []);

  const loadItems = useCallback(async () => {
    setLoadingItems(true);

    try {
      const data = await salesOrderAPI.getItems();

      setItems(data);

      setItemCatalog((current) => {
        const updated = { ...current };

        data.forEach((item) => {
          updated[item.IM_ID] = item;
        });

        return updated;
      });
    } catch (err) {
      setError(
        err instanceof Error ? err.message : "Unable to load items."
      );
    } finally {
      setLoadingItems(false);
    }
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    void loadOrders();
    void loadCustomers();
    void loadItems();
  }, [loadOrders, loadCustomers, loadItems]);

  const customerMap = useMemo(
    () =>
      new Map(
        customers.map((customer) => [
          customer.customer_id,
          customer.name,
        ])
      ),
    [customers]
  );

  const filteredOrders = useMemo(() => {
    const term = search.trim().toLowerCase();

    if (!term) return orders;

    return orders.filter((order) => {
      const customerName = customerMap.get(order.customer_id) ?? "";

      return (
        String(order.order_id).includes(term) ||
        customerName.toLowerCase().includes(term) ||
        order.status.toLowerCase().includes(term)
      );
    });
  }, [orders, search, customerMap]);

  const totalPages = Math.max(
    1,
    Math.ceil(filteredOrders.length / PAGE_SIZE)
  );

  const safePage = Math.min(currentPage, totalPages);
  const startIndex = (safePage - 1) * PAGE_SIZE;

  const paginatedOrders = useMemo(
    () => filteredOrders.slice(startIndex, startIndex + PAGE_SIZE),
    [filteredOrders, startIndex]
  );

  const endRecord = Math.min(
    startIndex + PAGE_SIZE,
    filteredOrders.length
  );

  const totalSales = orders.reduce(
    (sum, order) => sum + Number(order.total_amount || 0),
    0
  );

  const pendingOrders = orders.filter(
    (order) => order.status === "Pending"
  ).length;

  const completedOrders = orders.filter(
    (order) => order.status === "Completed"
  ).length;

  const requestedByItem = useMemo(() => {
    const totals = new Map<number, number>();

    lines.forEach((line) => {
      if (line.IM_ID > 0 && line.quantity > 0) {
        totals.set(
          line.IM_ID,
          (totals.get(line.IM_ID) ?? 0) + line.quantity
        );
      }
    });

    return totals;
  }, [lines]);

  const stockIssues = useMemo<StockIssue[]>(() => {
    const issues: StockIssue[] = [];

    requestedByItem.forEach((requested, itemId) => {
      const item = itemCatalog[itemId];

      if (!item) return;

      const available = Number(item.stock_available ?? 0);

      if (requested > available) {
        issues.push({
          IM_ID: itemId,
          itemName: item.Item_Name,
          requested,
          available,
        });
      }
    });

    return issues;
  }, [requestedByItem, itemCatalog]);

  const hasStockIssues = stockIssues.length > 0;

  const selectItems = useMemo(() => {
    const combined = new Map<number, SalesOrderItem>();

    items.forEach((item) => combined.set(item.IM_ID, item));

    lines.forEach((line) => {
      if (line.IM_ID > 0 && itemCatalog[line.IM_ID]) {
        combined.set(line.IM_ID, itemCatalog[line.IM_ID]);
      }
    });

    return Array.from(combined.values());
  }, [items, lines, itemCatalog]);

  function updateLineById(
    lineId: number,
    changes: Partial<OrderLine>
  ) {
    setLines((current) =>
      current.map((line) =>
        line.lineId === lineId ? { ...line, ...changes } : line
      )
    );
  }

  function handleItemSearch(line: OrderLine, value: string) {
    if (!customerSelected) return;

    setItemQueries((current) => ({
      ...current,
      [line.lineId]: value,
    }));

    updateLineById(line.lineId, { IM_ID: 0 });
    setActiveItemLine(line.lineId);
  }

  function chooseItem(line: OrderLine, item: SalesOrderItem) {
    if (!customerSelected) return;

    updateLineById(line.lineId, { IM_ID: item.IM_ID });

    setItemQueries((current) => ({
      ...current,
      [line.lineId]: `${item.SKU} - ${item.Item_Name}`,
    }));

    setActiveItemLine(null);
  }

  function addLine() {
    if (!customerSelected || saving) return;

    const newLineId = nextLineId;

    setLines((current) => [
      ...current,
      {
        lineId: newLineId,
        IM_ID: 0,
        quantity: 1,
        discount: 0,
      },
    ]);

    setNextLineId((current) => current + 1);
  }

  function removeLine(index: number) {
    if (!customerSelected || saving || lines.length === 1) return;

    const removedLine = lines[index];

    setLines((current) => current.filter((_, i) => i !== index));

    if (removedLine) {
      setItemQueries((current) => {
        const updated = { ...current };
        delete updated[removedLine.lineId];
        return updated;
      });

      if (activeItemLine === removedLine.lineId) {
        setActiveItemLine(null);
      }
    }
  }

  function resetForm() {
    setCustomerId("");
    setLines([{ lineId: 1, IM_ID: 0, quantity: 1, discount: 0 }]);
    setNextLineId(2);
    setItemQueries({});
    setActiveItemLine(null);
    setShowForm(false);
  }

  async function handleCreateOrder() {
    setError("");

    if (!customerSelected) {
      setError("Please select a customer.");
      return;
    }

    if (
      lines.length === 0 ||
      lines.some(
        (line) =>
          line.IM_ID <= 0 ||
          !Number.isInteger(line.quantity) ||
          line.quantity <= 0 ||
          !Number.isFinite(line.discount) ||
          line.discount < 0 ||
          line.discount > 100
      )
    ) {
      setError(
        "Please select valid items, whole-number quantities, and discounts between 0 and 100%."
      );
      return;
    }

    if (hasStockIssues) {
      const issueMessages = stockIssues.map(
        (issue) =>
          `${issue.itemName}: requested ${issue.requested}, available ${issue.available}.`
      );

      setError(`Insufficient stock. ${issueMessages.join(" ")}`);
      return;
    }

    const data: SalesOrderCreate = {
      customer_id: Number(customerId),
      details: lines.map((line) => ({
        IM_ID: line.IM_ID,
        quantity: line.quantity,
        discount: line.discount,
      })),
    };

    setSaving(true);

    try {
      await salesOrderAPI.create(data);
      resetForm();
      setCurrentPage(1);
      await loadOrders();
    } catch (err) {
      setError(
        err instanceof Error ? err.message : "Unable to create sales order."
      );
    } finally {
      setSaving(false);
    }
  }

  async function handleStatusChange(
    order: SalesOrder,
    newStatus: OrderStatusOption
  ) {
    if (order.status === newStatus) return;

    if (order.status !== "Pending") {
      setError("Completed and Cancelled orders cannot be changed.");
      return;
    }

    setError("");
    setUpdatingOrderId(order.order_id);

    try {
      const updated = await salesOrderAPI.updateStatus(
        order.order_id,
        newStatus as SalesOrderStatus
      );

      setOrders((current) =>
        current.map((item) =>
          item.order_id === updated.order_id ? updated : item
        )
      );
    } catch (err) {
      setError(
        err instanceof Error ? err.message : "Unable to update order status."
      );
    } finally {
      setUpdatingOrderId(null);
    }
  }

  const itemFieldsDisabled =
    !customerSelected || saving || loadingItems;

  return (
    <div className="min-h-full space-y-5 bg-gray text-slate-800">
      {error && (
        <div
          role="alert"
          className="flex items-start justify-between gap-3 rounded-lg border border-red-200 bg-red-50 p-4 text-sm text-red-700"
        >
          <span>{error}</span>

          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => setError("")}
            className="font-semibold hover:text-red-900"
            aria-label="Dismiss error"
          >
            âœ•
          </button>
        </div>
      )}

      <section className="grid gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2 xl:grid-cols-4">
        <SummaryCard title="Total Orders" value={orders.length.toString()} />
        <SummaryCard title="Total Sales" value={money(totalSales)} />

        <SummaryCard
          title="Pending Orders"
          value={pendingOrders.toString()}
          valueColor="text-amber-600"
        />

        <SummaryCard
          title="Completed Orders"
          value={completedOrders.toString()}
          valueColor="text-green-700"
        />
      </section>

      <section className="overflow-hidden rounded-xl border border-[#D1D5DB] bg-[#E5E7EB] shadow-sm">
        <div className="flex flex-col justify-between gap-4 border-b border-[#D1D5DB] p-4 sm:flex-row sm:items-center">
          <div>
            <h2 className="font-semibold text-slate-800">
              Sales Order List
            </h2>

            <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-slate-600">
              View and update your sales orders.
            </p>
          </div>

          <div className="flex flex-col gap-2 sm:flex-row">
            <input
              type="search"
              value={search}
              onChange={(e) => {
                setSearch(e.target.value);
                setCurrentPage(1);
              }}
              placeholder="Search order, customer, or status..."
              className="w-full rounded-lg border border-slate-300 bg-white px-3 py-2 text-sm text-slate-800 outline-none placeholder:text-slate-400 focus:border-green-700 focus:ring-2 focus:ring-green-100 sm:w-72"
            />

            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => {
                setError("");
                setShowForm(true);
              }}
              className="whitespace-nowrap rounded-lg bg-[#3F7657] px-4 py-2 text-sm font-semibold text-white hover:bg-[#315F46]"
            >
              + Create Sales Order
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="overflow-x-auto">
          <table className="w-full text-left text-sm">
            <thead className="bg-[#D1D5DB] text-xs uppercase tracking-wide text-slate-700">
              <tr>
                <th className="whitespace-nowrap px-5 py-3 text-center">
                  Order No.
                </th>
                <th className="whitespace-nowrap px-5 py-3">Customer</th>
                <th className="whitespace-nowrap px-5 py-3 text-center">
                  Order Date
                </th>
                <th className="whitespace-nowrap px-5 py-3 text-center">
                  Items
                </th>
                <th className="whitespace-nowrap px-5 py-3">Total Amount</th>
                <th className="whitespace-nowrap px-5 py-3 text-center">
                  Status
                </th>
              </tr>
            </thead>

            <tbody className="divide-y divide-[#D1D5DB]">
              {loading ? (
                <tr>
                  <td colSpan={6} className="px-5 py-10 text-center text-slate-600">
                    Loading sales orders...
                  </td>
                </tr>
              ) : filteredOrders.length === 0 ? (
                <tr>
                  <td colSpan={6} className="px-5 py-10 text-center text-slate-600">
                    {search
                      ? "No sales orders match your search."
                      : "No sales orders found."}
                  </td>
                </tr>
              ) : (
                paginatedOrders.map((order) => {
                  const isUpdating = updatingOrderId === order.order_id;
                  const canUpdate = order.status === "Pending";
                  const knownStatus = statuses.includes(
                    order.status as OrderStatusOption
                  );

                  return (
                    <tr
                      key={order.order_id}
                      className="bg-[#E5E7EB] transition-colors hover:bg-[#DDE1E5]"
                    >
                      <td className="whitespace-nowrap px-5 py-4 text-center font-semibold text-slate-800">
                        SO-{String(order.order_id).padStart(4, "0")}
                      </td>

                      <td className="px-5 py-4 text-slate-700">
                        {customerMap.get(order.customer_id) ??
                          `Customer #${order.customer_id}`}
                      </td>

                      <td className="whitespace-nowrap px-5 py-4 text-center text-slate-600">
                        {formatDate(order.order_date)}
                      </td>

                      <td className="px-5 py-4 text-center text-slate-600">
                        {order.details?.length ?? 0}
                      </td>

                      <td className="whitespace-nowrap px-5 py-4 font-medium text-slate-800">
                        {money(order.total_amount)}
                      </td>

                      <td className="whitespace-nowrap px-5 py-4">
                        <div className="flex items-center justify-center gap-2">
                          <select
                            value={knownStatus ? order.status : "Pending"}
                            disabled={isUpdating || !canUpdate || !knownStatus}
                            onChange={(e) =>
                              void handleStatusChange(
                                order,
                                e.target.value as OrderStatusOption
                              )
                            }
                            className={`rounded-full border px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-semibold outline-none transition disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-70 ${
                              statusStyles[order.status] ??
                              "border-slate-300 bg-slate-100 text-slate-600"
                            }`}
                            aria-label={`Update status for order ${order.order_id}`}
                          >
                            {statuses.map((status) => (
                              <option key={status} value={status}>
                                {status}
                              </option>
                            ))}
                          </select>

                          {isUpdating && (
                            <span className="text-xs text-slate-600">
                              Saving...
                            </span>
                          )}
                        </div>
                      </td>
                    </tr>
                  );
                })
              )}
            </tbody>
          </table>
        </div>

        {!loading && (
          <div className="flex flex-col gap-3 border-t border-[#D1D5DB] bg-gray px-5 py-4 sm:flex-row sm:items-center sm:justify-between">
            <p className="text-sm text-slate-500">
              Showing{" "}
              <span className="font-semibold text-slate-700">
                {filteredOrders.length === 0 ? 0 : startIndex + 1}
              </span>{" "}
              to{" "}
              <span className="font-semibold text-slate-700">
                {endRecord}
              </span>{" "}
              of{" "}
              <span className="font-semibold text-slate-700">
                {filteredOrders.length}
              </span>{" "}
              orders
            </p>

            <div className="flex items-center justify-end gap-2">
              <button
                type="button"
                disabled={safePage === 1}
                onClick={() => setCurrentPage(safePage - 1)}
                className="rounded-lg border border-slate-300 px-3 py-2 text-sm font-medium text-slate-700 transition hover:bg-slate-50 disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-40"
              >
                Previous
              </button>

              <span className="px-2 text-sm font-medium text-slate-600">
                Page {safePage} of {totalPages}
              </span>

              <button
                type="button"
                disabled={safePage === totalPages}
                onClick={() => setCurrentPage(safePage + 1)}
                className="rounded-lg border border-slate-300 px-3 py-2 text-sm font-medium text-slate-700 transition hover:bg-slate-50 disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-40"
              >
                Next
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>
        )}
      </section>

      {showForm && (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center overflow-y-auto bg-black/40 p-4">
          <div className="my-auto max-h-[90vh] w-full max-w-2xl overflow-y-auto rounded-xl border border-[#D1D5DB] bg-[#E5E7EB] p-5 shadow-xl sm:p-6">
            <div className="mb-6 flex items-start justify-between gap-4">
              <div>
                <h2 className="text-xl font-bold text-slate-900">
                  Create Sales Order
                </h2>

                <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-slate-600">
                  Select a customer and add the items for this order.
                </p>
              </div>

              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={resetForm}
                disabled={saving}
                className="rounded-lg p-2 text-slate-600 hover:bg-[#D1D5DB] hover:text-slate-900 disabled:opacity-50"
                aria-label="Close form"
              >
                âœ•
              </button>
            </div>

            <div className="space-y-5">
              <div>
                <label
                  htmlFor="order-customer"
                  className="mb-2 block text-sm font-medium text-slate-700"
                >
                  Customer
                </label>

                <select
                  id="order-customer"
                  value={customerId}
                  onChange={(e) => {
                    setCustomerId(e.target.value);
                    setActiveItemLine(null);
                  }}
                  disabled={saving}
                  className="w-full rounded-lg border border-slate-300 bg-white px-4 py-3 text-sm text-slate-800 outline-none focus:border-green-700"
                >
                  <option value="">Select customer</option>

                  {customers
                    .filter((customer) => customer.status)
                    .map((customer) => (
                      <option
                        key={customer.customer_id}
                        value={customer.customer_id}
                      >
                        {customer.name}
                      </option>
                    ))}
                </select>
              </div>

              <div>
                <div className="mb-3 flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2">
                  <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-slate-700">
                    Order Items
                  </label>

                  {!customerSelected && (
                    <span className="text-xs font-medium text-amber-700">
                      Select a customer first to enable order items.
                    </span>
                  )}
                </div>

                {loadingItems && (
                  <p className="mb-2 text-xs text-slate-600">
                    Loading items and stock...
                  </p>
                )}

                <div className="space-y-3">
                  {lines.map((line, index) => {
                    const selectedItem = itemCatalog[line.IM_ID];
                    const requested =
                      requestedByItem.get(line.IM_ID) ?? 0;
                    const available = Number(
                      selectedItem?.stock_available ?? 0
                    );
                    const isInsufficient =
                      Boolean(selectedItem) && requested > available;

                    const query = itemQueries[line.lineId] ?? "";
                    const term = query.trim().toLowerCase();

                    const matchingItems = selectItems.filter((item) => {
                      if (!term) return true;

                      const itemName = String(item.Item_Name ?? "").toLowerCase();
                      const sku = String(item.SKU ?? "").toLowerCase();

                      return itemName.includes(term) || sku.includes(term);
                    });

                    const showSuggestions =
                      activeItemLine === line.lineId && customerSelected;

                    return (
                      <div
                        key={line.lineId}
                        className={`rounded-lg border border-[#D1D5DB] bg-white p-3 ${
                          !customerSelected ? "opacity-60" : ""
                        }`}
                      >
                        <div className="grid gap-3 sm:grid-cols-[minmax(0,1fr)_90px_110px_auto]">
                          <div className="relative min-w-0">
                            <label
                              htmlFor={`item-search-${line.lineId}`}
                              className="mb-1 block text-xs font-medium text-slate-600"
                            >
                              Search or Select Item
                            </label>

                            <input
                              id={`item-search-${line.lineId}`}
                              type="search"
                              value={
                                query ||
                                (selectedItem
                                  ? `${selectedItem.SKU} - ${selectedItem.Item_Name}`
                                  : "")
                              }
                              onChange={(e) =>
                                handleItemSearch(line, e.target.value)
                              }
                              onFocus={() => {
                                if (customerSelected) {
                                  setActiveItemLine(line.lineId);
                                }
                              }}
                              onBlur={() => {
                                setTimeout(() => {
                                  setActiveItemLine((current) =>
                                    current === line.lineId ? null : current
                                  );
                                }, 150);
                              }}
                              disabled={itemFieldsDisabled}
                              placeholder={
                                customerSelected
                                  ? "Type item name or SKU..."
                                  : "Select a customer first"
                              }
                              autoComplete="off"
                              aria-expanded={showSuggestions}
                              aria-controls={`item-suggestions-${line.lineId}`}
                              className="w-full rounded-lg border border-slate-300 bg-white px-3 py-2 text-sm text-slate-800 outline-none placeholder:text-slate-400 focus:border-green-700 focus:ring-2 focus:ring-green-100 disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:bg-slate-100 disabled:text-slate-400"
                            />

                            {showSuggestions && (
                              <div
                                id={`item-suggestions-${line.lineId}`}
                                className="absolute left-0 right-0 top-full z-30 mt-1 max-h-64 overflow-y-auto rounded-lg border border-slate-200 bg-white shadow-lg"
                              >
                                {matchingItems.length > 0 ? (
                                  <>
                                    <p className="border-b border-slate-100 bg-slate-50 px-3 py-2 text-xs font-semibold text-slate-500">
                                      {term
                                        ? "Matching items"
                                        : "Available items"}
                                    </p>

                                    {matchingItems.map((item) => (
                                      <button
                                        key={item.IM_ID}
                                        type="button"
                                        onMouseDown={(e) =>
                                          e.preventDefault()
                                        }
                                        onClick={() =>
                                          chooseItem(line, item)
                                        }
                                        className="flex w-full flex-col gap-1 border-b border-slate-100 px-3 py-3 text-left last:border-b-0 hover:bg-[#E8F0EA] focus:bg-[#E8F0EA] focus:outline-none"
                                      >
                                        <span className="text-sm font-semibold text-slate-800">
                                          {item.Item_Name}
                                        </span>

                                        <span className="text-xs text-slate-500">
                                          {item.SKU} Â·{" "}
                                          {money(item.Price ?? 0)}
                                        </span>

                                        <span className="text-xs font-medium text-green-700">
                                          Stock available:{" "}
                                          {Number(item.stock_available ?? 0)}
                                          {item.unit_of_measure
                                            ? ` ${item.unit_of_measure}`
                                            : ""}
                                        </span>
                                      </button>
                                    ))}
                                  </>
                                ) : (
                                  <p className="px-3 py-3 text-sm text-slate-500">
                                    No matching items found.
                                  </p>
                                )}
                              </div>
                            )}
                          </div>

                          <div>
                            <label className="mb-1 block text-xs font-medium text-slate-600">
                              Quantity
                            </label>

                            <input
                              type="number"
                              min="1"
                              step="1"
                              value={line.quantity === 0 ? "" : line.quantity}
                              onChange={(e) =>
                                updateLineById(line.lineId, {
                                  quantity:
                                    e.target.value === ""
                                      ? 0
                                      : Number(e.target.value),
                                })
                              }
                              disabled={!customerSelected || saving}
                              aria-label="Quantity"
                              placeholder="Qty"
                              className="w-full rounded-lg border border-slate-300 bg-white px-3 py-2 text-sm text-slate-800 disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:bg-slate-100 disabled:text-slate-400"
                            />
                          </div>

                          <div>
                            <label className="mb-1 block text-xs font-medium text-slate-600">
                              Discount (%)
                            </label>

                            <div className="relative">
                              <input
                                type="number"
                                min="0"
                                max="100"
                                step="0.01"
                                value={line.discount === 0 ? "" : line.discount}
                                onChange={(e) =>
                                  updateLineById(line.lineId, {
                                    discount:
                                      e.target.value === ""
                                        ? 0
                                        : Number(e.target.value),
                                  })
                                }
                                disabled={!customerSelected || saving}
                                aria-label="Discount percentage"
                                placeholder="0"
                                className="w-full rounded-lg border border-slate-300 bg-white px-3 py-2 pr-8 text-sm text-slate-800 outline-none focus:border-green-700 disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:bg-slate-100 disabled:text-slate-400"
                              />

                              <span className="pointer-events-none absolute right-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-sm text-slate-500">
                                %
                              </span>
                            </div>
                          </div>

                          <div className="flex items-end">
                            <button
                              type="button"
                              onClick={() => removeLine(index)}
                              disabled={
                                !customerSelected ||
                                lines.length === 1 ||
                                saving
                              }
                              className="w-full rounded-lg px-3 py-2 text-sm font-medium text-red-600 hover:bg-red-50 disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-30"
                            >
                              Remove
                            </button>
                          </div>
                        </div>

                        {selectedItem && (
                          <div className="mt-3 flex flex-col gap-1 border-t border-slate-100 pt-3 text-xs sm:flex-row sm:items-center sm:justify-between">
                            <span className="text-slate-600">
                              Selected:{" "}
                              <strong className="text-slate-800">
                                {selectedItem.Item_Name}
                              </strong>
                            </span>

                            <span
                              className={
                                isInsufficient
                                  ? "font-semibold text-red-600"
                                  : "font-medium text-green-700"
                              }
                            >
                              {isInsufficient
                                ? `Insufficient stock: ${requested} requested, ${available} available`
                                : `Requested across order: ${requested} / ${available}`}
                            </span>
                          </div>
                        )}
                      </div>
                    );
                  })}
                </div>

                {!loadingItems && items.length === 0 && (
                  <p className="mt-2 text-xs text-slate-600">
                    No items available.
                  </p>
                )}

                {hasStockIssues && (
                  <div
                    role="alert"
                    className="mt-3 rounded-lg border border-red-200 bg-red-50 p-3 text-sm text-red-700"
                  >
                    <p className="font-semibold">
                      Insufficient stock for this order.
                    </p>

                    <ul className="mt-1 list-disc space-y-1 pl-5">
                      {stockIssues.map((issue) => (
                        <li key={issue.IM_ID}>
                          {issue.itemName}: {issue.requested} requested,
                          only {issue.available} available.
                        </li>
                      ))}
                    </ul>
                  </div>
                )}

                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={addLine}
                  disabled={!customerSelected || saving}
                  className="mt-3 text-sm font-semibold text-[#3F7657] hover:text-[#315F46] disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-40"
                >
                  + Add another item
                </button>
              </div>

              <div className="flex flex-col-reverse justify-end gap-3 border-t border-[#D1D5DB] pt-5 sm:flex-row">
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={resetForm}
                  disabled={saving}
                  className="rounded-lg border border-slate-300 bg-white px-5 py-3 text-sm font-medium text-slate-700 hover:bg-[#DDE1E5] disabled:opacity-50"
                >
                  Cancel
                </button>

                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => void handleCreateOrder()}
                  disabled={
                    saving ||
                    !customerSelected ||
                    hasStockIssues ||
                    loadingItems
                  }
                  className="rounded-lg bg-[#3F7657] px-5 py-3 text-sm font-semibold text-white hover:bg-[#315F46] disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-50"
                >
                  {saving ? "Creating..." : "Create Order"}
                </button>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

function SummaryCard({
  title,
  value,
  valueColor = "text-slate-900",
}: {
  title: string;
  value: string;
  valueColor?: string;
}) {
  return (
    <div className="rounded-xl border border-[#D1D5DB] bg-[#E5E7EB] p-5 shadow-sm">
      <p className="text-sm font-medium text-slate-600">{title}</p>

      <p className={`mt-2 text-2xl font-bold ${valueColor}`}>
        {value}
      </p>
    </div>
  );
}
